function getLang(request) {
  const cookies = parseCookies(request);
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  const param = url.searchParams.get("lang");
  if (param === "en" || param === "zh") return param;
  if (cookies[LANG_COOKIE] === "en") return "en";
  return "zh";
}

function _(key, lang) {
  return (I18N[lang] && I18N[lang][key]) || I18N.zh[key] || key;
}

function langSwitchLink(request, lang) {
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  url.searchParams.set("lang", lang === "zh" ? "en" : "zh");
  return url.pathname + url.search;
}

function langCookieHeader(lang) {
  return `${LANG_COOKIE}=${lang}; Path=/; Max-Age=31536000; SameSite=Lax`;
}


function currentLangQuery(lang) {
  return lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : "";
}

function withLang(path, lang) {
  return `${path}${lang === "en" ? (path.includes("?") ? "&lang=en" : "?lang=en") : ""}`;
}

function brandLogoHtml(favicon, label = DEFAULT_SITE_NAME, href = "/") {
  const src = safeUrl(favicon || "", "");
  const icon = src ? `<img class="brand-icon" src="${escapeAttr(src)}" alt="" loading="lazy">` : `<span class="brand-mark" aria-hidden="true">✦</span>`;
  return `<a class="brand-with-icon brand-link" href="${escapeAttr(href)}" rel="home">${icon}<span class="brand-text">${escapeHtml(label)}</span></a>`;
}

function publicNav(request, env, active = "") {
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const switchHref = langSwitchLink(request, lang);
  const nav = [
    ["/featured", lang === "en" ? "Featured" : "今日推荐", "featured"],
    ["/explore", lang === "en" ? "Explore" : "探索", "explore"],
    ["/daily", lang === "en" ? "Daily" : "每日精选", "daily"],
    ["/docs", lang === "en" ? "Docs" : "文档", "docs"],
    ["/url-builder", lang === "en" ? "URL Builder" : "链接生成器", "builder"],
    ["/feedback", lang === "en" ? "Feedback" : "💬 反馈", "feedback"],
  ].map(([href, label, key]) => `<a class="${active === key ? "active" : ""}" href="${escapeAttr(withLang(href, lang))}">${escapeHtml(label)}</a>`).join("");
  const themeLabels = { auto: "Auto", light: "Light", dark: "Dark" };
  const langSwitchText = lang === "en" ? "中文" : "English";
  return `<nav class="nav">${brandLogoHtml(siteFaviconHref(env), siteName(env))}<div class="nav-actions">${nav}<a class="ui-switch" href="${escapeAttr(switchHref)}" title="Language">🌐 ${escapeHtml(langSwitchText)}</a><button type="button" class="ui-switch theme-toggle" onclick="toggleTheme();return false" title="Theme"><span class="theme-icon">🌓</span><span class="theme-label" data-auto="${escapeAttr(themeLabels.auto)}" data-light="${escapeAttr(themeLabels.light)}" data-dark="${escapeAttr(themeLabels.dark)}">${escapeHtml(themeLabels.auto)}</span></button></div></nav>`;
}

let cachedCryptoKey = null;
let cachedAdminPassword = null;
let cachedLegacyCryptoKey = null;
let cachedLegacyAdminPassword = null;

function nowIso() {
  return new Date().toISOString();
}

function todayUtc8() {
  return new Date(Date.now() + 8 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

function randomString(length = 12) {
  const alphabet = "abcdefghijkmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789";
  const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(length));
  let out = "";
  for (const b of bytes) out += alphabet[b % alphabet.length];
  return out;
}

function bytesToBase64(bytes) {
  let binary = "";
  const chunkSize = 0x8000;
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunkSize) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + chunkSize));
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}

function base64ToBytes(base64) {
  const binary = atob(base64);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
  return bytes;
}

function base64UrlEncode(input) {
  const base64 = typeof input === "string" ? btoa(input) : bytesToBase64(input);
  return base64.replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/g, "");
}

function base64UrlDecodeToString(value) {
  const padded = value.replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/") + "===".slice((value.length + 3) % 4);
  return atob(padded);
}

async function sha256Hex(text) {
  const data = new TextEncoder().encode(text);
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

async function hmacSha256(secret, message) {
  const enc = new TextEncoder();
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", enc.encode(secret), { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" }, false, ["sign"]);
  return new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, enc.encode(message)));
}

const CURRENT_ENCRYPTION_VERSION = 2;

async function getCryptoKeyV1(password) {
  if (cachedLegacyCryptoKey && cachedLegacyAdminPassword === password) return cachedLegacyCryptoKey;
  const enc = new TextEncoder();
  const keyMaterial = await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", enc.encode(password), { name: "PBKDF2" }, false, ["deriveKey"]);
  cachedLegacyCryptoKey = await crypto.subtle.deriveKey(
    { name: "PBKDF2", salt: enc.encode("unsplash-salt-v1"), iterations: 1000, hash: "SHA-256" },
    keyMaterial,
    { name: "AES-GCM", length: 256 },
    false,
    ["decrypt"],
  );
  cachedLegacyAdminPassword = password;
  return cachedLegacyCryptoKey;
}

async function getCryptoKeyV2(password) {
  if (cachedCryptoKey && cachedAdminPassword === password) return cachedCryptoKey;
  const enc = new TextEncoder();
  const keyMaterial = await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", enc.encode(password), { name: "PBKDF2" }, false, ["deriveKey"]);
  cachedCryptoKey = await crypto.subtle.deriveKey(
    { name: "PBKDF2", salt: enc.encode("multi-source-image-host-v2"), iterations: 10000, hash: "SHA-256" },
    keyMaterial,
    { name: "AES-GCM", length: 256 },
    false,
    ["encrypt", "decrypt"],
  );
  cachedAdminPassword = password;
  return cachedCryptoKey;
}

async function getCryptoKeyByVersion(version, password) {
  if (version === 1) return getCryptoKeyV1(password);
  return getCryptoKeyV2(password);
}

async function encryptText(text, password, version = CURRENT_ENCRYPTION_VERSION) {
  const str = String(text || "");
  // 防护：如果输入已经是加密格式（vN:xxx），不再重复加密，防止套中套
  if (str.match(/^v\d+:/)) return str;
  const key = await getCryptoKeyByVersion(version, password);
  const iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));
  const encrypted = await crypto.subtle.encrypt({ name: "AES-GCM", iv }, key, new TextEncoder().encode(str));
  const combined = new Uint8Array(iv.length + encrypted.byteLength);
  combined.set(iv);
  combined.set(new Uint8Array(encrypted), iv.length);
  const base64 = bytesToBase64(combined);
  return `v${version}:${base64}`;
}

function parseEncryptedPayload(value) {
  const text = String(value || "");
  const match = text.match(/^v(\d+):(.+)$/);
  if (match) return { version: parseInt(match[1], 10), base64: match[2] };
  return { version: null, base64: text };
}

async function decryptWithVersion(base64, password, version) {
  const key = await getCryptoKeyByVersion(version, password);
  const combined = base64ToBytes(base64);
  const iv = combined.slice(0, 12);
  const data = combined.slice(12);
  const decrypted = await crypto.subtle.decrypt({ name: "AES-GCM", iv }, key, data);
  return new TextDecoder().decode(decrypted);
}

async function decryptText(value, password, depth = 0) {
  if (depth > 3) return "FAIL";
  try {
    const { version, base64 } = parseEncryptedPayload(value);
    let decrypted = null;
    if (version) {
      decrypted = await decryptWithVersion(base64, password, version);
    } else {
      try { decrypted = await decryptWithVersion(base64, password, CURRENT_ENCRYPTION_VERSION); } catch (_) {}
      if (!decrypted) try { decrypted = await decryptWithVersion(base64, password, 1); } catch (_) {}
    }
    if (!decrypted) return "FAIL";
    // 处理套中套：解密结果仍然是加密格式，递归再解一层
    if (String(decrypted).match(/^v\d+:/)) {
      return await decryptText(decrypted, password, depth + 1);
    }
    return decrypted;
  } catch (_) {
    return "FAIL";
  }
}

const encryptURL = encryptText;
const decryptURL = decryptText;

function escapeHtml(value) {
  return String(value ?? "")
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    .replace(/'/g, "&#39;");
}

function escapeAttr(value) {
  return escapeHtml(value).replace(/`/g, "&#96;");
}

function safeUrl(value, fallback = "") {
  const raw = String(value || "").trim();
  if (!raw) return fallback;
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(raw, "https://example.invalid");
    if (["http:", "https:"].includes(parsed.protocol)) return raw;
  } catch (_) {}
  return fallback;
}

function siteName(env) {
  return String(env?.SITE_NAME || "").trim() || DEFAULT_SITE_NAME;
}

function siteFaviconHref(env) {
  return safeUrl(env?.SITE_FAVICON || "", DEFAULT_FAVICON_DATA_URI);
}

function validateNotificationWebhookUrl(channel, value) {
  const raw = String(value || "").trim();
  if (!raw) return "";
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(raw);
    const host = parsed.hostname.toLowerCase();
    if (channel === "feishu") {
      if (parsed.protocol !== "https:") return "";
      if (!(host.endsWith("feishu.cn") || host.endsWith("larksuite.com"))) return "";
      if (!parsed.pathname.includes("/open-apis/bot/v2/hook/")) return "";
      return parsed.toString();
    }
    if (channel === "dingtalk") {
      if (parsed.protocol !== "https:") return "";
      if (host !== "oapi.dingtalk.com") return "";
      if (!parsed.pathname.includes("/robot/send")) return "";
      return parsed.toString();
    }
    if (channel === "webhook") {
      if (!["http:", "https:"].includes(parsed.protocol)) return "";
      return parsed.toString();
    }
    if (!["http:", "https:"].includes(parsed.protocol)) return "";
    return parsed.toString();
  } catch (_) {
    return "";
  }
}

function keepOrUpdateSecret(form, name, current = "") {
  if (form.get(`${name}_clear`) === "on") return "";
  const next = String(form.get(name) || "").trim();
  return next || String(current || "");
}

function keepOrUpdateUrl(form, name, current = "", channel = "") {
  if (form.get(`${name}_clear`) === "on") return "";
  const next = validateNotificationWebhookUrl(channel, form.get(name));
  return next || String(current || "");
}

function readCheckedValues(form, name) {
  return Array.from(new Set(form.getAll(name).map(v => String(v || "").trim()).filter(Boolean)));
}

function securityHeaders(extra = {}) {
  return {
    "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
    "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer-when-downgrade",
    "Content-Security-Policy": "default-src 'self'; img-src https: data: blob:; media-src https:; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' https://challenges.cloudflare.com; frame-src https://challenges.cloudflare.com; font-src 'self' data:; connect-src 'self' https: https://challenges.cloudflare.com; frame-ancestors 'self'; base-uri 'self'; form-action 'self'",
    ...extra,
  };
}

function htmlResponse(html, status = 200, headers = {}) {
  return new Response(html, {
    status,
    headers: securityHeaders({ "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8", ...headers }),
  });
}

function adminHtmlResponse(html, status = 200, headers = {}) {
  return htmlResponse(html, status, {
    "Cache-Control": "no-store",
    "X-Frame-Options": "DENY",
    ...headers,
  });
}

function jsonResponse(data, status = 200, headers = {}) {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data, null, 2), {
    status,
    headers: securityHeaders({ "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8", ...headers }),
  });
}

function publicJsonResponse(data, status = 200, headers = {}) {
  return jsonResponse(data, status, { "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*", ...headers });
}

function textResponse(text, status = 200, headers = {}) {
  return new Response(text, {
    status,
    headers: securityHeaders({ "content-type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", ...headers }),
  });
}

function redirectResponse(location, cacheControl = "no-store", extraHeaders = {}) {
  return new Response(null, {
    status: 302,
    headers: {
      Location: location,
      "Cache-Control": cacheControl,
      ...extraHeaders,
    },
  });
}

function wantsJson(request) {
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  const accept = request.headers.get("Accept") || "";
  return url.searchParams.get("info") === "true" || accept.includes("application/json");
}

function wantsHtml(request) {
  return (request.headers.get("Accept") || "").includes("text/html");
}

function formatDuration(seconds, lang = "zh") {
  const n = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(seconds) || 0));
  if (!n) return lang === "en" ? "soon" : "即将";
  const units = lang === "en"
    ? [[86400, "d"], [3600, "h"], [60, "m"], [1, "s"]]
    : [[86400, "天"], [3600, "小时"], [60, "分钟"], [1, "秒"]];
  const parts = [];
  let rest = n;
  for (const [size, label] of units) {
    const value = Math.floor(rest / size);
    if (value) {
      parts.push(`${value}${label}`);
      rest -= value * size;
    }
    if (parts.length >= 2) break;
  }
  return parts.join(lang === "en" ? " " : "");
}

function renderPublicErrorPage({ request, config = {}, status = 500, code = "error", title, message, detail = "", imageType = "generic", retryAfter = 0, actionText = "返回首页", actionHref = "/", site = DEFAULT_SITE_NAME }) {
  const lang = request ? getLang(request) : "zh";
  const en = lang === "en";
  const displayTitle = title || (en ? "Something went wrong" : "出了一点小问题");
  const displayMessage = message || (en ? "Please try again later." : "请稍后再试。日常的小波动，不影响继续使用。 ");
  const retry = retryAfter ? `<p class="muted" style="margin-top:10px">${en ? "Try again in" : "建议等待"} <strong>${escapeHtml(formatDuration(retryAfter, lang))}</strong></p>` : "";
  const art = `<div style="height:220px;border-radius:22px;border:1px solid var(--line);margin-bottom:22px;background:radial-gradient(circle at 30% 20%,rgba(167,139,250,.28),transparent 36%),radial-gradient(circle at 80% 10%,rgba(34,211,238,.22),transparent 34%),linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(15,23,42,.82),rgba(30,41,59,.48));display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:58px">✦</div>`;
  return htmlResponse(`<!doctype html><html lang="${en ? "en" : "zh-CN"}"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>${escapeHtml(displayTitle)} — ${escapeHtml(site)}</title>${commonPublicAssets()}<style>.error-shell{min-height:100vh;display:grid;place-items:center;padding:36px 18px}.error-card{width:min(760px,100%);text-align:center}.error-code{display:inline-flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;padding:6px 12px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(167,139,250,.14);border:1px solid rgba(167,139,250,.24);color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em}.error-card h1{font-size:clamp(32px,6vw,58px);margin:18px 0 12px}.error-card p{color:var(--muted);line-height:1.8}.error-actions{display:flex;gap:12px;justify-content:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:24px}</style></head><body><main class="error-shell"><section class="card error-card">${art}<span class="error-code">${escapeHtml(String(status))} · ${escapeHtml(code)}</span><h1>${escapeHtml(displayTitle)}</h1><p>${escapeHtml(displayMessage)}</p>${detail ? `<p class="muted">${escapeHtml(detail)}</p>` : ""}${retry}<div class="error-actions"><a class="btn btn-primary" href="${escapeAttr(actionHref)}">${escapeHtml(actionText)}</a><a class="btn" href="/docs">${en ? "Docs" : "查看文档"}</a></div></section></main></body></html>`, status);
}

function renderAdminErrorPage({ request, token = "", status = 500, title, message, detail = "", actionText, actionHref, site = DEFAULT_SITE_NAME }) {
  const lang = request ? getLang(request) : "zh";
  const en = lang === "en";
  const finalTitle = title || (en ? "Admin error" : "后台出现问题");
  const finalMessage = message || (en ? "Please refresh and try again." : "请刷新后重试。 ");
  const href = actionHref || (token ? `/${token}/login` : "/");
  const text = actionText || (en ? "Go back" : "返回");
  return adminHtmlResponse(`<!doctype html><html lang="${en ? "en" : "zh-CN"}"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>${escapeHtml(finalTitle)} — ${escapeHtml(site)}</title>${adminStyles()}<style>.admin-error{min-height:100vh;display:grid;place-items:center;padding:28px;background:radial-gradient(circle at 20% 20%,rgba(139,92,246,.18),transparent 34%),radial-gradient(circle at 80% 10%,rgba(6,182,212,.14),transparent 34%),var(--bg)}.admin-error-card{width:min(620px,100%);text-align:center}.admin-error-icon{width:70px;height:70px;border-radius:24px;margin:0 auto 20px;display:grid;place-items:center;background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(139,92,246,.24),rgba(6,182,212,.18));border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:32px}.admin-error-card p{color:var(--muted);line-height:1.8}</style></head><body><main class="admin-error"><section class="card admin-error-card"><div class="admin-error-icon">⚠</div><p class="muted">${escapeHtml(String(status))}</p><h1>${escapeHtml(finalTitle)}</h1><p>${escapeHtml(finalMessage)}</p>${detail ? `<p>${escapeHtml(detail)}</p>` : ""}<p style="margin-top:24px"><a class="btn" href="${escapeAttr(href)}">${escapeHtml(text)}</a></p></section></main></body></html>`, status);

function publicApiErrorResponse(data, status = 400, headers = {}) {
  return publicJsonResponse({ ok: false, ...data }, status, headers);
}

function classifyFetchError(result) {
  const text = JSON.stringify(result || {}).toLowerCase();
  if (/quota|rate.?limit|too many|exhaust|429|limit/.test(text)) return "quota";
  return "sourceFailed";
}

function parseCookies(request) {
  const header = request.headers.get("Cookie") || "";
  const cookies = {};
  for (const part of header.split(";")) {
    const idx = part.indexOf("=");
    if (idx === -1) continue;
    cookies[part.slice(0, idx).trim()] = decodeURIComponent(part.slice(idx + 1).trim());
  }
  return cookies;
}

// Generic JSON config helpers — D1-backed using the `config` key-value table.
async function getJsonValue(db, key, defaultValue) {
  try {
    const row = await db.prepare("SELECT value FROM config WHERE key = ?").bind(key).first();
    if (row) { try { return JSON.parse(row.value); } catch (_) { return row.value; } }
  } catch (_) {}
  return defaultValue;
}

async function setJsonValue(db, key, value) {
  try {
    await db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(key, JSON.stringify(value)).run();
  } catch (_) {}
}

function safeJsonParse(raw, fallback) {
  try { return JSON.parse(raw); } catch (_) { return fallback; }
}

function constantTimeEqual(a, b) {
  a = String(a); b = String(b);
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  let diff = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) diff |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);
  return diff === 0;
}

async function fetchWithTimeout(url, opts = {}, ms = 12000) {
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), ms);
  try { return await fetch(url, { ...opts, signal: ctrl.signal }); }
  finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
}

function daysBetween(d1, d2) {
  const a = Date.parse(d1 + "T00:00:00Z");
  const b = Date.parse(d2 + "T00:00:00Z");
  if (isNaN(a) || isNaN(b)) return 0;
  return Math.round((b - a) / 86400000);
}

async function d1GetConfig(env, key, defaultValue) {
  if (!env.DB) return null;
  try {
    const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM config WHERE key = ?").bind(key).first();
    return row ? row.value : defaultValue;
  } catch (_) { return null; }
}

async function d1SetConfig(env, key, value) {
  if (!env.DB) return;
  try {
    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") {
      await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM config WHERE key = ?").bind(key).run();
    } else {
      await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(key, String(value)).run();
    }
  } catch (_) {}
}

async function appendNotificationHistory(store, eventType, payload, status = "sent", env) {
  try {
    await env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO notification_history (event_type, status, payload, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(eventType, status, JSON.stringify(payload)).run();
  } catch (_) {}
}

async function getNotificationHistory(store, limit = 50, env) {
  try {
    const rows = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT event_type, status, payload, created_at FROM notification_history ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?").bind(limit).all();
    return (rows.results || []).map(r => ({ ts: r.created_at, eventType: r.event_type, status: r.status, payload: JSON.parse(r.payload || "{}") }));
  } catch (_) { return []; }
}

async function applyDynamicRateLimits(db, env, limits) {
  const quotas = [];
  try {
    const r = await db.prepare("SELECT source, limit_val, remaining FROM quota").all();
    const d1Quota = {};
    for (const row of (r.results || [])) d1Quota[row.source] = { limit: row.limit_val, remaining: row.remaining };
    for (const source of SOURCES) {
      const quota = d1Quota[source];
      if (quota && quota.limit && quota.remaining !== undefined && Number(quota.limit) > 0) {
        quotas.push(Number(quota.remaining) / Number(quota.limit));
      }
    }
  } catch (_) {}
  if (!quotas.length) return limits;
  const avgRemaining = quotas.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / quotas.length;
  if (avgRemaining >= 0.3) return limits;
  return {
    minute: Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(limits.minute || DEFAULT_RATE_LIMITS.minute) * 0.5)),
    hour: Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(limits.hour || DEFAULT_RATE_LIMITS.hour) * 0.5)),
    day: Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(limits.day || DEFAULT_RATE_LIMITS.day) * 0.5)),
    dynamic: true,
    quotaRemainingPercent: Math.round(avgRemaining * 100),
  };
}

// In-memory cache of runtime config. Cloudflare Worker isolates are reused across
// warm requests, so this avoids ~6 sequential D1 reads on every single API call.
// Short TTL keeps admin changes responsive; handleAdminApi invalidates on writes.
let runtimeConfigCache = null;
let runtimeConfigCacheTs = 0;
const RUNTIME_CONFIG_TTL_MS = 20000;

async function loadRuntimeConfig(db, env) {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (runtimeConfigCache && (now - runtimeConfigCacheTs) < RUNTIME_CONFIG_TTL_MS) return runtimeConfigCache;
  // Single round-trip for all config keys instead of sequential reads.
  const keys = [KV.CONFIG_CACHE_TTL, KV.CONFIG_CUSTOM_BG, KV.CONFIG_BG_ACCESS, KV.CONFIG_TURNSTILE, KV.CONFIG_BANNER_CONTENT, KV.CONFIG_BANNER_TYPE, KV.CONFIG_RATE_LIMITS, KV.CONFIG_BAN_DURATIONS, KV.CONFIG_SOURCE_ROUTING, KV.CONFIG_HOME, KV.CONFIG_QUALITY, KV.CONFIG_FEATURE_FLAGS];
  const placeholders = keys.map(() => "?").join(",");
  const map = {};
  try {
    const rows = await db.prepare(`SELECT key, value FROM config WHERE key IN (${placeholders})`).bind(...keys).all();
    for (const r of (rows.results || [])) map[r.key] = r.value;
  } catch (_) {}
  const cacheTtl = Math.max(60, parseInt(map[KV.CONFIG_CACHE_TTL] || DEFAULT_CACHE_TTL, 10) || DEFAULT_CACHE_TTL);
  const customBg = map[KV.CONFIG_CUSTOM_BG] || "";
  const bgAccess = map[KV.CONFIG_BG_ACCESS] ? safeJsonParse(map[KV.CONFIG_BG_ACCESS], DEFAULT_BG_ACCESS) : DEFAULT_BG_ACCESS;
  const turnstile = map[KV.CONFIG_TURNSTILE] ? safeJsonParse(map[KV.CONFIG_TURNSTILE], DEFAULT_TURNSTILE_CONFIG) : DEFAULT_TURNSTILE_CONFIG;
  const bannerRaw = map[KV.CONFIG_BANNER_CONTENT] || "";
  const bannerType = map[KV.CONFIG_BANNER_TYPE] || "fixed";
  const rateLimits = map[KV.CONFIG_RATE_LIMITS] ? safeJsonParse(map[KV.CONFIG_RATE_LIMITS], DEFAULT_RATE_LIMITS) : DEFAULT_RATE_LIMITS;
  const banDurations = map[KV.CONFIG_BAN_DURATIONS] ? safeJsonParse(map[KV.CONFIG_BAN_DURATIONS], DEFAULT_BAN_DURATIONS) : DEFAULT_BAN_DURATIONS;
  const sourceRouting = map[KV.CONFIG_SOURCE_ROUTING] ? safeJsonParse(map[KV.CONFIG_SOURCE_ROUTING], DEFAULT_SOURCE_ROUTING) : DEFAULT_SOURCE_ROUTING;
  const home = map[KV.CONFIG_HOME] ? safeJsonParse(map[KV.CONFIG_HOME], DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG) : DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG;
  const quality = map[KV.CONFIG_QUALITY] ? safeJsonParse(map[KV.CONFIG_QUALITY], DEFAULT_QUALITY_CONFIG) : DEFAULT_QUALITY_CONFIG;
  const featureFlags = map[KV.CONFIG_FEATURE_FLAGS] ? safeJsonParse(map[KV.CONFIG_FEATURE_FLAGS], DEFAULT_FEATURE_FLAGS) : DEFAULT_FEATURE_FLAGS;
  const bannerContent = bannerRaw ? bannerRaw.split(/\s*\|\|\|\s*/).filter(Boolean) : [];
  const dynamicRateLimits = await applyDynamicRateLimits(db, env, { ...DEFAULT_RATE_LIMITS, ...rateLimits });
  const result = { cacheTtl, customBg, bgAccess: { ...DEFAULT_BG_ACCESS, ...bgAccess }, turnstile: { ...DEFAULT_TURNSTILE_CONFIG, ...turnstile }, bannerContent, bannerType, rateLimits: dynamicRateLimits, baseRateLimits: { ...DEFAULT_RATE_LIMITS, ...rateLimits }, banDurations: { ...DEFAULT_BAN_DURATIONS, ...banDurations }, sourceRouting: { ...DEFAULT_SOURCE_ROUTING, ...sourceRouting, priority: Array.isArray(sourceRouting.priority) ? sourceRouting.priority.filter(s => SOURCES.includes(s)) : DEFAULT_SOURCE_ROUTING.priority }, home: normalizeHomeConfig(home), quality: { ...DEFAULT_QUALITY_CONFIG, ...quality }, featureFlags: { ...DEFAULT_FEATURE_FLAGS, ...featureFlags } };
  runtimeConfigCache = result;
  runtimeConfigCacheTs = now;
  return result;
}

function invalidateRuntimeConfigCache() {
  runtimeConfigCache = null;
  runtimeConfigCacheTs = 0;
}

async function appendLog(store, level, message, meta = {}, env) {
  try {
    await env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO error_logs (level, message, meta, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(level, String(message), JSON.stringify(meta)).run();
  } catch (_) {}
}

async function getLogs(store, limit = 50, env) {
  try {
    const rows = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT level, message, meta, created_at FROM error_logs ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?").bind(limit).all();
    return (rows.results || []).map(r => ({ ts: r.created_at, level: r.level, message: r.message, meta: JSON.parse(r.meta || "{}") }));
  } catch (_) { return []; }
}

async function clearLogs(store, env) {
  try { await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM error_logs").run(); } catch (_) {}
}

function normalizeQuery(value) {
  const q = String(value || "wallpaper").trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ").slice(0, 100);
  return q || "wallpaper";
}

function normalizeOrientation(value) {
  return ALLOWED_ORIENTATIONS.includes(value) ? value : "all";
}

function normalizeColor(value) {
  const color = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[-\s]+/g, "_");
  return ALLOWED_COLORS.includes(color) ? color : "";
}

function normalizeAspectRatio(value) {
  const ratio = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  return ALLOWED_ASPECT_RATIOS.includes(ratio) ? ratio : "";
}

function normalizeDimension(value) {
  const n = parseInt(value, 10);
  return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? Math.min(12000, n) : 0;
}

function normalizeBooleanParam(value, defaultValue = false) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") return defaultValue;
  return !["0", "false", "off", "no"].includes(String(value).toLowerCase());
}

function normalizeSlug(value) {
  return String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/g, "-").replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "").slice(0, 60);
}

function splitLines(value, fallback, limit = 4) {
  const items = String(value || "").split(/\r?\n|\s*\|\|\|\s*/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean).slice(0, limit);
  return items.length ? items : fallback;
}

function normalizeHomeConfig(value = {}) {
  const cfg = { ...DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG, ...(value || {}) };
  return {
    titlesZh: Array.isArray(cfg.titlesZh) ? cfg.titlesZh.map(s => String(s).trim()).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 6) : DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.titlesZh,
    titlesEn: Array.isArray(cfg.titlesEn) ? cfg.titlesEn.map(s => String(s).trim()).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 6) : DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.titlesEn,
    heroZh: String(cfg.heroZh || DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.heroZh).trim().slice(0, 220),
    heroEn: String(cfg.heroEn || DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.heroEn).trim().slice(0, 220),
    randomZh: String(cfg.randomZh || DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.randomZh).trim().slice(0, 80),
    randomEn: String(cfg.randomEn || DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.randomEn).trim().slice(0, 80),
    builderZh: String(cfg.builderZh || DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.builderZh).trim().slice(0, 80),
    builderEn: String(cfg.builderEn || DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.builderEn).trim().slice(0, 80),
    docsZh: String(cfg.docsZh || DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.docsZh).trim().slice(0, 80),
    docsEn: String(cfg.docsEn || DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.docsEn).trim().slice(0, 80),
    backgroundQuery: normalizeQuery(cfg.backgroundQuery || DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.backgroundQuery),
    dailyQuery: normalizeQuery(cfg.dailyQuery || DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.dailyQuery),
  };
}

function normalizeQuality(value, fallback = DEFAULT_QUALITY_CONFIG.default) {
  const q = String(value || fallback || "balanced").toLowerCase();
  return ["fast", "balanced", "high"].includes(q) ? q : "balanced";
}

function scoreImageQuality(image, params = {}, sourceHealth = {}) {
  const w = Number(image?.width || 0);
  const h = Number(image?.height || 0);
  const pixels = w * h;
  let score = 40;
  if (pixels >= 3840 * 2160) score += 30;
  else if (pixels >= 2560 * 1440) score += 24;
  else if (pixels >= 1920 * 1080) score += 18;
  else if (pixels >= 1280 * 720) score += 10;
  if (params.orientation && params.orientation !== "all" && image.orientation === params.orientation) score += 10;
  if (params.aspectRatio && w && h) {
    const ratio = w / h;
    if (params.aspectRatio === "wide" && ratio >= 1.6) score += 8;
    if (params.aspectRatio === "tall" && ratio <= 0.75) score += 8;
    if (params.aspectRatio === "square" && Math.abs(w - h) / Math.max(w, h) <= 0.18) score += 8;
  }
  const health = sourceHealth[image?.source || params.source];
  if (health?.ok) score += 6;
  if (health?.latency) score -= Math.min(8, Math.round(Number(health.latency) / 700));
  if (image?.author && image.author !== "Unknown") score += 3;
  if (image?.source_url) score += 2;
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(score)));
}

function applyQualityScores(images, params = {}, sourceHealth = {}) {
  const q = normalizeQuality(params.quality);
  const scored = (Array.isArray(images) ? images : []).map(img => ({ ...img, quality_score: scoreImageQuality(img, params, sourceHealth), quality_mode: q }));
  if (q === "fast") return scored;
  scored.sort((a, b) => Number(b.quality_score || 0) - Number(a.quality_score || 0));
  if (q === "high") {
    const high = scored.filter(img => Number(img.quality_score || 0) >= DEFAULT_QUALITY_CONFIG.highThreshold);
    return high.length ? high : scored;
  }
  return scored;
}

function canonicalParamsForCache(params) {
  return {
    query: normalizeQuery(params.query).toLowerCase(),
    orientation: params.orientation || "all",
    color: params.color || "",
    min_width: Number(params.minWidth || 0),
    min_height: Number(params.minHeight || 0),
    aspect_ratio: params.aspectRatio || "",
    safe: params.safeMode === false ? "0" : "1",
    expand: params.expand === false ? "0" : "1",
    quality: normalizeQuality(params.quality),
  };
}

function filterImagesByParams(images, params) {
  let out = Array.isArray(images) ? images.filter(Boolean) : [];
  if (params.minWidth) out = out.filter(img => !img.width || Number(img.width) >= params.minWidth);
  if (params.minHeight) out = out.filter(img => !img.height || Number(img.height) >= params.minHeight);
  if (params.aspectRatio === "wide") out = out.filter(img => !img.width || !img.height || Number(img.width) / Number(img.height) >= 1.6);
  if (params.aspectRatio === "tall") out = out.filter(img => !img.width || !img.height || Number(img.height) / Number(img.width) >= 1.35);
  if (params.aspectRatio === "square") out = out.filter(img => !img.width || !img.height || Math.abs(Number(img.width) - Number(img.height)) / Math.max(Number(img.width), Number(img.height)) <= 0.18);
  return out;
}

function buildApiPath(params = {}, includeInfo = false) {
  const u = new URL("https://omniwall.local/api/random");
  const set = (k, v) => { if (v !== undefined && v !== null && String(v) !== "" && String(v) !== "0") u.searchParams.set(k, String(v)); };
  set("preset", params.preset);
  set("query", params.query);
  if (params.orientation && params.orientation !== "all") set("orientation", params.orientation);
  if (params.source && params.source !== "random") set("source", params.source);
  set("color", params.color);
  set("min_width", params.minWidth || params.min_width);
  set("min_height", params.minHeight || params.min_height);
  set("aspect", params.aspectRatio || params.aspect || params.aspect_ratio);
  if (params.quality && params.quality !== "balanced") u.searchParams.set("quality", params.quality);
  if (params.expand === false) u.searchParams.set("expand", "false");
  if (includeInfo) u.searchParams.set("info", "true");
  return u.pathname + u.search;
}
