function detectDangerousInput(value) {
  const text = String(value || "");
  const patterns = [
    /<\s*script/i,
    /javascript\s*:/i,
    /\son[a-z]+\s*=/i,
    /data\s*:\s*text\/html/i,
    /vbscript\s*:/i,
    /<\s*iframe/i,
    /eval\s*\(/i,
    /expression\s*\(/i,
    /\bimport\s*\(/i,
    /__proto__/i,
    /constructor\s*\[/i,
  ];
  const match = patterns.find(pattern => pattern.test(text));
  return match ? { ok: false, reason: "dangerous_pattern", pattern: String(match) } : { ok: true };
}

async function getKeywords(store, env) {
  try {
    const rows = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT word, reason FROM keywords ORDER BY id ASC").all();
    if (rows.results) return rows.results.map(r => ({ word: r.word, reason: r.reason || "" }));
  } catch (_) {}
  return [];
}

async function moderateContent(text, env) {
  const local = detectDangerousInput(text);
  if (!local.ok) return { ok: false, provider: "local", reason: local.reason, pattern: local.pattern };
  const lowered = String(text || "").toLowerCase();
  for (const word of BUILTIN_KEYWORDS) {
    if (word && lowered.includes(word)) return { ok: false, provider: "builtin_keywords", reason: "keyword", severity: "moderate" };
  }
  const keywords = await getKeywords(env.DB, env);
  for (const item of keywords) {
    const word = String(item.word || item).toLowerCase();
    if (word && lowered.includes(word)) return { ok: false, provider: "local_keywords", reason: item.reason || "keyword", severity: item.severity || "moderate" };
  }
  const external = await runExternalModeration(text, env);
  return external || { ok: true, provider: "local" };
}

async function runExternalModeration(text, env) {
  const errors = [];
  if (env.OPENAI_API_KEY) {
    try {
      const response = await fetch("https://api.openai.com/v1/moderations", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json", Authorization: `Bearer ${env.OPENAI_API_KEY}` },
        body: JSON.stringify({ model: "omni-moderation-latest", input: String(text || "") }),
      });
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        const result = data.results && data.results[0];
        if (result?.flagged) return { ok: false, provider: "openai", reason: "flagged", categories: result.categories };
      } else errors.push(`openai:${response.status}`);
    } catch (error) { errors.push(`openai:${error.message}`); }
  }
  if (env.PERSPECTIVE_API_KEY) {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`https://commentanalyzer.googleapis.com/v1alpha1/comments:analyze?key=${encodeURIComponent(env.PERSPECTIVE_API_KEY)}`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ comment: { text: String(text || "") }, languages: ["zh", "en"], requestedAttributes: { TOXICITY: {}, INSULT: {}, PROFANITY: {}, THREAT: {} } }),
      });
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        const scores = data.attributeScores || {};
        for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(scores)) {
          if ((value.summaryScore?.value || 0) >= 0.85) return { ok: false, provider: "perspective", reason: name, score: value.summaryScore.value };
        }
      } else errors.push(`perspective:${response.status}`);
    } catch (error) { errors.push(`perspective:${error.message}`); }
  }
  if (env.AZURE_CONTENT_SAFETY_ENDPOINT && env.AZURE_CONTENT_SAFETY_KEY) {
    try {
      const endpoint = env.AZURE_CONTENT_SAFETY_ENDPOINT.replace(/\/$/, "");
      const response = await fetch(`${endpoint}/contentsafety/text:analyze?api-version=2023-10-01`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "Ocp-Apim-Subscription-Key": env.AZURE_CONTENT_SAFETY_KEY },
        body: JSON.stringify({ text: String(text || ""), categories: ["Hate", "Sexual", "SelfHarm", "Violence"] }),
      });
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        const bad = (data.categoriesAnalysis || []).find(item => Number(item.severity || 0) >= 4);
        if (bad) return { ok: false, provider: "azure", reason: bad.category, severity: bad.severity };
      } else errors.push(`azure:${response.status}`);
    } catch (error) { errors.push(`azure:${error.message}`); }
  }
  if (errors.length) return { ok: true, provider: "external_failed", suspicious: true, errors };
  return null;
}

function validatePublicInput(params) {
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(params)) {
    if (value === undefined || value === null || value === "") continue;
    if (String(value).length > 200) return { ok: false, reason: `${name} is too long` };
    if (/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/.test(String(value))) return { ok: false, reason: `${name} contains control characters` };
    const dangerous = detectDangerousInput(value);
    if (!dangerous.ok) return { ok: false, reason: `${name} contains ${dangerous.reason}` };
  }
  return { ok: true };
}

function getClientIp(request) {
  return request.headers.get("CF-Connecting-IP") || request.headers.get("X-Forwarded-For")?.split(",")[0]?.trim() || "unknown";
}

async function hashIdentifier(value, secret) {
  return (await sha256Hex(`${secret}:${value}`)).slice(0, 48);
}

async function checkBan(request, env) {
  const ipHash = await hashIdentifier(getClientIp(request), env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "fallback");
  try {
    const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM bans WHERE ip_hash = ?").bind(ipHash).first();
    if (row) {
      if (row.expires_at && Date.parse(row.expires_at) <= Date.now()) {
        await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM bans WHERE ip_hash = ?").bind(ipHash).run();
        return { ok: true, ipHash };
      }
      return { ok: false, ipHash, ban: { reason: row.reason, level: row.level, created_at: row.created_at, expires_at: row.expires_at } };
    }
  } catch (_) {}
  return { ok: true, ipHash };
}

function isBgAccessBanReason(reason) {
  return /^bg_(token|cookie|signature)/.test(String(reason || ""));
}

async function clearBanByHash(db, ipHash) {
  try { await db.prepare("DELETE FROM bans WHERE ip_hash = ?").bind(ipHash).run(); } catch (_) {}
}

async function checkWhitelist(db, ipHash, env) {
  try {
    const row = await db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM whitelist WHERE ip_hash = ?").bind(ipHash).first();
    return !!row;
  } catch (_) { return false; }
}

async function getApiKeyCredential(request) {
  const auth = request.headers.get("Authorization") || "";
  if (auth.toLowerCase().startsWith("bearer ")) return auth.slice(7).trim();
  return (request.headers.get("X-API-Key") || "").trim();
}

async function authenticateApiKey(request, env) {
  const key = await getApiKeyCredential(request);
  if (!key) return { ok: true, key: null };
  if (!/^ow_[A-Za-z0-9_-]{24,}$/.test(key)) return { ok: false, response: publicApiErrorResponse({ error: "invalid_api_key", message: "API Key 格式无效" }, 401, { "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*" }) };
  const keyHash = await sha256Hex(key + ":" + (env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "fallback"));
  try {
    const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id, name, key_prefix, status, minute_limit, hour_limit, day_limit FROM api_keys WHERE key_hash = ?").bind(keyHash).first();
    if (!row || row.status !== "active") return { ok: false, response: publicApiErrorResponse({ error: "invalid_api_key", message: "API Key 不存在或已禁用" }, 401, { "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*" }) };
    await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE api_keys SET last_used_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?").bind(row.id).run();
    return { ok: true, key: { id: row.id, name: row.name, prefix: row.key_prefix, identity: `api:${row.id}`, limits: { minute: row.minute_limit || DEFAULT_API_KEY_LIMITS.minute, hour: row.hour_limit || DEFAULT_API_KEY_LIMITS.hour, day: row.day_limit || DEFAULT_API_KEY_LIMITS.day } } };
  } catch (_) {
    return { ok: false, response: publicApiErrorResponse({ error: "api_key_check_failed", message: "API Key 校验失败" }, 500, { "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*" }) };
  }
}

async function checkRateLimit(request, env, config, principal = null) {
  const ipHash = principal?.identity ? await hashIdentifier(principal.identity, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "fallback") : await hashIdentifier(getClientIp(request), env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "fallback");
  if (!principal && await checkWhitelist(env.DB, ipHash, env)) return { ok: true, ipHash, whitelisted: true };
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  let data;
  try {
    const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT timestamps FROM rate_limits WHERE ip_hash = ? AND window_type = 'combined'").bind(ipHash).first();
    data = row ? JSON.parse(row.timestamps) : { m: [], h: [], d: [] };
  } catch (_) { data = { m: [], h: [], d: [] }; }
  const windows = [
    ["minute", 60, principal?.limits?.minute || config.rateLimits.minute, "m"],
    ["hour", 3600, principal?.limits?.hour || config.rateLimits.hour, "h"],
    ["day", 86400, principal?.limits?.day || config.rateLimits.day, "d"],
  ];
  for (const [name, ttl, limit, code] of windows) {
    data[code] = data[code].filter(t => now - t < ttl);
    data[code].push(now);
    if (data[code].length > Number(limit)) {
      await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO rate_limits (ip_hash, window_type, timestamps, updated_at) VALUES (?, 'combined', ?, datetime('now'))").bind(ipHash, JSON.stringify(data)).run();
      return { ok: false, ipHash, window: name, limit, retryAfter: ttl };
    }
  }
  await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO rate_limits (ip_hash, window_type, timestamps, updated_at) VALUES (?, 'combined', ?, datetime('now'))").bind(ipHash, JSON.stringify(data)).run();
  return { ok: true, ipHash };
}

function publicSecurityErrorResponse(request, config, type, payload = {}, status = 403, headers = {}) {
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const retryAfter = Number(payload.retryAfter || 0);
  const expiresAt = payload.expiresAt || "";
  const remainingSeconds = expiresAt ? Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.parse(expiresAt) - Date.now()) / 1000)) : retryAfter;
  const message = payload.message || (type === "banned"
    ? (lang === "en" ? "Your access is currently restricted." : "当前访问已被限制。")
    : (lang === "en" ? "Too many requests. Please slow down." : "访问过于频繁，请稍后再试。"));
  if (wantsJson(request)) {
    return publicApiErrorResponse({ error: type, message, reason: payload.reason || "", retryAfter, expiresAt, remainingSeconds }, status, headers);
  }
  return renderPublicErrorPage({
    request,
    config,
    status,
    code: type,
    title: type === "banned" ? (lang === "en" ? "Access restricted" : "访问已受限") : (lang === "en" ? "Please slow down" : "请稍等片刻"),
    message,
    detail: expiresAt ? `${lang === "en" ? "Restriction lifts at" : "预计解除时间"}: ${expiresAt}` : "",
    imageType: type === "banned" ? "banned" : "rateLimit",
    retryAfter: remainingSeconds,
    actionText: lang === "en" ? "View docs" : "查看文档",
    actionHref: "/docs",
  });
}

async function handleRateLimitViolation(request, env, ctx, ipHash, window, config) {
  if (await checkWhitelist(env.DB, ipHash, env)) return null;
  const violKey = `violations:${ipHash}`;
  const today = todayUtc8();
  let current = 0;
  const parseViol = (raw) => {
    if (!raw) return 0;
    const m = String(raw).match(/^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}):(\d+)$/);
    if (m) return daysBetween(m[1], today) >= 7 ? 0 : parseInt(m[2], 10);
    return parseInt(raw, 10) || 0;
  };
  try {
    const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM config WHERE key = ?").bind(violKey).first();
    current = parseViol(row?.value);
    await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(violKey, `${today}:${current + 1}`).run();
  } catch (_) { current = 0; }
  const next = current + 1;
  const levels = [
    { count: 1, seconds: 0, msg: "" },
    { count: 2, seconds: 86400, msg: "您因调用频率过快已被封禁 24 小时" },
    { count: 3, seconds: 604800, msg: "您因调用频率过快已被封禁 7 天" },
    { count: 4, seconds: 7776000, msg: "您因调用频率过快已被封禁 3 个月" },
  ];
  let matched = levels.find(l => next === l.count);
  if (!matched && next >= 5) matched = { count: next, seconds: 31536000, msg: "您因多次违规已被永久封禁" };
  if (matched && matched.seconds > 0) {
    await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO bans (ip_hash, reason, level, created_at, expires_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)").bind(ipHash, "auto_rate_limit", next, nowIso(), new Date(Date.now() + matched.seconds * 1000).toISOString()).run();
    ctx.waitUntil(sendNotification("ip_banned", { ip: ipHash.slice(0, 16), reason: "auto_rate_limit", level: next, seconds: matched.seconds }, env));
    return publicSecurityErrorResponse(request, config, "banned", { message: matched.msg, reason: "auto_rate_limit", retryAfter: matched.seconds, expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + matched.seconds * 1000).toISOString() }, 403, { "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*" });
  }
  return publicSecurityErrorResponse(request, config, "rate_limited", { message: `您访问频率过快（${window}窗口），请稍后再试`, reason: window, retryAfter: 60 }, 429, { "Retry-After": "60", "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*" });
}

async function applyPublicSecurity(request, env, ctx, config) {
  const ban = await checkBan(request, env);
  if (!ban.ok) {
    return publicSecurityErrorResponse(request, config, "banned", { reason: ban.ban?.reason || "ip_banned", expiresAt: ban.ban?.expires_at || "" }, 403, { "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*" });
  }
  const rate = await checkRateLimit(request, env, config);
  if (!rate.ok) {
    return await handleRateLimitViolation(request, env, ctx, rate.ipHash, rate.window, config);
  }
  return null;
}

async function applyApiKeyLimit(request, env, config, apiKey) {
  if (!apiKey) return null;
  const rate = await checkRateLimit(request, env, config, apiKey);
  if (rate.ok) return null;
  return publicSecurityErrorResponse(request, config, "rate_limited", { message: `API Key 调用频率过快（${rate.window}窗口）`, reason: `api_key_${rate.window}`, retryAfter: rate.retryAfter || 60 }, 429, { "Retry-After": String(rate.retryAfter || 60), "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*" });
}

async function buildCacheKey(source, queryOrParams, orientation) {
  if (source === "bing") return `cache:bing_daily:${todayUtc8()}`;
  const params = typeof queryOrParams === "object" && queryOrParams !== null ? queryOrParams : { query: queryOrParams, orientation };
  const keyPayload = JSON.stringify(canonicalParamsForCache(params));
  const queryHash = (await sha256Hex(keyPayload)).slice(0, 20);
  return `cache:${source}:${queryHash}`;
}

function imagePublicCopy(image) {
  const copy = { ...image };
  delete copy.raw_url;
  return copy;
}

async function putCachedPool(store, cacheKey, source, params, images, ttl, password, env) {
  const encryptedImages = [];
  for (const image of images) {
    const plainUrl = image.raw_url || image.url;
    encryptedImages.push({ ...imagePublicCopy(image), url: await encryptURL(plainUrl, password) });
  }
  const createdAt = nowIso();
  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + ttl * 1000).toISOString();
  const payload = {
    version: 2, source, query: params.query, orientation: params.orientation,
    created_at: createdAt, expires_at: expiresAt, images: encryptedImages,
  };
  const pStr = JSON.stringify(payload);
  try { await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO image_cache (cache_key, payload, expires_at, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)").bind(cacheKey, pStr, expiresAt, createdAt).run(); } catch (_) {}
}

async function decryptCachedImage(image, password) {
  const finalUrl = await decryptURL(image.url, password);
  // If decryption fails, return null URL so callers can skip this image
  // instead of showing an unusable v2:xxx encrypted string to end users.
  return { ...image, url: finalUrl === "FAIL" ? "" : finalUrl, _decryptFailed: finalUrl === "FAIL" };
}

const recentImageIds = new Map();

function imageIdentity(image) {
  return String(image?.id || image?.url || image?.raw_url || "");
}

function rememberRecentImage(cacheKey, image) {
  if (!cacheKey || !image) return;
  const id = imageIdentity(image);
  if (!id) return;
  const recent = (recentImageIds.get(cacheKey) || []).filter(item => item !== id);
  recent.unshift(id);
  recentImageIds.set(cacheKey, recent.slice(0, RECENT_IMAGE_LIMIT));
}

function selectRandomImage(images, cacheKey = "") {
  if (!Array.isArray(images) || images.length === 0) return null;
  let pool = images;
  if (cacheKey && images.length > 1) {
    const recent = new Set(recentImageIds.get(cacheKey) || []);
    const fresh = images.filter(image => !recent.has(imageIdentity(image)));
    if (fresh.length) pool = fresh;
  }
  const image = pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)];
  rememberRecentImage(cacheKey, image);
  return image;
}

function normalizeLegacyImage(image) {
  const copy = { ...image };
  if (copy.source === "Unsplash") copy.source = "unsplash";
  if (!copy.id && copy.url) copy.id = "legacy";
  return copy;
}

async function getCachedPool(store, cacheKey, password, env) {
  let raw = null;
  try {
    const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT payload FROM image_cache WHERE cache_key = ? AND (expires_at IS NULL OR julianday(expires_at) > julianday('now'))").bind(cacheKey).first();
    if (row) raw = row.payload;
  } catch (_) {}
  if (!raw) return null;
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (parsed && parsed.version === 2 && Array.isArray(parsed.images)) return parsed;
    if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
      return { version: 1, source: "unsplash", query: "legacy", orientation: "all", images: parsed.map(normalizeLegacyImage) };
    }
    return { version: 1, source: parsed.source || "unsplash", query: "legacy", orientation: "all", images: [normalizeLegacyImage(parsed)] };
  } catch (_) {
    return { version: 0, source: "legacy", query: "legacy", orientation: "all", images: [{ url: raw, source: "Legacy" }] };
  }
}

function mapOrientationForPexels(orientation) {
  if (orientation === "squarish") return "square";
  if (["landscape", "portrait"].includes(orientation)) return orientation;
  return "";
}

function mapOrientationForPixabay(orientation) {
  if (orientation === "landscape") return "horizontal";
  if (orientation === "portrait") return "vertical";
  return "";
}

function inferOrientation(width, height) {
  if (!width || !height) return "unknown";
  if (Math.abs(width - height) / Math.max(width, height) < 0.1) return "squarish";
  return width > height ? "landscape" : "portrait";
}

async function updateQuota(store, source, quota, env = null) {
  if (!quota) return;
  if (quota.limit !== undefined) {
    try {
      await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO quota (source, used, limit_val, remaining, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(source, Number(quota.used || 0), Number(quota.limit || 0), Number(quota.remaining || 0)).run();
    } catch (_) {}
  }
  if (env && quota.limit && quota.remaining !== undefined && Number(quota.limit) > 0) {
    const percent = Number(quota.remaining) / Number(quota.limit);
    if (percent < 0.1) {
      try {
        const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM config WHERE key = ?").bind(`quota_warn:${source}`).first();
        const lastWarn = row ? row.value : "";
        if (lastWarn !== String(quota.remaining)) {
          await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(`quota_warn:${source}`, String(quota.remaining)).run();
          await sendNotification("quota_warning", { source, remaining: quota.remaining, limit: quota.limit, percent: Math.round(percent * 100) }, env);
        }
      } catch (_) {}
    }
  }
}

function parseQuotaHeaders(response, source, defaultLimit = null) {
  const limit = response.headers.get("x-ratelimit-limit") || response.headers.get("X-Ratelimit-Limit") || defaultLimit;
  const remaining = response.headers.get("x-ratelimit-remaining") || response.headers.get("X-Ratelimit-Remaining");
  const reset = response.headers.get("x-ratelimit-reset") || response.headers.get("X-Ratelimit-Reset");
  const quota = { source };
  if (limit !== null && limit !== undefined) quota.limit = Number(limit);
  if (remaining !== null && remaining !== undefined) quota.remaining = Number(remaining);
  if (reset) quota.reset_at = /^\d+$/.test(reset) ? new Date(Number(reset) * 1000).toISOString() : reset;
  if (quota.limit && quota.remaining !== undefined) quota.used = Math.max(0, quota.limit - quota.remaining);
  return quota;
}

async function doTranslate(text, env) {
  // Custom translation API (highest priority, requires env setup)
  if (env.TRANSLATE_API_URL) {
    try {
      const endpoint = new URL(env.TRANSLATE_API_URL);
      endpoint.searchParams.set("q", text);
      endpoint.searchParams.set("target", "en");
      const response = await fetch(endpoint.toString(), { headers: env.TRANSLATE_API_KEY ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${env.TRANSLATE_API_KEY}` } : {} });
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        const out = data.translatedText || data.translation || data.text || text;
        if (out && out !== text) return out;
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }
  // Google Translate (free, no API key needed)
  try {
    const api = new URL("https://translate.googleapis.com/translate_a/single");
    api.searchParams.set("client", "gtx");
    api.searchParams.set("sl", "auto");
    api.searchParams.set("tl", "en");
    api.searchParams.set("dt", "t");
    api.searchParams.set("q", text);
    const response = await fetch(api.toString());
    if (response.ok) {
      const data = await response.json();
      const result = data?.[0]?.map?.(s => s?.[0]).filter(Boolean).join("") || text;
      if (result && result !== text) return result;
    }
  } catch (_) {}
  // MyMemory API (fallback)
  try {
    const api = new URL("https://api.mymemory.translated.net/get");
    api.searchParams.set("q", text);
    api.searchParams.set("langpair", "zh-CN|en");
    const response = await fetch(api.toString());
    if (response.ok) {
      const data = await response.json();
      return data.responseData?.translatedText || text;
    }
  } catch (_) {}
  return text;
}

async function translateQueryIfNeeded(query, env) {
  const text = String(query || "").trim();
  if (!text || /^[\x00-\x7F]*$/.test(text)) return text;
  const cacheKey = `trans:${(await sha256Hex(text.toLowerCase())).slice(0, 16)}`;
  try { const r = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM config WHERE key = ?").bind(cacheKey).first(); if (r && r.value) return r.value; } catch (_) {}
  const translated = await doTranslate(text, env);
  try { await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(cacheKey, translated).run(); } catch (_) {}
  return translated;
}


const QUERY_EXPANSIONS = {
  nature: ["landscape", "forest", "mountain", "wallpaper"],
  city: ["urban", "street", "architecture", "night"],
  cat: ["cute cat", "kitten", "pet"],
  dog: ["dog", "puppy", "pet"],
  anime: ["illustration", "wallpaper", "digital art"],
  dark: ["minimal", "black", "moody"],
  minimal: ["clean", "simple", "abstract"],
  ocean: ["sea", "waves", "coast"],
  space: ["stars", "galaxy", "nebula"],
  flower: ["botanical", "bloom", "macro"],
};

async function expandQueryIfNeeded(query, env, config, params = {}) {
  const text = normalizeQuery(query);
  if (params.expand === false || config?.featureFlags?.keywordExpansion === false) return { query: text, expanded: false, terms: [] };
  const cacheKey = `expand:${(await sha256Hex(text.toLowerCase())).slice(0, 16)}`;
  try {
    const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM config WHERE key = ?").bind(cacheKey).first();
    if (row?.value) return { ...safeJsonParse(row.value, { query: text, expanded: false, terms: [] }), cached: true };
  } catch (_) {}
  const lowered = text.toLowerCase();
  const terms = [];
  for (const [key, values] of Object.entries(QUERY_EXPANSIONS)) {
    if (lowered.includes(key)) terms.push(...values);
  }
  const unique = [...new Set(terms.filter(t => !lowered.includes(String(t).toLowerCase())))].slice(0, 3);
  const expandedQuery = unique.length ? `${text} ${unique.join(" ")}`.slice(0, 160) : text;
  const result = { query: expandedQuery, expanded: unique.length > 0, terms: unique };
  try { await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(result)).run(); } catch (_) {}
  return result;
}

function mergePresetParams(base, preset) {
  if (!preset?.params) return base;
  const merged = { ...base };
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(preset.params)) {
    if (value !== undefined && value !== null && String(value) !== "" && (merged[key] === undefined || merged[key] === null || String(merged[key]) === "" || merged[key] === "random" || merged[key] === "all")) merged[key] = value;
  }
  return merged;
}

async function getPresetBySlug(db, slug) {
  const clean = normalizeSlug(slug);
  if (!clean) return null;
  const builtin = BUILTIN_PRESETS.find(preset => preset.slug === clean);
  if (builtin) return builtin;
  try {
    const row = await db.prepare("SELECT slug, name, description, params_json, is_public, created_at, updated_at FROM presets WHERE slug = ? AND is_public = 1").bind(clean).first();
    if (!row) return null;
    return { slug: row.slug, name: row.name, description: row.description || "", params: safeJsonParse(row.params_json, {}), builtin: false, created_at: row.created_at, updated_at: row.updated_at };
  } catch (_) { return null; }
}

async function getAllPresets(db) {
  let custom = [];
  try {
    const rows = await db.prepare("SELECT slug, name, description, params_json, is_public, created_at, updated_at FROM presets ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 100").all();
    custom = (rows.results || []).map(row => ({ slug: row.slug, name: row.name, description: row.description || "", params: safeJsonParse(row.params_json, {}), builtin: false, is_public: !!row.is_public, created_at: row.created_at, updated_at: row.updated_at }));
  } catch (_) {}
  return [...BUILTIN_PRESETS, ...custom];
}

async function trackRequestAnalytics(env, request, params, result, startMs, ipHash, status = 200) {
  try {
    const latency = Math.max(0, Date.now() - startMs);
    await env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO request_analytics (path, source, query, orientation, color, aspect_ratio, cache_hit, ok, status, latency, ip_hash, api_key_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)")
      .bind(new URL(request.url).pathname, result?.source || params.source || "random", String(params.originalQuery || params.query || "").slice(0, 120), params.orientation || "all", params.color || "", params.aspectRatio || "", result?.cacheHit ? 1 : 0, result?.ok ? 1 : 0, Number(status) || 200, latency, ipHash || "", params.apiKeyId || "")
      .run();
  } catch (_) {}
}

async function fetchUnsplashImages(params, env, ctx) {
  if (!env.UNSPLASH_ACCESS_KEY) return { ok: false, status: 503, error: "UNSPLASH_ACCESS_KEY is not configured" };
  let apiUrl = `https://api.unsplash.com/photos/random?client_id=${encodeURIComponent(env.UNSPLASH_ACCESS_KEY)}&query=${encodeURIComponent(params.query)}&count=${IMAGE_POOL_SIZE}`;
  if (ALLOWED_ORIENTATIONS.includes(params.orientation)) apiUrl += `&orientation=${params.orientation}`;
  if (params.color) apiUrl += `&color=${encodeURIComponent(params.color)}`;
  const response = await fetch(apiUrl);
  const quota = parseQuotaHeaders(response, "unsplash", 5000);
  ctx.waitUntil(updateQuota(env.DB, "unsplash", quota, env));
  if (!response.ok) return { ok: false, status: response.status, error: `Unsplash API ${response.status}` };
  const data = await response.json();
  const list = Array.isArray(data) ? data : [data];
  const images = list.filter(img => img && img.urls).map(img => ({
    id: img.id,
    url: img.urls.regular,
    raw_url: img.urls.regular,
    title: img.description || img.alt_description || "Unsplash Image",
    author: img.user?.name || "Unknown",
    author_url: img.user?.links?.html || "",
    source: "unsplash",
    source_url: img.links?.html || "",
    download_url: img.links?.download_location || "",
    width: img.width,
    height: img.height,
    orientation: inferOrientation(img.width, img.height),
    query: params.query,
    fetched_at: nowIso(),
  }));
  return images.length ? { ok: true, source: "unsplash", images, quota } : { ok: false, status: 404, error: "No Unsplash photos" };
}

async function fetchPexelsImages(params, env, ctx) {
  if (!env.PEXELS_API_KEY) return { ok: false, status: 503, error: "PEXELS_API_KEY is not configured" };
  const api = new URL("https://api.pexels.com/v1/search");
  api.searchParams.set("query", params.query);
  api.searchParams.set("per_page", String(IMAGE_POOL_SIZE));
  const mapped = mapOrientationForPexels(params.orientation);
  if (mapped) api.searchParams.set("orientation", mapped);
  if (params.color) api.searchParams.set("color", params.color === "black_and_white" ? "black" : params.color);
  if (params.minWidth >= 2400 || params.minHeight >= 1600) api.searchParams.set("size", "large");
  const response = await fetch(api.toString(), { headers: { Authorization: env.PEXELS_API_KEY } });
  const quota = parseQuotaHeaders(response, "pexels", 200);
  ctx.waitUntil(updateQuota(env.DB, "pexels", quota, env));
  if (!response.ok) return { ok: false, status: response.status, error: `Pexels API ${response.status}` };
  const data = await response.json();
  const photos = data.photos || [];
  const images = photos.map(photo => ({
    id: String(photo.id),
    url: photo.src?.large2x || photo.src?.large || photo.src?.original,
    raw_url: photo.src?.large2x || photo.src?.large || photo.src?.original,
    title: photo.alt || "Pexels Image",
    author: photo.photographer || "Unknown",
    author_url: photo.photographer_url || "",
    source: "pexels",
    source_url: photo.url || "",
    download_url: photo.url || "",
    width: photo.width,
    height: photo.height,
    orientation: inferOrientation(photo.width, photo.height),
    query: params.query,
    fetched_at: nowIso(),
  })).filter(img => img.url);
  return images.length ? { ok: true, source: "pexels", images, quota } : { ok: false, status: 404, error: "No Pexels photos" };
}

async function fetchPixabayImages(params, env, ctx) {
  if (!env.PIXABAY_API_KEY) return { ok: false, status: 503, error: "PIXABAY_API_KEY is not configured" };
  const api = new URL("https://pixabay.com/api/");
  api.searchParams.set("key", env.PIXABAY_API_KEY);
  api.searchParams.set("q", params.query);
  api.searchParams.set("per_page", String(IMAGE_POOL_SIZE));
  api.searchParams.set("safesearch", params.safeMode === false ? "false" : "true");
  api.searchParams.set("image_type", "photo");
  if (params.minWidth) api.searchParams.set("min_width", String(params.minWidth));
  if (params.minHeight) api.searchParams.set("min_height", String(params.minHeight));
  if (params.color) api.searchParams.set("colors", params.color === "black_and_white" ? "grayscale" : params.color);
  const mapped = mapOrientationForPixabay(params.orientation);
  if (mapped) api.searchParams.set("orientation", mapped);
  const response = await fetch(api.toString());
  const quota = parseQuotaHeaders(response, "pixabay", 5000);
  ctx.waitUntil(updateQuota(env.DB, "pixabay", quota, env));
  if (!response.ok) return { ok: false, status: response.status, error: `Pixabay API ${response.status}` };
  const data = await response.json();
  const images = (data.hits || []).map(hit => ({
    id: String(hit.id),
    url: hit.largeImageURL || hit.webformatURL,
    raw_url: hit.largeImageURL || hit.webformatURL,
    title: hit.tags || "Pixabay Image",
    author: hit.user || "Unknown",
    author_url: hit.user_id ? `https://pixabay.com/users/${encodeURIComponent(hit.user || "")}-${hit.user_id}/` : "",
    source: "pixabay",
    source_url: hit.pageURL || "",
    download_url: hit.pageURL || "",
    width: hit.imageWidth,
    height: hit.imageHeight,
    orientation: inferOrientation(hit.imageWidth, hit.imageHeight),
    query: params.query,
    fetched_at: nowIso(),
  })).filter(img => img.url);
  return images.length ? { ok: true, source: "pixabay", images, quota } : { ok: false, status: 404, error: "No Pixabay photos" };
}

async function fetchBingDailyImage(params, env, ctx) {
  const response = await fetch("https://www.bing.com/HPImageArchive.aspx?format=js&idx=0&n=1&mkt=en-US");
  // Bing has no rate-limit headers; skip quota update to avoid polluting the quota table.
  if (!response.ok) return { ok: false, status: response.status, error: `Bing API ${response.status}` };
  const data = await response.json();
  const item = data.images && data.images[0];
  if (!item) return { ok: false, status: 404, error: "No Bing daily image" };
  const imageUrl = item.url?.startsWith("http") ? item.url : `https://www.bing.com${item.url}`;
  const copyrightLink = item.copyrightlink?.startsWith("http") ? item.copyrightlink : `https://www.bing.com${item.copyrightlink || ""}`;
  return {
    ok: true,
    source: "bing",
    images: [{
      id: item.hsh || todayUtc8(),
      url: imageUrl,
      raw_url: imageUrl,
      title: item.title || item.copyright || "Bing Daily Wallpaper",
      author: "Bing",
      author_url: copyrightLink,
      source: "bing",
      source_url: copyrightLink,
      download_url: imageUrl,
      width: 1920,
      height: 1080,
      orientation: "landscape",
      query: "daily",
      fetched_at: nowIso(),
    }],
    quota: { source: "bing" },
  };
}

function getAvailableSources(env, params) {
  const available = [];
  if (env.UNSPLASH_ACCESS_KEY) available.push("unsplash");
  if (env.PEXELS_API_KEY) available.push("pexels");
  if (env.PIXABAY_API_KEY) available.push("pixabay");
  if (!params.hasExplicitQuery) available.push("bing");
  return available;
}

function shuffle(items) {
  const arr = [...items];
  for (let i = arr.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    [arr[i], arr[j]] = [arr[j], arr[i]];
  }
  return arr;
}

async function getLatestSourceHealthMap(env) {
  const map = {};
  try {
    const rows = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT source, ok, latency, status_code, checked_at FROM source_health WHERE checked_at >= datetime('now','-2 hours') ORDER BY checked_at DESC").all();
    for (const r of (rows.results || [])) if (!map[r.source]) map[r.source] = { ok: !!r.ok, latency: Number(r.latency) || 0, status: r.status_code, checked_at: r.checked_at, stale: (Date.now() - parseTs(r.checked_at)) > 600000 };
  } catch (_) {}
  return map;
}

async function getSourceCooldownMap(env) {
  const map = {};
  try {
    const rows = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT source, fail_count, disabled_until FROM source_failures WHERE disabled_until IS NOT NULL AND julianday(disabled_until) > julianday('now')").all();
    for (const r of (rows.results || [])) map[r.source] = { failCount: r.fail_count, disabledUntil: r.disabled_until };
  } catch (_) {}
  return map;
}

async function recordSourceFailure(env, source, config = {}) {
  const seconds = Math.max(60, Number(config.sourceRouting?.failureCooldownSeconds || DEFAULT_SOURCE_ROUTING.failureCooldownSeconds));
  try {
    await env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO source_failures (source, fail_count, last_error, disabled_until, updated_at) VALUES (?, 1, ?, datetime('now', ?), datetime('now')) ON CONFLICT(source) DO UPDATE SET fail_count = fail_count + 1, last_error = excluded.last_error, disabled_until = datetime('now', ?), updated_at = datetime('now')")
      .bind(source, "fetch_failed", `+${seconds} seconds`, `+${seconds} seconds`).run();
  } catch (_) {}
}

async function recordSourceSuccess(env, source) {
  try { await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM source_failures WHERE source = ?").bind(source).run(); } catch (_) {}
}

async function getQuotaMap(env) {
  const map = {};
  try {
    const rows = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT source, limit_val, remaining FROM quota").all();
    for (const r of (rows.results || [])) map[r.source] = { limit: Number(r.limit_val || 0), remaining: Number(r.remaining || 0) };
  } catch (_) {}
  return map;
}

async function chooseSourceOrder(requestedSource, availableSources, params, env = null, config = {}) {
  const source = String(requestedSource || "random").toLowerCase();
  if (source && source !== "random" && !SOURCES.includes(source)) return [];
  if (source === "bing" && params.hasExplicitQuery) return shuffle(availableSources.filter(s => s !== "bing"));
  if (source && source !== "random") return availableSources.includes(source) ? [source] : [];

  const routing = { ...DEFAULT_SOURCE_ROUTING, ...(config.sourceRouting || {}) };
  const priority = Array.isArray(routing.priority) && routing.priority.length ? routing.priority.filter(s => SOURCES.includes(s)) : DEFAULT_SOURCE_ROUTING.priority;
  const base = availableSources.filter(s => priority.includes(s));
  const candidates = base.filter(s => s !== "bing").length ? base.filter(s => s !== "bing") : base;
  const health = routing.healthAware && env ? await getLatestSourceHealthMap(env) : {};
  const cooldowns = env ? await getSourceCooldownMap(env) : {};
  const quotas = routing.quotaAware && env ? await getQuotaMap(env) : {};
  const scored = candidates.map(s => {
    const h = health[s];
    const priorityScore = priority.indexOf(s) >= 0 ? priority.indexOf(s) : 99;
    const unhealthyPenalty = h && h.ok === false && !h.stale ? 50 : 0;
    const stalePenalty = h?.stale ? 8 : 0;
    const latencyPenalty = h?.latency ? Math.min(10, Math.round(h.latency / 500)) : 0;
    const quota = quotas[s];
    const quotaPenalty = quota?.limit ? Math.max(0, 20 - Math.round((quota.remaining / quota.limit) * 20)) : 0;
    const cooled = !!cooldowns[s];
    return { source: s, score: priorityScore * 10 + unhealthyPenalty + stalePenalty + latencyPenalty + quotaPenalty + (cooled ? 80 : 0), unhealthy: h && h.ok === false && !h.stale, cooled };
  }).filter(item => !(routing.skipUnhealthy && (item.unhealthy || item.cooled)));
  if (!scored.length) return shuffle(candidates);
  scored.sort((a, b) => a.score - b.score || Math.random() - 0.5);
  return scored.map(x => x.source);
}

async function fetchSource(source, params, env, ctx) {
  if (source === "unsplash") return fetchUnsplashImages(params, env, ctx);
  if (source === "pexels") return fetchPexelsImages(params, env, ctx);
  if (source === "pixabay") return fetchPixabayImages(params, env, ctx);
  if (source === "bing") return fetchBingDailyImage(params, env, ctx);
  return { ok: false, status: 400, error: "Unknown source" };
}

async function maybeTrackDownload(image, env, ctx) {
  if (image.source === "unsplash" && image.download_url && env.UNSPLASH_ACCESS_KEY && Math.random() < 0.3) {
    ctx.waitUntil(fetch(`${image.download_url}?client_id=${encodeURIComponent(env.UNSPLASH_ACCESS_KEY)}`).catch(() => {}));
  }
}

async function fetchWithFallback(params, env, ctx, config) {
  const available = getAvailableSources(env, params);
  const order = await chooseSourceOrder(params.source, available, params, env, config);
  if (order.length === 0) {
    return { ok: false, status: 503, error: "No configured image source is available for this request", attempts: [] };
  }
  const attempts = [];
  for (const source of order) {
    const cacheKey = await buildCacheKey(source, params);
    if (!params.forceReload) {
      const cached = await getCachedPool(env.DB, cacheKey, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, env);
      const cachedImage = cached && selectRandomImage(cached.images, cacheKey);
      if (cachedImage) {
        const image = await decryptCachedImage(cachedImage, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD);
        // Skip corrupted cache entries and fall through to fresh fetch
        if (image._decryptFailed || !image.url) {
          ctx.waitUntil(appendLog(env.DB, "warn", "Cache decrypt failed, refetching", { cacheKey }));
        } else {
          await maybeTrackDownload(image, env, ctx);
          return { ok: true, source, image, cacheHit: true, cacheKey };
        }
      }
    }
    try {
      const result = await fetchSource(source, params, env, ctx);
      if (!result.ok) {
        attempts.push({ source, ok: false, error: result.error, status: result.status });
        ctx.waitUntil(recordSourceFailure(env, source, config));
        ctx.waitUntil(appendLog(env.DB, "warn", `Image source failed: ${source}`, { status: result.status, error: result.error }));
        continue;
      }
      ctx.waitUntil(recordSourceSuccess(env, source));
      const healthMap = await getLatestSourceHealthMap(env);
      const filteredImages = applyQualityScores(filterImagesByParams(result.images, params), params, healthMap);
      if (!filteredImages.length) {
        attempts.push({ source, ok: false, error: "No images match filters", status: 404 });
        continue;
      }
      await putCachedPool(env.DB, cacheKey, source, params, filteredImages, config.cacheTtl, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, env);
      const qualityPool = normalizeQuality(params.quality) === "high" ? filteredImages.slice(0, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filteredImages.length / 3))) : filteredImages;
      const image = selectRandomImage(qualityPool, cacheKey) || qualityPool[0] || filteredImages[0];
      await maybeTrackDownload(image, env, ctx);
      return { ok: true, source, image: imagePublicCopy(image), cacheHit: false, cacheKey };
    } catch (error) {
      attempts.push({ source, ok: false, error: error.message });
      ctx.waitUntil(recordSourceFailure(env, source, config));
      ctx.waitUntil(appendLog(env.DB, "error", `Image source exception: ${source}`, { error: error.message }));
    }
  }
  return { ok: false, status: 503, error: "All image sources failed", attempts };
}

async function createApiKey(env, name, limits = {}) {
  const plain = `ow_${randomString(34)}`;
  const hash = await sha256Hex(plain + ":" + (env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "fallback"));
  const prefix = plain.slice(0, 10);
  const finalLimits = { ...DEFAULT_API_KEY_LIMITS, ...limits };
  await env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO api_keys (name, key_hash, key_prefix, status, minute_limit, hour_limit, day_limit, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'active', ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'), datetime('now'))")
    .bind(String(name || "API Key").trim().slice(0, 80), hash, prefix, finalLimits.minute, finalLimits.hour, finalLimits.day).run();
  return plain;
}

async function listApiKeys(env) {
  try {
    const rows = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id, name, key_prefix, status, minute_limit, hour_limit, day_limit, last_used_at, created_at FROM api_keys ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 100").all();
    return rows.results || [];
  } catch (_) { return []; }
}

async function getDailyImage(env, ctx, config, options = {}) {
  const date = options.date || todayUtc8();
  const orientation = normalizeOrientation(options.orientation || "landscape");
  const key = `${date}:${orientation}`;
  try {
    const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT payload FROM daily_images WHERE date = ? AND orientation = ?").bind(date, orientation).first();
    if (row?.payload) return { ok: true, image: safeJsonParse(row.payload, null), cacheHit: true, date, orientation };
  } catch (_) {}
  const params = {
    query: normalizeQuery(options.query || config.home?.dailyQuery || "nature wallpaper"),
    orientation,
    source: "random",
    color: "",
    minWidth: orientation === "landscape" ? 1920 : 0,
    minHeight: orientation === "portrait" ? 1600 : 0,
    aspectRatio: orientation === "landscape" ? "wide" : orientation === "portrait" ? "tall" : "",
    safeMode: true,
    expand: true,
    forceReload: false,
    hasExplicitQuery: false,
    quality: "high",
  };
  const result = await fetchWithFallback(params, env, ctx, config);
  if (!result.ok) return result;
  const image = { ...imagePublicCopy(result.image), daily_date: date, daily_orientation: orientation, cache_hit: result.cacheHit, cache_key: result.cacheKey };
  try { await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO daily_images (date, orientation, payload, source, image_id, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(date, orientation, JSON.stringify(image), image.source || result.source || "random", imageIdentity(image)).run(); } catch (_) {}
  return { ok: true, image, cacheHit: false, date, orientation };
}

async function getQuotaRows(store, env) {
  try {
    const rows = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT source, used, limit_val, remaining, updated_at FROM quota ORDER BY source").all();
    if ((rows.results || []).length > 0) return (rows.results || []).map(r => ({ source: r.source, used: r.used, limit: r.limit_val, remaining: r.remaining, updated_at: r.updated_at }));
  } catch (_) {}
  return [];
}

// ── Source health monitoring (ping + 24h uptime, Nezha-style) ──
function parseTs(ts) {
  if (!ts) return 0;
  const s = String(ts);
  if (s.includes("T")) return Date.parse(s) || 0;
  return Date.parse(s.replace(" ", "T") + "Z") || 0;
}

function sourceConfigured(source, env) {
  if (source === "unsplash") return !!env.UNSPLASH_ACCESS_KEY;
  if (source === "pexels") return !!env.PEXELS_API_KEY;
  if (source === "pixabay") return !!env.PIXABAY_API_KEY;
  return true; // bing
}

async function pingSource(source, env) {
  if (!sourceConfigured(source, env)) return { ok: false, skip: true };
  let url, opts = {};
  if (source === "unsplash") url = `https://api.unsplash.com/photos/random?client_id=${encodeURIComponent(env.UNSPLASH_ACCESS_KEY)}&count=1`;
  else if (source === "pexels") { url = "https://api.pexels.com/v1/search?query=test&per_page=1"; opts = { headers: { Authorization: env.PEXELS_API_KEY } }; }
  else if (source === "pixabay") url = `https://pixabay.com/api/?key=${encodeURIComponent(env.PIXABAY_API_KEY)}&q=test&per_page=3`;
  else url = "https://www.bing.com/HPImageArchive.aspx?format=js&idx=0&n=1&mkt=en-US";
  const start = Date.now();
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), 8000);
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, { ...opts, signal: ctrl.signal });
    return { ok: res.status >= 200 && res.status < 400, latency: Date.now() - start, status: res.status };
  } catch (e) {
    return { ok: false, latency: Date.now() - start, status: 0, error: e.message };
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

async function recordHealth(env, source, r) {
  if (r.skip) return;
  try { await env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO source_health (source, ok, latency, status_code, checked_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(source, r.ok ? 1 : 0, Number(r.latency) || 0, Number(r.status) || 0).run(); } catch (_) {}
}

async function pingSources(env) {
  await Promise.all(SOURCES.map(async (s) => {
    const r = await pingSource(s, env);
    await recordHealth(env, s, r);
  }));
}

async function getHealth24h(env) {
  const bySource = {};
  for (const s of SOURCES) bySource[s] = [];
  try {
    const rows = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT source, ok, latency, status_code, checked_at FROM source_health WHERE checked_at >= datetime('now','-24 hours') ORDER BY checked_at ASC").all();
    for (const r of (rows.results || [])) {
      if (bySource[r.source]) bySource[r.source].push({ ts: r.checked_at, ok: !!r.ok, latency: r.latency, status: r.status_code });
    }
  } catch (_) {}
  const now = Date.now();
  return SOURCES.map(s => {
    const samples = bySource[s] || [];
    const up = samples.filter(x => x.ok).length;
    const latest = samples.length ? samples[samples.length - 1] : null;
    const stale = !latest || (now - parseTs(latest.ts)) > 300000;
    return {
      source: s,
      configured: sourceConfigured(s, env),
      latest,
      stale,
      uptimePct: samples.length ? Math.round((up / samples.length) * 100) : null,
      sampleCount: samples.length,
      samples,
    };
  });
}

// Reusable 24h heatmap widget (style + container + loader), rendered client-side
// from /api/source-health. Used on /docs and the admin Monitor page.
function monitorWidget(containerId, lang) {
  const en = lang === "en";
  const L = JSON.stringify({
    loading: en ? "Loading monitoring data..." : "正在加载监控数据...",
    online: en ? "Online" : "在线",
    offline: en ? "Offline" : "离线",
    nodata: en ? "No data" : "无数据",
    notconf: en ? "Not configured" : "未配置",
    latency: en ? "Latency" : "延迟",
    uptime: en ? "24h uptime" : "24小时在线率",
    last: en ? "Last check" : "最近检测",
    samples: en ? "samples" : "个采样",
    now: en ? "now" : "刚刚",
    ago: en ? "ago" : "前",
    na: "-",
  });
  return `<style>.hbars{display:flex;gap:1px;align-items:flex-end;height:30px;overflow-x:auto;padding-bottom:2px}.hb{flex:0 0 3px;min-width:3px;height:100%;border-radius:1px}.hbu{background:#22c55e}.hbd{background:#ef4444}.hbn{background:rgba(148,163,184,.18)}.mcard{background:rgba(0,0,0,.22);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;padding:16px 18px;margin-bottom:14px}.mhead{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:10px}.mhead strong{font-size:15px}.mbadge{font-size:11px;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:999px}.mbadge.up{background:rgba(34,197,94,.15);color:#86efac}.mbadge.down{background:rgba(239,68,68,.15);color:#fca5a5}.mbadge.off{background:rgba(148,163,184,.15);color:var(--muted)}.mstat{margin-left:auto;font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);display:flex;gap:14px;flex-wrap:wrap}.mstat b{color:var(--text)}</style>
<div id="${containerId}"><p class="muted" style="font-size:13px">${en ? "Loading monitoring data..." : "正在加载监控数据..."}</p></div>
<script>(function(){var L=${L};function fmtAgo(ts){var t=parseTs(ts);if(!t)return L.na;var s=Math.round((Date.now()-t)/1000);if(s<60)return L.now;if(s<3600)return Math.floor(s/60)+'m '+L.ago;if(s<86400)return Math.floor(s/3600)+'h '+L.ago;return Math.floor(s/86400)+'d '+L.ago}function parseTs(ts){if(!ts)return 0;var s=String(ts);if(s.indexOf('T')>=0)return Date.parse(s)||0;return Date.parse(s.replace(' ','T')+'Z')||0}function render(data){var h='';data.forEach(function(s){var bars=(s.samples||[]).map(function(x){return '<span class="hb '+(x.ok?'hbu':'hbd')+'" title="'+(x.ok?L.online:L.offline)+'"></span>'}).join('')||('<span class="muted" style="font-size:12px">'+L.nodata+'</span>');var cls=s.configured?(s.latest?(s.latest.ok?'up':'down'):'off'):'off';var label=s.configured?(s.latest?(s.latest.ok?L.online:L.offline):L.nodata):L.notconf;var pct=s.uptimePct===null?L.na:s.uptimePct+'%';var lat=s.latest?(s.latest.latency||0)+'ms':L.na;var last=s.latest?fmtAgo(s.latest.ts):L.na;h+='<div class="mcard"><div class="mhead"><strong>'+s.source+'</strong><span class="mbadge '+cls+'">'+label+'</span><span class="mstat"><span>'+L.latency+': <b>'+lat+'</b></span><span>'+L.uptime+': <b>'+pct+'</b></span><span>'+L.last+': <b>'+last+'</b></span><span>'+(s.sampleCount||0)+' '+L.samples+'</span></span></div><div class="hbars">'+bars+'</div></div>'});document.getElementById('${containerId}').innerHTML=h}fetch('/api/source-health?_t='+Date.now()).then(function(r){return r.json()}).then(render).catch(function(){document.getElementById('${containerId}').innerHTML='<p class="muted">error</p>'})})();</script>`;
}

async function getEncryptedJson(store, key, password, defaultValue = {}, env) {
  const channel = key.replace("notify:", "");
  try {
    const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT config_json FROM notify_configs WHERE channel = ?").bind(channel).first();
    if (row) {
      const plain = await decryptText(row.config_json, password);
      try { return JSON.parse(plain === "FAIL" ? row.config_json : plain); } catch (_) { return defaultValue; }
    }
  } catch (_) {}
  return defaultValue;
}

async function setEncryptedJson(store, key, value, password, env) {
  const channel = key.replace("notify:", "");
  try {
    await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO notify_configs (channel, config_json, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(channel, await encryptText(JSON.stringify(value), password)).run();
  } catch (_) {}
}

async function readNotifyJson(response) {
  try {
    const text = await response.text();
    if (!text) return null;
    return JSON.parse(text);
  } catch (_) {
    return null;
  }
}

function notifyPlatformError(channel, data) {
  if (!data || typeof data !== "object") return "";
  if (channel === "telegram" && data.ok === false) return data.description || "Telegram API rejected the request";
  if (channel === "dingtalk" && data.errcode !== undefined && Number(data.errcode) !== 0) return data.errmsg || `DingTalk errcode ${data.errcode}`;
  if (channel === "feishu") {
    const code = data.code ?? data.StatusCode ?? data.status_code;
    if (code !== undefined && Number(code) !== 0) return data.msg || data.message || data.StatusMessage || `Feishu code ${code}`;
  }
  return "";
}

async function assertNotifyOk(response, channel) {
  const data = await readNotifyJson(response);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`${channel} notification failed: HTTP ${response.status}`);
  const platformError = notifyPlatformError(channel, data);
  if (platformError) throw new Error(`${channel} notification failed: ${platformError}`);
}

function signedWebhookUrl(webhookUrl, params) {
  try {
    const url = new URL(webhookUrl);
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(params)) url.searchParams.set(key, String(value));
    return url.toString();
  } catch (_) {
    const query = Object.entries(params).map(([key, value]) => `${encodeURIComponent(key)}=${encodeURIComponent(value)}`).join("&");
    return `${webhookUrl}${String(webhookUrl).includes("?") ? "&" : "?"}${query}`;
  }
}

async function createDingTalkWebhookUrl(webhookUrl, secret) {
  if (!secret) return webhookUrl;
  const timestamp = Date.now();
  const sign = bytesToBase64(await hmacSha256(secret, `${timestamp}\n${secret}`));
  return signedWebhookUrl(webhookUrl, { timestamp, sign });
}

async function createFeishuPayload(config, title, message) {
  const payload = { msg_type: "text", content: { text: `${title}\n${message}` } };
  if (config.secret) {
    const timestamp = String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000));
    payload.timestamp = timestamp;
    payload.sign = bytesToBase64(await hmacSha256(`${timestamp}\n${config.secret}`, ""));
  }
  return payload;
}

async function sendTelegram(config, title, message) {
  if (!config.botToken || !config.chatId) return;
  const response = await fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${config.botToken}/sendMessage`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ chat_id: config.chatId, text: `${title}\n${message}` }),
  });
  await assertNotifyOk(response, "telegram");
}

async function sendFeishu(config, title, message) {
  if (!config.webhookUrl) return;
  const response = await fetch(config.webhookUrl, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify(await createFeishuPayload(config, title, message)),
  });
  await assertNotifyOk(response, "feishu");
}

async function sendDingTalk(config, title, message) {
  if (!config.webhookUrl) return;
  const response = await fetch(await createDingTalkWebhookUrl(config.webhookUrl, config.secret), {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ msgtype: "text", text: { content: `${title}\n${message}` } }),
  });
  await assertNotifyOk(response, "dingtalk");
}

async function sendEmail(config, title, message) {
  if (!config.enabled || !config.provider) return;
  if (config.provider === "resend" && config.apiKey && config.from && config.to) {
    const response = await fetch("https://api.resend.com/emails", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json", Authorization: `Bearer ${config.apiKey}` },
      body: JSON.stringify({ from: config.from, to: [config.to], subject: title, text: message }),
    });
    await assertNotifyOk(response, "email:resend");
  } else if (config.provider === "sendgrid" && config.apiKey && config.from && config.to) {
    const response = await fetch("https://api.sendgrid.com/v3/mail/send", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json", Authorization: `Bearer ${config.apiKey}` },
      body: JSON.stringify({ personalizations: [{ to: [{ email: config.to }] }], from: { email: config.from }, subject: title, content: [{ type: "text/plain", value: message }] }),
    });
    await assertNotifyOk(response, "email:sendgrid");
  } else if (config.provider === "webhook" && config.webhookUrl) {
    const response = await fetch(config.webhookUrl, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ title, message, to: config.to, from: config.from }),
    });
    await assertNotifyOk(response, "email:webhook");
  }
}

const TOTP_ALPHABET = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";

function base32Encode(bytes) {
  let bits = "";
  for (const byte of bytes) bits += byte.toString(2).padStart(8, "0");
  let out = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < bits.length; i += 5) {
    const chunk = bits.slice(i, i + 5).padEnd(5, "0");
    out += TOTP_ALPHABET[parseInt(chunk, 2)];
  }
  return out;
}

function base32Decode(secret) {
  const clean = String(secret || "").replace(/=+$/g, "").replace(/\s+/g, "").toUpperCase();
  let bits = "";
  for (const char of clean) {
    const idx = TOTP_ALPHABET.indexOf(char);
    if (idx < 0) continue;
    bits += idx.toString(2).padStart(5, "0");
  }
  const bytes = [];
  for (let i = 0; i + 8 <= bits.length; i += 8) bytes.push(parseInt(bits.slice(i, i + 8), 2));
  return new Uint8Array(bytes);
}

function generateTotpSecret() {
  return base32Encode(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(20)));
}

async function hotp(secret, counter) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", base32Decode(secret), { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-1" }, false, ["sign"]);
  const buffer = new ArrayBuffer(8);
  const view = new DataView(buffer);
  view.setUint32(4, counter, false);
  const digest = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, buffer));
  const offset = digest[digest.length - 1] & 0x0f;
  const code = ((digest[offset] & 0x7f) << 24) | (digest[offset + 1] << 16) | (digest[offset + 2] << 8) | digest[offset + 3];
  return String(code % 1000000).padStart(6, "0");
}

async function verifyTotp(code, secret) {
  const clean = String(code || "").replace(/\s+/g, "");
  if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(clean)) return false;
  const step = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000 / 30);
  for (const drift of [-1, 0, 1]) {
    if (await hotp(secret, step + drift) === clean) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

async function getTotpState(store, password, env) {
  try {
    const e = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM admin_config WHERE key = ?").bind(KV.CONFIG_TOTP_ENABLED).first();
    const s = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM admin_config WHERE key = ?").bind(KV.CONFIG_TOTP_SECRET).first();
    const enabled = e?.value === "true";
    if (!s?.value) return { enabled, secret: "" };
    const secret = await decryptText(s.value, password);
    return { enabled, secret: secret === "FAIL" ? "" : secret };
  } catch (_) {}
  return { enabled: false, secret: "" };
}

async function setTotpState(store, password, enabled, secret, env) {
  try {
    if (secret) await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO admin_config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(KV.CONFIG_TOTP_SECRET, await encryptText(secret, password)).run();
    await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO admin_config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(KV.CONFIG_TOTP_ENABLED, enabled ? "true" : "false").run();
  } catch (_) {}
}

// Feedback system: calculate severity based on type and keywords.
function calculateFeedbackSeverity(type, title, content) {
  let score = 0;
  const text = (title + " " + content).toLowerCase();
  // Type-based base score
  if (type === "bug") score += 3;
  else if (type === "security") score += 5;
  else if (type === "feature") score += 1;
  else if (type === "other") score += 0;
  // Keyword boosters
  const critical = ["crash", "崩溃", "down", "宕机", "无法访问", "not working", "broken", "error 500", "security", "安全", "漏洞", "泄露", "leak"];
  const high = ["slow", "慢", "timeout", "超时", "error", "错误", "fail", "失败", "cannot", "无法"];
  if (critical.some(k => text.includes(k))) score += 3;
  else if (high.some(k => text.includes(k))) score += 1;
  return Math.min(5, score);
}

async function submitFeedback(db, password, type, title, content, contact, ipHash, userAgent, env) {
  const severity = calculateFeedbackSeverity(type, title, content);
  const encTitle = await encryptText(title, password);
  const encContent = await encryptText(content, password);
  const encContact = contact ? await encryptText(contact, password) : "";
  try {
    await db.prepare("INSERT INTO feedback (type, title, content, contact, ip_hash, user_agent, severity, status, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'pending', datetime('now'))").bind(type, encTitle, encContent, encContact, ipHash, userAgent, severity).run();
    return { ok: true };
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: e.message }; }
}

async function getFeedbackList(db, password, limit = 50, status = null, env) {
  try {
    const query = status ? "SELECT * FROM feedback WHERE status = ? ORDER BY severity DESC, created_at DESC LIMIT ?" : "SELECT * FROM feedback ORDER BY severity DESC, created_at DESC LIMIT ?";
    const stmt = status ? db.prepare(query).bind(status, limit) : db.prepare(query).bind(limit);
    const rows = await stmt.all();
    const result = [];
    for (const r of (rows.results || [])) {
      const title = await decryptText(r.title, password);
      const content = await decryptText(r.content, password);
      const contact = r.contact ? await decryptText(r.contact, password) : "";
      if (title !== "FAIL" && content !== "FAIL") {
        result.push({ ...r, title, content, contact });
      }
    }
    return result;
  } catch (_) { return []; }
}

// Fetch a single feedback by id and decrypt in-place. Avoids scanning the full
// table (which getFeedbackList would do) just to render one detail page.
async function getFeedbackById(db, password, id) {
  try {
    const r = await db.prepare("SELECT * FROM feedback WHERE id = ?").bind(id).first();
    if (!r) return null;
    const title = await decryptText(r.title, password);
    const content = await decryptText(r.content, password);
    const contact = r.contact ? await decryptText(r.contact, password) : "";
    if (title === "FAIL" || content === "FAIL") return null;
    return { ...r, title, content, contact };
  } catch (_) { return null; }
}

async function updateFeedbackStatus(db, id, status, adminNote, env) {
  try {
    await db.prepare("UPDATE feedback SET status = ?, admin_note = ?, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?").bind(status, adminNote || "", id).run();
    return { ok: true };
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function notificationDedupeKey(eventType, payload = {}) {
  const ip = payload.ip || payload.ip_hash || "";
  const reason = payload.reason || payload.source || payload.user || "";
  return `notify_dedupe:${eventType}:${ip}:${reason}`.slice(0, 220);
}

async function shouldSendNotification(eventType, payload, env) {
  const cooldowns = {
    ip_banned: 1800,
    quota_warning: 3600,
    login_failed: 300,
    feedback_received: 0,
    test: 0,
  };
  const cooldown = cooldowns[eventType] ?? 600;
  if (!cooldown) return { ok: true };
  const key = notificationDedupeKey(eventType, payload || {});
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  try {
    const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM config WHERE key = ?").bind(key).first();
    const last = parseInt(row?.value || "0", 10) || 0;
    if (last && now - last < cooldown) return { ok: false, key, remaining: cooldown - (now - last) };
    await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(key, String(now)).run();
  } catch (_) {}
  return { ok: true, key };
}

function sleep(ms) {
  return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

function isRetryableNotifyError(error) {
  const message = String(error?.message || error || "");
  return /HTTP\s+(429|5\d\d)\b|fetch failed|network|timeout|temporarily unavailable/i.test(message);
}

async function sendWithRetry(channel, fn, attempts = 3) {
  let lastError = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < attempts; i++) {
    try {
      return await fn();
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = error;
      if (i < attempts - 1 && isRetryableNotifyError(error)) {
        await sleep(200 * (2 ** i));
        continue;
      }
      throw error;
    }
  }
  throw lastError || new Error(`${channel} notification failed`);
}

async function sendNotification(eventType, payload, env, options = {}) {
  const titles = {
    daily_url_changed: "🔑 管理地址已更新",
    login_success: "✅ 管理员登录成功",
    login_failed: "⚠️ 登录失败警告",
    ip_banned: "🚫 IP已封禁",
    quota_warning: "⚠️ API配额告警",
    totp_enabled: "🔐 TOTP已启用",
    feedback_received: "💬 收到新反馈",
    test: "🧪 通知测试",
  };
  const dedupe = await shouldSendNotification(eventType, payload, env);
  if (!dedupe.ok) {
    await appendNotificationHistory(env.DB, eventType, { ...(payload || {}), deduped: true, cooldownRemaining: dedupe.remaining }, "deduped", env);
    return;
  }
  const title = titles[eventType] || "图床通知";
  const message = Object.entries(payload || {}).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`).join("\n");
  const targetChannels = Array.isArray(options.channels) && options.channels.length ? new Set(options.channels) : null;
  const bypassSubscription = eventType === "test" || options.force === true;
  const configs = await Promise.all(["telegram", "feishu", "dingtalk", "email"].map(async channel => ({
    channel,
    config: await getEncryptedJson(env.DB, `notify:${channel}`, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, {}, env),
  })));
  const tasks = [];
  for (const { channel, config } of configs) {
    if (targetChannels && !targetChannels.has(channel)) continue;
    if (!config.enabled) continue;
    if (!bypassSubscription && Array.isArray(config.events) && !config.events.includes(eventType)) continue;
    if (channel === "telegram") tasks.push(sendWithRetry(channel, () => sendTelegram(config, title, message)));
    if (channel === "feishu") tasks.push(sendWithRetry(channel, () => sendFeishu(config, title, message)));
    if (channel === "dingtalk") tasks.push(sendWithRetry(channel, () => sendDingTalk(config, title, message)));
    if (channel === "email") tasks.push(sendWithRetry(channel, () => sendEmail(config, title, message)));
  }
  const results = await Promise.allSettled(tasks);
  const failed = results.filter(result => result.status === "rejected");
  if (failed.length) {
    for (const result of failed) await appendLog(env.DB, "error", "Notification failed", { error: result.reason?.message || String(result.reason) }, env);
    await appendNotificationHistory(env.DB, eventType, payload, "partial_failed", env);
  } else {
    await appendNotificationHistory(env.DB, eventType, payload, tasks.length ? "sent" : "skipped", env);
  }
}

async function getAdminUsername(store, env) {
  try {
    const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM admin_config WHERE key = ?").bind(KV.CONFIG_ADMIN_USERNAME).first();
    if (row) return row.value;
  } catch (_) {}
  return env.ADMIN_USERNAME || "admin";
}

function getPublicOrigin(request, env) {
  if (request?.url) return new URL(request.url).origin;
  return safeUrl(env.PUBLIC_BASE_URL || env.SITE_URL || env.APP_URL || "", "").replace(/\/+$/g, "");
}

function tomorrowCstDateText() {
  return new Date(Date.now() + 8 * 60 * 60 * 1000 + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

function buildAdminUrl(origin, path) {
  return origin ? `${origin}/${path}/dashboard` : `/${path}/dashboard`;
}

function buildAdminPathPayload(path, date, request, env) {
  const origin = getPublicOrigin(request, env);
  const payload = { path, date, admin_url: buildAdminUrl(origin, path), expires_at: `${tomorrowCstDateText()} 00:00 CST` };
  if (!origin) payload.note = "Set PUBLIC_BASE_URL to include an absolute admin_url in scheduled notifications.";
  return payload;
}

async function getDailyAdminPath(store, env, ctx, request = null) {
  const username = await getAdminUsername(store, env);
  const today = todayUtc8();
  let firstDone, savedDate, savedPath;
  try {
    const fd = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM admin_config WHERE key = ?").bind(KV.CONFIG_FIRST_LOGIN_DONE).first();
    firstDone = fd ? fd.value : null;
    const sd = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM admin_config WHERE key = ?").bind(KV.CONFIG_DAILY_PATH_DATE).first();
    savedDate = sd ? sd.value : null;
    const sp = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM admin_config WHERE key = ?").bind(KV.CONFIG_DAILY_PATH).first();
    savedPath = sp ? sp.value : null;
  } catch (_) {}
  if (!firstDone && savedPath && savedPath !== username) {
    await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO admin_config (key, value) VALUES (?, 'true')").bind(KV.CONFIG_FIRST_LOGIN_DONE).run();
    if (savedDate === today) return savedPath;
  }
  if (!firstDone) return username;
  if (savedPath && savedDate === today) return savedPath;
  const next = randomString(12);
  await Promise.all([
    env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO admin_config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(KV.CONFIG_DAILY_PATH, next).run(),
    env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO admin_config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(KV.CONFIG_DAILY_PATH_DATE, today).run(),
  ]);
  if (ctx) ctx.waitUntil(sendNotification("daily_url_changed", buildAdminPathPayload(next, today, request, env), env));
  return next;
}

async function rotateAdminPathAfterFirstLogin(store, env, ctx, request = null) {
  const today = todayUtc8();
  const next = randomString(12);
  await Promise.all([
    env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO admin_config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(KV.CONFIG_FIRST_LOGIN_DONE, "true").run(),
    env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO admin_config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(KV.CONFIG_DAILY_PATH, next).run(),
    env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO admin_config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(KV.CONFIG_DAILY_PATH_DATE, today).run(),
  ]);
  if (ctx) ctx.waitUntil(sendNotification("daily_url_changed", buildAdminPathPayload(next, today, request, env), env));
  return next;
}

async function signSession(payload, secret) {
  const body = base64UrlEncode(JSON.stringify(payload));
  const sig = base64UrlEncode(await hmacSha256(secret, body));
  return `${body}.${sig}`;
}

async function verifySession(token, secret) {
  if (!token || !token.includes(".")) return null;
  const [body, sig] = token.split(".");
  const expected = base64UrlEncode(await hmacSha256(secret, body));
  if (sig !== expected) return null;
  try {
    const payload = JSON.parse(base64UrlDecodeToString(body));
    if (!payload.exp || payload.exp < Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)) return null;
    return payload;
  } catch (_) {
    return null;
  }
}

function bgAccessEnabled(config) {
  return config?.bgAccess?.enabled !== false;
}

function turnstileEnabled(config, env, area) {
  if (!env.TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY || !env.TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY) return false;
  const cfg = { ...DEFAULT_TURNSTILE_CONFIG, ...(config.turnstile || {}) };
  if (!cfg.enabled) return false;
  if (area === "feedback") return cfg.protectFeedback !== false;
  if (area === "adminLogin") return cfg.protectAdminLogin !== false;
  if (area === "checkContent") return cfg.protectContentCheck === true;
  return true;
}

function renderTurnstileWidget(config, env, area = "feedback") {
  if (!turnstileEnabled(config, env, area)) return "";
  return `<div class="cf-turnstile" data-sitekey="${escapeAttr(env.TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY)}" style="margin:18px 0"></div>`;
}

function turnstileScript(config, env) {
  if (!env.TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY || !env.TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY || config?.turnstile?.enabled === false) return "";
  return `<script src="https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/api.js" async defer></script>`;
}

async function verifyTurnstileToken(request, env, token) {
  if (!token) return { ok: false, error: "missing_token" };
  const body = new URLSearchParams();
  body.set("secret", env.TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY || "");
  body.set("response", token);
  body.set("remoteip", getClientIp(request));
  try {
    const res = await fetch("https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/siteverify", { method: "POST", body });
    const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (data.success) return { ok: true };
    return { ok: false, error: "turnstile_failed", codes: data["error-codes"] || [] };
  } catch (error) {
    return { ok: false, error: "turnstile_unavailable", message: error.message };
  }
}

function randomInt(min, max) {
  const lo = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(min) || 0));
  const hi = Math.max(lo, Math.floor(Number(max) || lo));
  const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(1));
  return lo + (bytes[0] % (hi - lo + 1));
}

function bgAccessCookieHeader(sessionId, ttlSeconds) {
  return `${BG_SESSION_COOKIE}=${encodeURIComponent(sessionId)}; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Strict; Path=/api/bg; Max-Age=${Math.max(60, Number(ttlSeconds) || 3600)}`;
}

async function makeBgAccessToken(env, config, sessionId) {
  const cfg = { ...DEFAULT_BG_ACCESS, ...(config.bgAccess || {}) };
  const exp = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + Math.max(60, Number(cfg.ttlSeconds) || DEFAULT_BG_ACCESS.ttlSeconds);
  const nonce = randomString(24);
  const payload = `v1:${exp}:${nonce}:${sessionId}`;
  const sig = base64UrlEncode(await hmacSha256(env.BG_TOKEN_SECRET || env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, payload));
  return `v1.${exp}.${nonce}.${sig}`;
}

async function verifyBgAccessToken(request, env, config) {
  if (!bgAccessEnabled(config)) return { ok: true };
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  const token = url.searchParams.get("bg_token") || "";
  const sessionId = parseCookies(request)[BG_SESSION_COOKIE] || "";
  if (!token) return { ok: false, reason: "bg_token_missing", ban: true };
  const parts = token.split(".");
  if (parts.length !== 4 || parts[0] !== "v1") return { ok: false, reason: "bg_token_invalid", ban: true };
  const [, expRaw, nonce, sig] = parts;
  if (!sessionId) return { ok: false, reason: "bg_cookie_missing", ban: false };
  const exp = parseInt(expRaw, 10);
  if (!exp || !nonce || !sig) return { ok: false, reason: "bg_token_invalid", ban: true };
  const payload = `v1:${exp}:${nonce}:${sessionId}`;
  const expected = base64UrlEncode(await hmacSha256(env.BG_TOKEN_SECRET || env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, payload));
  if (sig !== expected) return { ok: false, reason: "bg_signature_invalid", ban: true };
  if (exp < Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)) return { ok: false, reason: "bg_token_expired", ban: false };
  return { ok: true };
}

function isCrossSiteImageRequest(request) {
  const site = request.headers.get("Sec-Fetch-Site") || "";
  const dest = request.headers.get("Sec-Fetch-Dest") || "";
  return site === "cross-site" && dest === "image";
}

async function handleBgAccessViolation(request, env, ctx, config, reason, wantInfo, canBan = true) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const cfg = { ...DEFAULT_BG_ACCESS, ...(config.bgAccess || {}) };
  const ipHash = await hashIdentifier(getClientIp(request), env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "fallback");
  const whitelisted = await checkWhitelist(env.DB, ipHash, env);
  const shouldBan = canBan && !whitelisted && reason !== "bg_token_expired" && reason !== "bg_cookie_missing" && (cfg.strictHotlinkBan || !isCrossSiteImageRequest(request));
  let banSeconds = 0;
  let expiresAt = "";
  if (shouldBan) {
    banSeconds = randomInt(cfg.banMinSeconds, cfg.banMaxSeconds);
    expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + banSeconds * 1000).toISOString();
    try { await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO bans (ip_hash, reason, level, created_at, expires_at) VALUES (?, ?, 1, ?, ?)").bind(ipHash, reason, nowIso(), expiresAt).run(); } catch (_) {}
    ctx.waitUntil(sendNotification("ip_banned", { ip: ipHash.slice(0, 16), reason, seconds: banSeconds }, env));
  }
  ctx.waitUntil(appendLog(env.DB, "warn", "Blocked /api/bg access", { reason, ip: ipHash.slice(0, 16), banned: shouldBan, seconds: banSeconds }, env));
  const message = reason === "bg_token_expired" ? "页面背景访问凭证已过期，请刷新首页后重试。" : `这个背景接口只供 ${site} 首页内部使用，请使用 /api/random 获取公开随机图片。`;
  if (wantInfo || wantsJson(request)) return publicApiErrorResponse({ error: reason, message, retryAfter: banSeconds, expiresAt }, 403, { "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*" });
  return renderPublicErrorPage({ request, config, status: 403, code: reason, title: "背景接口受保护", message, detail: shouldBan ? `当前访问已被临时限制，预计解除时间：${expiresAt}` : "", retryAfter: banSeconds, actionText: "使用公开图片 API", actionHref: "/docs", site });
}

async function makeCsrfToken(session, secret) {
  return base64UrlEncode(await hmacSha256(secret, `${session.nonce}:${session.sub}:${session.exp}`));
}

async function verifyCsrfToken(session, token, secret) {
  if (!session || !token) return false;
  return token === await makeCsrfToken(session, secret);
}

function setSessionCookieHeader(token) {
  return `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${encodeURIComponent(token)}; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Strict; Path=/; Max-Age=${SESSION_TTL_SECONDS}`;
}

function clearSessionCookieHeader() {
  return `${SESSION_COOKIE}=; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Strict; Path=/; Max-Age=0`;
}

async function requireAdmin(request, env, ctx, token) {
  const daily = await getDailyAdminPath(env.DB, env, ctx, request);
  if (token !== daily) return { ok: false, status: 403, daily };
  const session = await verifySession(parseCookies(request)[SESSION_COOKIE], env.ADMIN_PASSWORD);
  if (!session) return { ok: false, status: 401, daily };
  return { ok: true, session, daily };
}

function renderMarkdownLite(markdown) {
  let html = escapeHtml(markdown || "");
  html = html.replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, "<strong>$1</strong>");
  html = html.replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\((https?:\/\/[^\s)]+)\)/g, (_, text, href) => `<a href="${escapeAttr(safeUrl(href, "#"))}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${text}</a>`);
  const lines = html.split("\n");
  if (lines.some(line => /^\s*-\s+/.test(line))) {
    html = lines.map(line => /^\s*-\s+/.test(line) ? `<li>${line.replace(/^\s*-\s+/, "")}</li>` : `<p>${line}</p>`).join("");
    html = html.replace(/((?:<li>.*?<\/li>)+)/g, "<ul>$1</ul>");
  } else {
    html = html.replace(/\n/g, "<br>");
  }
  return html;
}

function renderBanner(config) {
  const msgs = Array.isArray(config.bannerContent) ? config.bannerContent : (config.bannerContent ? [config.bannerContent] : []);
  if (!msgs.length) return "";
  const type = ["fixed", "dismissible", "toast"].includes(config.bannerType) ? config.bannerType : "fixed";
  const icon = `<div class="notice-icon" aria-hidden="true">📣</div>`;
  const items = msgs.map((m, i) => `<span class="marquee-item" data-index="${i}">${renderMarkdownLite(m)}</span>`).join(`<span class="marquee-sep" aria-hidden="true">•</span>`);
  const close = type === "dismissible" ? '<button class="banner-close" onclick="dismissBanner()" aria-label="关闭通知">×</button>' : "";
  return `<div id="noticeBanner" class="notice-card notice-${type} notice-marquee" role="status" aria-live="polite">${icon}<div class="notice-viewport"><div class="notice-track"><div class="notice-run">${items}</div><div class="notice-run" aria-hidden="true">${items}</div></div></div>${close}</div>`;
}
