<script>
function toast(msg,type){var el=document.createElement('div');el.className='notice-banner notice-toast'+(type?' notice-'+type:'');el.textContent=msg;document.body.appendChild(el);setTimeout(function(){el.remove()},3500)}
function applyTheme(mode){var root=document.documentElement;var prefer=window.matchMedia&&window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: light)').matches;var light=mode==='light'||(!mode||mode==='auto')&&prefer;root.classList.toggle('light',!!light);root.dataset.theme=mode||'auto';var labels=document.querySelectorAll('.theme-label');labels.forEach(function(el){el.textContent=(mode==='light'?(el.getAttribute('data-light')||'Light'):mode==='dark'?(el.getAttribute('data-dark')||'Dark'):(el.getAttribute('data-auto')||'Auto'))});var icons=document.querySelectorAll('.theme-icon');icons.forEach(function(el){el.textContent=(mode==='light'?'☀️':mode==='dark'?'🌙':'🌓')})}
function toggleTheme(){var cur=localStorage.getItem('omniwall_theme')||'auto';var next=cur==='auto'?'light':cur==='light'?'dark':'auto';localStorage.setItem('omniwall_theme',next);applyTheme(next)}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){var qp=new URLSearchParams(location.search);var lang=qp.get('lang');if(lang==='en'||lang==='zh')document.cookie='omniwall_lang='+lang+';path=/;max-age=31536000;samesite=lax';applyTheme(localStorage.getItem('omniwall_theme')||'auto');if(window.matchMedia){window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: light)').addEventListener('change',function(){if((localStorage.getItem('omniwall_theme')||'auto')==='auto')applyTheme('auto')})}});
function dismissBanner(){document.cookie='banner_dismissed=1;path=/;max-age=86400';var el=document.getElementById('noticeBanner');if(el)el.remove()}
function startBannerCarousel(){var b=document.getElementById('noticeBanner');if(!b||b.dataset.started==='1')return;b.dataset.started='1';var s=b.querySelectorAll('.banner-slide');var d=b.querySelectorAll('.banner-dot');if(s.length>1){var i=0;setInterval(function(){s[i].style.display='none';d[i]&&(d[i].className=d[i].className.replace(' active',''));i=(i+1)%s.length;s[i].style.display='block';d[i]&&(d[i].className+=' active')},5000)}var t=b.querySelectorAll('.notice-toast-item');if(t.length>0){var ti=0;function showToast(){t.forEach(function(el){el.classList.remove('show')});t[ti].classList.add('show');ti=(ti+1)%t.length;setTimeout(showToast,3500)}showToast()}}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){if(document.cookie.indexOf('banner_dismissed=1')!==-1){var b=document.querySelector('.notice-dismissible');if(b)b.remove()}else{startBannerCarousel()}var menu=document.createElement('div');menu.className='ctx-menu';menu.innerHTML='<button data-a="refresh">🔄 刷新页面</button><button data-a="copy">🔗 复制链接</button>';document.body.appendChild(menu);document.addEventListener('contextmenu',function(e){if(e.target.closest('input,textarea,select,[contenteditable],pre,code'))return;var sel=String(getSelection());if(sel)return;e.preventDefault();menu.style.display='block';menu.style.left=e.clientX+'px';menu.style.top=e.clientY+'px'});document.addEventListener('click',function(e){var a=e.target.getAttribute('data-a');if(a==='refresh')location.reload();if(a==='copy')navigator.clipboard.writeText(location.href).then(function(){toast('链接已复制')});menu.style.display='none'})});
</script>`;
}

async function renderHome(request, url, env, config) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const homeCfg = normalizeHomeConfig(config.home || DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG);
  const bgSessionId = randomString(24);
  const bgToken = await makeBgAccessToken(env, config, bgSessionId);
  const bgQuery = encodeURIComponent(homeCfg.backgroundQuery || "wallpaper");
  const bgImagePath = `/api/bg?orientation=landscape&query=${bgQuery}&bg_token=${encodeURIComponent(bgToken)}`;
  const bgInfoPath = `/api/bg?info=true&orientation=landscape&query=${bgQuery}&bg_token=${encodeURIComponent(bgToken)}`;
  const bgImageJs = JSON.stringify(bgImagePath);
  const bgInfoJs = JSON.stringify(bgInfoPath);
  const bg = config.customBg ? safeUrl(config.customBg, "") : `${url.origin}${bgImagePath}`;
  const favicon = escapeAttr(siteFaviconHref(env));
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const en = lang === "en";
  const homeTitles = en ? homeCfg.titlesEn : homeCfg.titlesZh;
  const homeText = {
    hero: en ? homeCfg.heroEn : homeCfg.heroZh,
    random: en ? homeCfg.randomEn : homeCfg.randomZh,
    builder: en ? homeCfg.builderEn : homeCfg.builderZh,
    docs: en ? homeCfg.docsEn : homeCfg.docsZh,
    cards: en ? [["✨", "Random inspiration", "Every refresh brings a fresh visual experience for your designs, blogs and apps."], ["⚡", "Fast API", "Clean API links with keyword, orientation and source switching."], ["🌐", "Chinese keywords", "Chinese search terms are translated automatically before image search."]] : [["✨", "随机灵感", "每次刷新都是全新视觉体验，为你的设计、博客或项目提供源源不断的高质量图片素材。"], ["⚡", "极速调用", "简洁的 API 接口，支持关键词、方向、多源切换。几行代码即可接入，无需复杂配置。"], ["🌐", "中文支持", "支持中文关键词自动翻译，无需手动转换。输入“猫”即可获得 cat 相关的高质量图片。"]],
    latest: en ? "📢 Latest Updates" : "📢 最新动态",
    viewAll: en ? "View all →" : "查看全部 →",
    source: en ? "📷 Image Source" : "📷 图片来源",
    loading: en ? "Loading..." : "加载中...",
    thirdParty: en ? "Third-party platform" : "第三方平台",
    sources: en ? "Sources" : "图源接入",
    cache: en ? "Cache hit latency" : "缓存命中延迟",
    creativity: en ? "Creative possibilities" : "创意可能",
  };
  let changelog = [];
  try { const r = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT date, version, title, content FROM changelog ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 10").all(); changelog = (r.results || []).map(x => x); } catch (_) {}
  const changelogHtml = changelog.slice(0, 3).map(item => `<div class="changelog-item"><span class="changelog-date">${escapeHtml(item.date)}</span><strong>${escapeHtml(item.version ? item.version + ' ' : '')}${escapeHtml(item.title)}</strong><p>${escapeHtml(item.content).slice(0, 120)}</p></div>`).join("");
  const particles = Array.from({length: 16}, (_, i) => {
    const left = ((i * 37 + 13) % 100);
    const delay = (i * 0.9).toFixed(1);
    const dur = (12 + (i * 17) % 8).toFixed(1);
    return `<div class="particle" style="left:${left}%;animation-delay:${delay}s;animation-duration:${dur}s"></div>`;
  }).join("");
  const titleText = escapeHtml(homeTitles[0] || site);
  const titleListJs = JSON.stringify(homeTitles);
  const homeCards = homeText.cards.map(([icon, title, desc]) => `<div class="card"><div class="feature-icon">${icon}</div><h3>${escapeHtml(title)}</h3><p>${escapeHtml(desc)}</p></div>`).join("");
  const thirdPartyJs = JSON.stringify(homeText.thirdParty);
  return htmlResponse(`<!doctype html><html lang="zh-CN"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><link rel="icon" href="${favicon}"><title>${escapeHtml(site)}</title>${commonPublicAssets()}<style>body.home-page{background-image:none!important}.home-bg{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:-3;background-image:var(--home-bg);background-size:cover;background-position:center;background-repeat:no-repeat;transform:translateZ(0);will-change:transform}.home-bg::after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(5,8,16,.52) 0%,rgba(5,8,16,.58) 48%,rgba(5,8,16,.86) 100%),radial-gradient(circle at 50% 20%,rgba(167,139,250,.18),transparent 42%)}.light .home-bg::after{background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(248,250,252,.58) 0%,rgba(248,250,252,.72) 52%,rgba(238,244,251,.94) 100%),radial-gradient(circle at 48% 18%,rgba(109,93,252,.16),transparent 46%)}.light .hero p{color:#475569;text-shadow:0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.75)}.light .title-rotate{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#252061 0%,#6257f5 46%,#168fc0 100%);-webkit-background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent;background-clip:text}.particles{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:-1;overflow:hidden;pointer-events:none}.particle{position:absolute;width:3px;height:3px;background:rgba(167,139,250,.3);border-radius:50%;animation:float 15s infinite;will-change:transform,opacity}.particle:nth-child(2n){background:rgba(6,182,212,.25)}@keyframes float{0%,100%{transform:translateY(100vh) scale(0);opacity:0}10%{opacity:1}90%{opacity:1}100%{transform:translateY(-10vh) scale(1)}}.preview-frame{width:100%;max-width:720px;height:380px;border-radius:24px;border:1px solid var(--line);overflow:hidden;margin:40px auto 0;box-shadow:0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,.4);background:rgba(0,0,0,.3);position:relative}.preview-frame::before{content:"Live - 48px,980px);padding:.05em 0 .16em;line-height:1.16;color:var(--text);background:linear-gradient(135deg,#e0e7ff 0%,#a78bfa 30%,#22d3ee 70%,#c4b5fd .3s}.copyright-card:hover{border-color:rgba(167,139,250,.4);color:var(--text)}.copyright-card strong{color:var(--text);font-size:13px;display:block;margin-bottom:4px}.copyright-card class="title-rotate">${titleText}</span></h1><p>${escapeHtml(homeText.hero)}</p><div class="actions"><a class="btn btn-primary" href="/api/random" target="_blank">${escapeHtml(homeText.random)}</a> <a img=document.getElementById('livePreview');var PREVIEW=${bgImageJs};function fetchImgInfo(){var x=new s=document.getElementById('imgSource');if(i)i.textContent=d.author?(d.title+' — '+d.author):(d.source);if(s)s.textContent=d.source||${thirdPartyJs}}catch(e){}}};x.send()}function */};next.src=PREVIEW+'&_t='+Date.now();fetchImgInfo()}img&&(img.onerror=function(){img.style.background='linear-gradient(135deg,#1e293b,#0f172a)'});fetchImgInfo();setInterval(refreshPreview,15000);})();startBannerCarousel();</script></body></html>`, 200, { "Set-Cookie": bgAccessCookieHeader(bgSessionId, config.bgAccess?.ttlSeconds) });
}

async function renderFeatured(request, url, env, config) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const en = lang === "en";
  const favicon = escapeAttr(siteFaviconHref(env));
  const presets = await getAllPresets(env.DB);
  const title = en ? "Featured Wallpapers" : "今日推荐";
  const desc = en ? "Curated preset themes powered by the same random image API. Pick a scene and get a stable, shareable endpoint." : "基于预设模板生成的主题推荐。选择一个场景，即可得到稳定、可分享的随机图片入口。";
  const cards = presets.slice(0, 6).map(preset => {
    const href = buildApiPath({ preset: preset.slug }, false);
    const jsonHref = buildApiPath({ preset: preset.slug }, true);
    const params = preset.params || {};
    const pills = [params.query, params.orientation, params.color, params.source].filter(Boolean).map(v => `<span class="pill">${escapeHtml(v)}</span>`).join(" ");
    return `<article class="card feature-card"><img src="${escapeAttr(href)}" alt="${escapeAttr(preset.name)}" loading="lazy"><div class="feature-body"><h3>${escapeHtml(preset.name)}</h3><p>${escapeHtml(preset.description || "")}</p><div class="preset-pills">${pills}</div><div class="toolbar" style="margin-top:16px"><a class="btn" href="${escapeAttr(href)}" target="_blank">${en ? "Open" : "打开图片"}</a><a class="btn" href="${escapeAttr(jsonHref)}" target="_blank">JSON</a><a class="btn" href="/url-builder?preset=${escapeAttr(preset.slug)}${en ? "&lang=en" : ""}">${en ? "Customize" : "继续定制"}</a><a class="btn" href="/share/preset/${escapeAttr(preset.slug)}${en ? "?lang=en" : ""}">${en ? "Share" : "分享"}</a></div></div></article>`;
  }).join("");
  return htmlResponse(`<!doctype html><html lang="${en ? "en" : "zh-CN"}"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><link rel="icon" href="${favicon}"><title>${escapeHtml(title)} — ${escapeHtml(site)}</title>${commonPublicAssets()}<style>.feature-hero{max-width:860px;margin:0 auto 34px;text-align:center}.feature-hero h1{font-size:clamp(34px,6vw,64px);margin:0 0 14px;font-weight:900;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#e0e7ff,#a78bfa,#22d3ee);-webkit-background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent}.feature-hero p{color:var(--muted);line-height:1.8;font-size:18px}.feature-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(280px,1fr));gap:22px}.feature-card{padding:0;overflow:hidden}.feature-card img{width:100%;height:210px;object-fit:cover;display:block;background:rgba(0,0,0,.22)}.feature-body{padding:24px}.preset-pills{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:12px}</style></head><body>${renderBanner(config)}<main class="shell">${publicNav(request, env, "featured")}<section class="feature-hero"><h1>${escapeHtml(title)}</h1><p>${escapeHtml(desc)}</p><div class="actions" style="display:flex;gap:12px;justify-content:center;flex-wrap:wrap"><a class="btn btn-primary" href="/api/random?info=true" target="_blank">${en ? "Try random JSON" : "试试随机 JSON"}</a><a class="btn" href="/url-builder${en ? "?lang=en" : ""}">${en ? "Build my own" : "自己生成链接"}</a></div></section><section class="feature-grid">${cards}</section></main></body></html>`);
}

async function renderAnalytics(env, token, session, csrf, config, flash, request) {
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const en = lang === "en";
  let totals = { total: 0, ok: 0, cache: 0, avgLatency: 0, uniqueIps: 0 };
  let sources = [], queries = [], filters = [];
  try {
    totals = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) total, SUM(ok) ok, SUM(cache_hit) cache, AVG(latency) avgLatency, COUNT(DISTINCT ip_hash) uniqueIps FROM request_analytics WHERE ts >= datetime('now','-7 days')").first() || totals;
    sources = (await env.DB.prepare("SELECT source, COUNT(*) total, SUM(ok) ok, SUM(cache_hit) cache, AVG(latency) avgLatency FROM request_analytics WHERE ts >= datetime('now','-7 days') GROUP BY source ORDER BY total DESC").all()).results || [];
    queries = (await env.DB.prepare("SELECT query, COUNT(*) total FROM request_analytics WHERE ts >= datetime('now','-7 days') AND query <> '' GROUP BY query ORDER BY total DESC LIMIT 12").all()).results || [];
    filters = (await env.DB.prepare("SELECT orientation, color, aspect_ratio, COUNT(*) total FROM request_analytics WHERE ts >= datetime('now','-7 days') GROUP BY orientation, color, aspect_ratio ORDER BY total DESC LIMIT 12").all()).results || [];
  } catch (_) {}
  const total = Number(totals.total || 0);
  const pct = n => total ? Math.round((Number(n || 0) / total) * 100) : 0;
  const stat = (label, value, hint, icon) => `<div class="card stat-card"><h3>${icon} ${escapeHtml(label)}</h3><p>${escapeHtml(value)}</p><span class="muted">${escapeHtml(hint || "")}</span></div>`;
  const sourceRows = sources.map(r => {
    const okPct = r.total ? Math.round((Number(r.ok || 0) / Number(r.total)) * 100) : 0;
    const cachePct = r.total ? Math.round((Number(r.cache || 0) / Number(r.total)) * 100) : 0;
    return `<tr><td>${escapeHtml(r.source || "random")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(r.total || 0)}</td><td><span class="pill">✅ ${okPct}%</span></td><td><span class="pill">⚡ ${cachePct}%</span></td><td>${Math.round(Number(r.avgLatency || 0))}ms</td></tr>`;
  }).join("");
  const queryRows = queries.map(r => `<tr><td>${escapeHtml(r.query || "-")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(r.total || 0)}</td><td><div style="height:8px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.08);overflow:hidden"><div style="height:100%;width:${Math.max(4, pct(r.total))}%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--brand),var(--brand2))"></div></div></td></tr>`).join("");
  const filterRows = filters.map(r => `<tr><td>${escapeHtml(r.orientation || "all")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(r.color || "-")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(r.aspect_ratio || "-")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(r.total || 0)}</td></tr>`).join("");
  const body = `<section class="grid">${stat(en ? "Requests" : "请求量", String(total), en ? "Last 7 days" : "最近 7 天", "📈")}${stat(en ? "Success" : "成功率", pct(totals.ok) + "%", en ? "Non-error API responses" : "非错误 API 响应", "✅")}${stat(en ? "Cache hit" : "缓存命中", pct(totals.cache) + "%", en ? "Served from D1 image pools" : "来自 D1 图片池", "⚡")}${stat(en ? "Unique clients" : "独立客户端", String(totals.uniqueIps || 0), en ? "Hashed IP count" : "按 IP 哈希统计", "👥")}</section><section class="card"><h2>${en ? "Source performance" : "图源表现"}</h2><table><tr><th>${en ? "Source" : "图源"}</th><th>${en ? "Requests" : "请求"}</th><th>${en ? "Success" : "成功"}</th><th>${en ? "Cache" : "缓存"}</th><th>${en ? "Avg latency" : "平均延迟"}</th></tr>${sourceRows || `<tr><td colspan="5" class="muted">${en ? "No analytics yet" : "暂无统计数据"}</td></tr>`}</table></section><div class="grid"><section class="card"><h2>${en ? "Popular keywords" : "热门关键词"}</h2><table><tr><th>${en ? "Keyword" : "关键词"}</th><th>${en ? "Requests" : "请求"}</th><th>${en ? "Share" : "占比"}</th></tr>${queryRows || `<tr><td colspan="3" class="muted">-</td></tr>`}</table></section><section class="card"><h2>${en ? "Filter usage" : "筛选使用"}</h2><table><tr><th>${en ? "Orientation" : "方向"}</th><th>${en ? "Color" : "颜色"}</th><th>${en ? "Aspect" : "比例"}</th><th>${en ? "Requests" : "请求"}</th></tr>${filterRows || `<tr><td colspan="4" class="muted">-</td></tr>`}</table></section></div>`;
  return renderAdminLayout({ token, active: "analytics", title: en ? "Analytics" : "统计分析", body, csrfToken: csrf, flash, favicon: siteFaviconHref(env), site: siteName(env), lang, request });
}

async function renderPresets(env, token, session, csrf, config, flash, request) {
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const en = lang === "en";
  const presets = await getAllPresets(env.DB);
  const rows = presets.map(p => {
    const api = buildApiPath({ preset: p.slug }, false);
    const params = JSON.stringify(p.params || {});
    const deleteForm = p.builtin ? `<span class="muted">${en ? "Built-in" : "内置"}</span>` : `<form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/presets/delete${en ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><input type="hidden" name="slug" value="${escapeAttr(p.slug)}"><button class="danger" style="padding:7px 12px;font-size:12px">${en ? "Delete" : "删除"}</button></form>`;
    return `<tr><td><strong>${escapeHtml(p.name)}</strong><br><span class="muted">${escapeHtml(p.slug)}</span></td><td>${escapeHtml(p.description || "")}</td><td><code>${escapeHtml(params).slice(0, 180)}</code></td><td><a href="${escapeAttr(api)}" target="_blank">${en ? "Open" : "打开"}</a></td><td>${deleteForm}</td></tr>`;
  }).join("");
  const body = `<form class="card" method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/presets/add${en ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><h2>${en ? "Add preset" : "添加预设"}</h2><div class="grid"><div><label>Slug</label><input name="slug" placeholder="desktop-blue"></div><div><label>${en ? "Name" : "名称"}</label><input name="name" placeholder="Desktop Blue"></div></div><label>${en ? "Description" : "描述"}</label><input name="description" placeholder="${en ? "Short preset summary" : "简短说明"}"><div class="grid"><div><label>${en ? "Keyword" : "关键词"}</label><input name="query" placeholder="nature / city night"></div><div><label>${en ? "Orientation" : "方向"}</label><select name="orientation"><option value="all">${en ? "Any" : "任意"}</option><option value="landscape">landscape</option><option value="portrait">portrait</option><option value="squarish">squarish</option></select></div><div><label>${en ? "Source" : "图源"}</label><select name="source"><option value="random">random</option>${SOURCES.map(s => `<option value="${s}">${s}</option>`).join("")}</select></div><div><label>${en ? "Color" : "颜色"}</label><select name="color"><option value="">-</option>${ALLOWED_COLORS.map(c => `<option value="${c}">${c}</option>`).join("")}</select></div></div><button>${en ? "Save preset" : "保存预设"}</button></form><div class="card"><h2>${en ? "Preset list" : "预设列表"}</h2><table><tr><th>${en ? "Preset" : "预设"}</th><th>${en ? "Description" : "描述"}</th><th>${en ? "Params" : "参数"}</th><th>API</th><th>${en ? "Action" : "操作"}</th></tr>${rows}</table></div>`;
  return renderAdminLayout({ token, active: "presets", title: en ? "Presets" : "预设模板", body, csrfToken: csrf, flash, favicon: siteFaviconHref(env), site: siteName(env), lang, request });
}

async function renderPresetShare(request, url, env, config, slug) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const en = lang === "en";
  const preset = await getPresetBySlug(env.DB, slug);
  if (!preset) return renderPublicErrorPage({ request, config, status: 404, code: "preset_not_found", title: en ? "Preset not found" : "预设不存在", message: en ? "This preset may be private or removed." : "这个预设可能已关闭公开访问或已被删除。", actionText: en ? "View featured" : "查看推荐", actionHref: "/featured", site });
  const api = buildApiPath({ preset: preset.slug }, false);
  const json = buildApiPath({ preset: preset.slug }, true);
  const shareUrl = `${url.origin}/share/preset/${encodeURIComponent(preset.slug)}`;
  const qr = `https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=180x180&data=${encodeURIComponent(shareUrl)}`;
  const params = JSON.stringify(preset.params || {}, null, 2);
  const title = en ? "Preset Share" : "预设分享";
  return htmlResponse(`<!doctype html><html lang="${en ? "en" : "zh-CN"}"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><link rel="icon" href="${escapeAttr(siteFaviconHref(env))}"><title>${escapeHtml(preset.name)} — ${escapeHtml(site)}</title>${commonPublicAssets()}<style>.share-hero{text-align:center;max-width:820px;margin:0 auto 28px}.share-hero h1{font-size:clamp(34px,6vw,64px);margin:0 0 12px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#e0e7ff,#a78bfa,#22d3ee);-webkit-background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent}.share-layout{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) 240px;gap:22px}.share-preview{width:100%;height:360px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:20px;border:1px solid var(--line)}.qr{background:white;padding:16px;border-radius:18px;width:212px}@media(max-width:760px){.share-layout{grid-template-columns:1fr}.qr{width:180px}}</style></head><body>${renderBanner(config)}<main class="shell">${publicNav(request, env, "featured")}<section class="share-hero"><h1>${escapeHtml(preset.name)}</h1><p>${escapeHtml(preset.description || (en ? "Shareable OmniWall preset" : "可分享的 OmniWall 预设"))}</p></section><section class="share-layout"><div class="card"><img class="share-preview" src="${escapeAttr(api)}" alt="${escapeAttr(preset.name)}"><div class="toolbar" style="margin-top:18px"><a class="btn btn-primary" href="${escapeAttr(api)}" target="_blank">${en ? "Open image" : "打开图片"}</a><a class="btn" href="${escapeAttr(json)}" target="_blank">JSON</a><a class="btn" href="/url-builder?preset=${escapeAttr(preset.slug)}${en ? "&lang=en" : ""}">${en ? "Customize" : "继续定制"}</a><button class="btn" onclick="navigator.clipboard.writeText('${escapeAttr(shareUrl)}').then(function(){toast('${en ? "Copied" : "已复制"}')})">${en ? "Copy share link" : "复制分享链接"}</button></div><h3>${en ? "Parameters" : "参数"}</h3><pre>${escapeHtml(params)}</pre></div><aside class="card" style="text-align:center"><img class="qr" src="${escapeAttr(qr)}" alt="QR"><p class="muted">${en ? "QR code for this share page" : "扫码打开此分享页"}</p><code>${escapeHtml(preset.slug)}</code></aside></section></main></body></html>`);
}

async function renderDaily(request, url, env, config) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const en = lang === "en";
  const title = en ? "Daily Picks" : "每日精选";
  const desc = en ? "Stable daily wallpapers generated from your OmniWall sources." : "从 OmniWall 多图源自动生成的稳定每日壁纸。";
  const cards = ["landscape", "portrait", "squarish"].map(o => {
    const api = `/api/daily?orientation=${o}`;
    return `<article class="card feature-card"><img src="${api}" alt="${o}" loading="lazy"><div class="feature-body"><h3>${o}</h3><p>${en ? "Same image for the current CST day." : "当天固定返回同一张图片。"}</p><div class="toolbar" style="margin-top:16px"><a class="btn" href="${api}" target="_blank">${en ? "Open" : "打开"}</a><a class="btn" href="${api}&info=true" target="_blank">JSON</a></div></div></article>`;
  }).join("");
  return htmlResponse(`<!doctype html><html lang="${en ? "en" : "zh-CN"}"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><link rel="icon" href="${escapeAttr(siteFaviconHref(env))}"><title>${escapeHtml(title)} — ${escapeHtml(site)}</title>${commonPublicAssets()}<style>.feature-hero{max-width:860px;margin:0 auto 34px;text-align:center}.feature-hero h1{font-size:clamp(34px,6vw,64px);margin:0 0 14px;font-weight:900;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#e0e7ff,#a78bfa,#22d3ee);-webkit-background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent}.feature-hero p{color:var(--muted);line-height:1.8;font-size:18px}.feature-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(260px,1fr));gap:22px}.feature-card{padding:0;overflow:hidden}.feature-card img{width:100%;height:240px;object-fit:cover;display:block;background:rgba(0,0,0,.22)}.feature-body{padding:24px}</style></head><body>${renderBanner(config)}<main class="shell">${publicNav(request, env, "daily")}<section class="feature-hero"><h1>${escapeHtml(title)}</h1><p>${escapeHtml(desc)}</p></section><section class="feature-grid">${cards}</section></main></body></html>`);
}

async function renderExplore(request, url, env, config) {

  const en = lang === "en";
  return htmlResponse(`<!doctype html><html lang="${en ? "Explore wallpapers" : "探索壁纸"}</h1><p>${en ? "Search, filter and copy ready-to-use OmniWall image links." : "搜索、筛选并复制可直接使用的 OmniWall 图片链接。"}</p></section><section class="card"><div class="explore-form"><label>${en ? "Keyword" : "关键词"}<input id="q" value="nature"></label><label>${en ? "Source" : "图源"}<select id="src"><option value="random">random</option>${SOURCES.map(s => `<option value="${s}">${s}</option>`).join("")}</select></label><label>${en ? "Orientation" : "方向"}<select id="ori"><option value="landscape">landscape</option><option value="portrait">portrait</option><option value="squarish">squarish</option><option value="all">all</option></select></label><label>${en ? "Quality" : "质量"}<select id="qual"><option value="balanced">balanced</option><option value="high">high</option><option value="fast">fast</option></select></label><button id="load" type="button">${en ? "Load" : "加载"}</button></div></section><section id="waterfall" class="waterfall"></section><p style="text-align:center"><button id="more" type="button">${en ? "Load more" : "加载更多"}</button></p></main><script>(function(){var box=document.getElementById('waterfall');function api(){var u='/api/random?info=true';var q=document.getElementById('q').value.trim();if(q)u+='&query='+encodeURIComponent(q);var s=document.getElementById('src').value;if(s&&s!=='random')u+='&source='+s;var o=document.getElementById('ori').value;if(o&&o!=='all')u+='&orientation='+o;var qu=document.getElementById('qual').value;if(qu)u+='&quality='+qu;return u}function esc(x){return String(x||'').replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]})}function card(d){var link='/api/random?query='+encodeURIComponent(document.getElementById('q').value.trim()||'wallpaper')+'&orientation='+document.getElementById('ori').value+'&source='+document.getElementById('src').value;var img=esc(d.url);return '<article class="wcard"><img src="'+img+'" alt=""><div class="wbody"><strong>'+esc(d.title||d.source||'Image')+'</strong><p>'+esc(d.author||'')+' · '+esc(d.source||'')+' · Q '+esc(d.quality_score||'-')+'</p><div class="toolbar"><a class="btn smallbtn" href="'+img+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${en ? "Copy API" : "复制 API"}</button></div></div></article>'}function load(reset){if(reset)box.innerHTML='';Promise.all(Array.from({length:8},function(){return fetch(api()).then(function(r){return r.json()}).catch(function(){return null})})).then(function(list){box.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',list.filter(Boolean).filter(function(x){return x.url}).map(card).join(''))})}document.addEventListener('click',function(e){var c=e.target.getAttribute&&e.target.getAttribute('data-copy');if(c)navigator.clipboard.writeText(c).then(function(){toast('${en ? }

async function renderDocs(request, url, env, config) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const en = lang === "en";
  const quotaRows = await getQuotaRows(env.DB, env);
  const quotaHtml = quotaRows.map(q => `<tr><td>${escapeHtml(q.source)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(q.used ?? "-")}/${escapeHtml(q.limit ?? "-")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(q.remaining ?? "-")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(q.updated_at || "-")}</td></tr>`).join("");
  const ipHash = await hashIdentifier(getClientIp(request), env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "fallback");
  let rateData;
  try {
    const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT timestamps FROM rate_limits WHERE ip_hash = ? AND window_type = 'combined'").bind(ipHash).first();
    rateData = row ? JSON.parse(row.timestamps) : { m: [], h: [], d: [] };
  } catch (_) { rateData = { m: [], h: [], d: [] }; }
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const rateWindows = en ? [["Minute", "minute", 60, "m"], ["Hour", "hour", 3600, "h"], ["Day", "day", 86400, "d"]] : [["分钟", "minute", 60, "m"], ["小时", "hour", 3600, "h"], ["天", "day", 86400, "d"]];
  const myRateHtml = rateWindows.map(([rname, label, ttl, code]) => {
    const count = (rateData[code] || []).filter(t => now - t < ttl).length;
    const limit = config.rateLimits[label];
    const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((count / limit) * 100));
    const barColor = pct >= 90 ? "#ef4444" : pct >= 70 ? "#f59e0b" : "#22c55e";
    return `<tr><td>${rname}</td><td>${count} / ${limit}</td><td><div style="width:100%;height:8px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.08);border-radius:4px;overflow:hidden"><div style="width:${pct}%;height:100%;background:${barColor};border-radius:4px;transition:width .5s"></div></div></td></tr>`;
  }).join("");
  const favicon = escapeAttr(siteFaviconHref(env));
  let changelog = [];
  try { const r = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT date, version, title, content FROM changelog ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 20").all(); changelog = (r.results || []).map(x => x); } catch (_) {}
  const origin = escapeHtml(url.origin);
  const linkItem = (title, href, jsonHref, fixHref) => `<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:10px;font-size:16px">${title}</div><a href="${href}" target="_blank" style="word-break:break-all;color:#93c5fd">${origin}${href}</a><div style="margin-top:10px;font-size:13px;color:var(--muted)"><a href="${jsonHref}" target="_blank" style="color:var(--muted);text-decoration:none">ℹ️ JSON</a> · <a href="${fixHref}" target="_blank" style="color:#f87171;text-decoration:none">🔄 修复</a></div></div>`;
  return htmlResponse(`<!doctype html><html lang="zh-CN"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><link rel="icon" href="${favicon}"><title>${en ? "Docs" : "文档"} — ${escapeHtml(site)}</title>${commonPublicAssets()}<style>table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin-top:12px}td,th{padding:12px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);text-align:left;font-size:14px}th{color:var(--muted);font-weight:600;font-size:13px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em}h2{margin-top:40px;font-size:20px;font-weight:700;padding-bottom:10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}h2:first-child{margin-top:0}h3{font-size:16px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text);margin:24px 0 8px}h1{font-size:36px;font-weight:900;margin-bottom:8px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#e0e7ff,#a78bfa);-webkit-background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent;background-clip:text}ul{padding-left:20px;line-height:2}li{color:var(--muted)}code{background:rgba(167,139,250,.15);color:#c4b5fd;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:6px;font-size:13px;border:1px solid rgba(167,139,250,.2)}pre code{background:transparent;border:none;padding:0;color:inherit}.policy-section{margin-top:20px}.policy-section h3{margin-top:0;color:#a78bfa}</style></head><body>${renderBanner(config)}<main class="shell">${publicNav(request, env, "docs")}<div class="card"><h1>${en ? "Docs" : "文档"}</h1><p style="color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:24px">${en ? "Base endpoint" : "基础接口"}：<code>${origin}/api/random</code></p><pre>GET /api/random
GET /api/random?source=unsplash
GET /api/random?orientation=landscape
GET /api/random?query=nature
GET /api/random?info=true
GET /api/random?reload=true
GET /api/random?preset=desktop-nature
GET /api/random?query=city&color=blue&min_width=1920&aspect=wide</pre><h2>${en ? "Parameters" : "参数说明"}</h2><ul><li><code>preset</code> — 预设模板：desktop-nature / mobile-city / minimal-dark / warm-orange，后台可新增自定义预设</li><li><code>source</code> — 图源：random / unsplash / pexels / pixabay / bing；random 会按后台优先级和健康状态路由</li><li><code>orientation</code> — 方向：landscape / portrait / squarish</li><li><code>query</code> — 搜索关键词，支持中文自动翻译和智能扩展</li><li><code>color</code> — 主色筛选：black / white / yellow / orange / red / purple / magenta / green / teal / blue / black_and_white</li><li><code>min_width</code> / <code>min_height</code> — 最小尺寸筛选</li><li><code>aspect</code> — 比例筛选：wide / tall / square</li><li><code>expand=false</code> — 关闭智能关键词扩展</li><li><code>info=true</code> — 返回 JSON 元数据，不跳转图片</li><li><code>reload=true</code> — 强制刷新缓存</li></ul><h2>${en ? "Quick Links" : "快捷链接"}</h2>${linkItem("💻 电脑壁纸 (横屏)", "/api/random?orientation=landscape", "/api/random?orientation=landscape&info=true", "/api/random?orientation=landscape&reload=true&info=true")}${linkItem("📱 手机壁纸 (竖屏)", "/api/random?orientation=portrait", "/api/random?orientation=portrait&info=true", "/api/random?orientation=portrait&reload=true&info=true")}${linkItem("🎲 随机 (任意)", "/api/random", "/api/random?info=true", "/api/random?reload=true&info=true")}${linkItem("🔍 指定关键词 (nature)", "/api/random?query=nature", "/?query=nature&info=true", "/?query=nature&reload=true&info=true")}${linkItem("🔍 中文关键词 (猫)", "/api/random?query=猫", "/api/random?query=猫&info=true", "/api/random?query=猫&reload=true&info=true")}${linkItem("⭐ 预设模板 (desktop-nature)", "/api/random?preset=desktop-nature", "/api/random?preset=desktop-nature&info=true", "/api/random?preset=desktop-nature&reload=true&info=true")}${linkItem("🎨 蓝色宽屏", "/api/random?query=city&color=blue&aspect=wide", "/api/random?query=city&color=blue&aspect=wide&info=true", "/api/random?query=city&color=blue&aspect=wide&reload=true&info=true")}<div style="background:rgba(34,197,94,.08);border:1px solid rgba(34,197,94,.2);border-radius:14px;padding:14px 18px;margin-top:16px"><span style="color:#86efac;font-weight:600">💡 翻译提示：</span><span style="color:var(--muted)">输入中文关键词时，系统会自动调用翻译 API（Google Translate → MyMemory 备用）将其转换为英文。例如"猫"会被翻译为"cat"。在返回的 JSON 中可查看 <code>translated_query</code> 字段确认翻译结果。</span></div><h2>${en ? "Current Rate Limits" : "当前限速"}</h2><table><tr><th>${en ? "Window" : "窗口"}</th><th>${en ? "Limit" : "限制"}</th></tr><tr><td>分钟</td><td>${escapeHtml(config.rateLimits.minute)}</td></tr><tr><td>小时</td><td>${escapeHtml(config.rateLimits.hour)}</td></tr><tr><td>天</td><td>${escapeHtml(config.rateLimits.day)}</td></tr></table><h2>${en ? "Your IP Usage" : "你的 IP 使用情况"}</h2><table><tr><th>${en ? "Window" : "窗口"}</th><th>${en ? "Used / Limit" : "已用 / 限制"}</th><th>${en ? "Progress" : "进度"}</th></tr>${myRateHtml}</table><h2>${en ? "API Quota" : "API 配额"}</h2><table><tr><th>${en ? "Source" : "图源"}</th><th>${en ? "Used/Total" : "已用/总量"}</th><th>${en ? "Remaining" : "剩余"}</th><th>${en ? "Updated" : "更新时间"}</th></tr>${quotaHtml}</table><h2 id="copyright">版权与隐私声明</h2><div style="color:var(--muted);line-height:1.9"><div class="policy-section"><h3>一、数据来源与版权归属</h3><p>本服务提供的图片均来自第三方公开图片平台，包括 Unsplash、Pexels、Pixabay 和 Bing 每日壁纸。图片版权归原作者和对应平台所有，本服务仅作为重定向代理，不存储、不修改原始图片内容。</p><p>使用本服务时请遵守以下平台的使用条款：Unsplash License、Pexels Terms of Use、Pixabay Terms of Service 以及 Microsoft Bing 使用条款。商业用途时请注意部分图片可能需要署名。</p></div><div class="policy-section"><h3>二、数据收集与处理</h3><p>为保护服务和防止滥用，系统会收集以下信息：</p><ul><li><strong>IP 地址哈希值</strong>：原始 IP 经 SHA-256 哈希后截取前 48 位，不可逆还原。用于限速计数和临时封禁。</li><li><strong>访问日志</strong>：记录请求时间、请求参数（关键词、方向等）及响应状态，用于故障排查和配额监控。</li><li><strong>搜索关键词</strong>：用于图片缓存和中文自动翻译，不用于用户画像分析。</li></ul></div><div class="policy-section"><h3>三、Cookie 与本地存储</h3><p>本服务使用以下 Cookie：</p><ul><li><strong>banner_dismissed</strong>：记录用户是否关闭了通知横幅，有效期 24 小时。</li><li><strong>omniwall_lang</strong>：记录用户语言偏好（中文/英文），有效期 1 年。</li></ul></div><div class="policy-section"><h3>四、服务免责声明</h3><p>本服务按"现状"提供，不对图片内容的准确性、完整性或适用性做任何担保。因第三方 API 故障、网络中断或其他不可抗力导致的服务不可用，本服务不承担任何责任。</p></div><div class="policy-section"><h3>五、内容安全</h3><p>系统会对搜索关键词进行本地内容审核，拦截包含恶意代码、危险脚本或违规内容的关键词请求。同时可选接入 OpenAI Moderation、Google Perspective API 和 Azure Content Safety 进行外部审核。</p></div><p style="margin-top:20px;font-size:13px;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:40px">最后更新：2026-06-26 · ${site}</p></div>
<div class="card" id="json-info"><h2>JSON 元数据说明</h2><p style="color:var(--muted);line-height:1.8">当您访问 API 时加上 <code>info=true</code> 参数，接口将返回 JSON 格式的图片元数据而不是直接跳转到图片。JSON（JavaScript Object Notation）是一种轻量级的数据交换格式，适合程序读取和处理。</p><h3 style="margin-top:16px">返回字段说明</h3><ul><li><code>source</code> — 图片来源（unsplash / pexels / pixabay / bing）</li><li><code>url</code> — 图片直链地址</li><li><code>title</code> — 图片标题或描述</li><li><code>author</code> — 摄影师名称</li><li><code>author_url</code> — 作者主页链接</li><li><code>width</code> / <code>height</code> — 图片原始尺寸</li><li><code>translated</code> — 是否经过中文翻译（<code>true</code>/<code>false</code>）</li><li><code>translated_query</code> — 翻译后的英文关键词</li><li><code>original_query</code> — 用户输入的原始关键词</li><li><code>expanded</code> / <code>expanded_query</code> — 是否进行了智能关键词扩展以及最终检索词</li><li><code>preset</code> / <code>color</code> / <code>aspect_ratio</code> — 当前使用的预设和筛选参数</li></ul><pre style="margin-top:12px">{
  "source": "unsplash",
  "url": "https://images.unsplash.com/xxx",
  "title": "Mountain landscape",
  "author": "John Doe",
  "width": 4032,
  "height": 3024,
  "translated": false
}</pre></div>
<div class="card" id="api-status"><h2>图源实时状态</h2><p style="color:var(--muted);font-size:13px;margin:0 0 16px">每 5 分钟检测一次，展示最近 24 小时在线度与延迟（绿色=在线，红色=离线）。</p>${monitorWidget("docsMonitor", lang)}</div>
<div class="card" id="changelog"><h2>更新动态</h2>${changelog.length ? `<div style="color:var(--muted);min-height:0">${changelog.slice(0,10).map(item => `<div style="background:rgba(167,139,250,.06);border:1px solid rgba(167,139,250,.1);border-radius:12px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:12px"><div style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start"><span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);white-space:nowrap">${escapeHtml(item.date)}</span><span style="font-size:12px;background:rgba(167,139,250,.15);color:#c4b5fd;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:6px">${escapeHtml(item.version || "v1.0")}</span></div><strong style="display:block;margin:8px 0 4px;color:var(--text)">${escapeHtml(item.title)}</strong><p style="font-size:13px;margin:0;line-height:1.6">${escapeHtml(item.content)}</p></div>`).join("")}</div>` : `<p class="muted">暂无更新记录</p>`}</div>
<div class="card" id="copyright-attribution"><h2>版权署名指南</h2><h3>首页版权卡片说明</h3><p style="color:var(--muted);line-height:1.8">${site} 首页右下角的版权卡片会动态展示当前预览图片的实际来源信息。它通过首页内部带签名凭证的 <code>/api/bg?info=true</code> 获取当前图片的标题、摄影师和来源平台，实时更新显示。</p><p style="color:var(--muted);line-height:1.8">显示格式：<code>"图片标题 — 摄影师名称"</code> + 来源标签（如 unsplash）。此信息与图片 API 返回的 JSON 元数据一致。</p><h3>为什么需要署名？</h3><p style="color:var(--muted);line-height:1.8">本服务使用的图片来自第三方开放平台，图片版权归原作者所有。在商业或公开项目中使用时，请遵守各平台的署名要求，尊重原创作者权益。</p><h3>各平台署名要求</h3><ul><li><strong>Unsplash</strong>：建议附上摄影师链接，非强制但推荐。格式：<code>Photo by [Author] on Unsplash</code></li><li><strong>Pexels</strong>：可自由使用，无需署名。但附上作者来源是专业素养的体现。</li><li><strong>Pixabay</strong>：大部分图片无需署名。请查阅具体图片的许可说明。</li><li><strong>Bing</strong>：仅供个人非商业用途，请遵循微软使用条款。</li></ul><h3>如何在您自己的网站中制作版权卡片</h3><p style="color:var(--muted);line-height:1.8">如果您使用 OmniWall API 嵌入图片，可以在页面底部添加署名：</p><pre>&lt;a href="作者主页链接"&gt;
  Photo by 作者名称
&lt;/a&gt;
&lt;a href="https://unsplash.com"&gt;
  on Unsplash
&lt;/a&gt;</pre></div></div></main></body></html>`);
}

async function renderUrlBuilder(request, url, env, config) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const en = lang === "en";
  const favicon = escapeAttr(siteFaviconHref(env));
  const origin = escapeHtml(url.origin);
  const presets = await getAllPresets(env.DB);
  const presetOptions = presets.map(p => `<option value="${escapeAttr(p.slug)}">${escapeHtml(p.name)}</option>`).join("");
  const colorOptions = ALLOWED_COLORS.map(c => `<option value="${escapeAttr(c)}">${escapeHtml(c)}</option>`).join("");
  // Pre-encode the JS string that appears in the template to avoid any escaping issues
  const JS = `"use strict";
var ubQ=document.getElementById('ub-q'),ubO=document.getElementById('ub-o'),ubS=document.getElementById('ub-s'),ubP=document.getElementById('ub-p'),ubC=document.getElementById('ub-c'),ubW=document.getElementById('ub-w'),ubH=document.getElementById('ub-h'),ubA=document.getElementById('ub-a'),ubE=document.getElementById('ub-e');
var ubResult=document.getElementById('ub-result'),ubResultJson=document.getElementById('ub-result-json');
var ubPrev=document.getElementById('ub-preview'),ubPrevJ=document.getElementById('ub-preview-json');
var ubTs=document.getElementById('ub-translate-status');
function ubDecode(s){try{return decodeURIComponent(s)}catch(e){return s}}
function ubHasChinese(s){return /[^\\x00-\\x7F]/.test(s)}
function ubGetParams(){var u=new URL('/api/random',location.origin);var q=ubQ.value.trim();var o=ubO.value;var s=ubS.value;var p=ubP.value;var c=ubC.value;var w=ubW.value;var h=ubH.value;var a=ubA.value;
  if(p)u.searchParams.set('preset',p);if(q)u.searchParams.set('query',q);if(o)u.searchParams.set('orientation',o);
  if(s&&s!=='random')u.searchParams.set('source',s);if(c)u.searchParams.set('color',c);if(w)u.searchParams.set('min_width',w);if(h)u.searchParams.set('min_height',h);if(a)u.searchParams.set('aspect',a);if(ubE&&!ubE.checked)u.searchParams.set('expand','false');
  if(ubTs){ubTs.style.display=ubHasChinese(q)?'block':'none';ubTs.innerHTML=ubHasChinese(q)?'💡 中文关键词已自动编码，访问时服务端会将其翻译为英文再搜图':''}
  return u}
function ubBuildUrl(){var u=ubGetParams();var href=u.href;
  var uJson=ubGetParams();uJson.searchParams.set('info','true');var hrefJson=uJson.href;
  ubResult.textContent=ubDecode(href);ubResultJson.textContent=ubDecode(hrefJson);
  ubPrev.href=href;ubPrevJ.href=hrefJson}
function ubCopy(){ubBuildUrl();var raw=ubPrev.href;navigator.clipboard.writeText(raw).then(function(){if(typeof toast==='function')toast(${en ? "'Link copied'" : "'链接已复制'"},'success')}).catch(function(){alert(${en ? "'Copy failed: '" : "'复制失败，请手动复制：'"}+raw)})}
function ubCopyJson(){ubBuildUrl();var raw=ubPrevJ.href;navigator.clipboard.writeText(raw).then(function(){if(typeof toast==='function')toast(${en ? "'JSON link copied'" : "'JSON 链接已复制'"},'success')}).catch(function(){alert(${en ? "'Copy failed: '" : "'复制失败，请手动复制：'"}+raw)})}
document.getElementById('ub-btn-build').addEventListener('click',function(){ubBuildUrl()});
document.getElementById('ub-btn-copy').addEventListener('click',function(){ubCopy()});
document.getElementById('ub-btn-copy-json').addEventListener('click',function(){ubCopyJson()});`;
  return htmlResponse(`<!doctype html><html lang="zh-CN"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><link rel="icon" href="${favicon}"><title>${en ? "URL Builder" : "链接生成器"} — ${escapeHtml(site)}</title>${commonPublicAssets()}
<style>#ub-result,#ub-result-json{background:var(--glass);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;padding:16px;font-family:'SF Mono',monospace;font-size:14px;word-break:break-all}#ub-result{margin-top:20px;color:var(--brand)}#ub-result-json{margin-top:10px;color:var(--brand2,#22d3ee)}#ub-result-json::before{content:"JSON 链接：";display:block;font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:6px;font-family:inherit;letter-spacing:.02em}</style></head><body>${renderBanner(config)}
<main class="shell">${publicNav(request, env, "builder")}
<div class="card"><h1>${en ? "URL Builder" : "链接生成器"}</h1><p style="color:var(--muted)">${en ? "Configure parameters visually and generate API links in one click." : "可视化配置参数，一键生成 API 链接"}</p>
<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(220px,1fr));gap:20px;margin:24px 0">
	<div><label style="margin-top:0;font-weight:500;color:var(--text)">${en ? "Preset" : "预设模板"}<select id="ub-p"><option value="">${en ? "None" : "不使用"}</option>${presetOptions}</select></label></div>
	<div><label style="margin-top:0;font-weight:500;color:var(--text)">${en ? "Keyword" : "关键词（中英文均可）"}<input id="ub-q" placeholder="nature / city / 猫"></label></div>

	<div><label style="margin-top:0;font-weight:500;color:var(--text)">${en ? "Aspect" : "比例"}<select id="ub-a"><option value="">${en ? "Any" : "任意"}</option><option value="wide">wide</option><option value="tall">tall</option><option value="square">square</option></select></label></div></div>
	<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px"><input id="ub-e" type="checkbox" checked style="width:auto;margin:0"> ${en ? "Enable smart keyword expansion" : "启用智能关键词扩展"}</label>
	<div style="display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:24px 0">

<button type="button" class="btn" id="ub-btn-copy">${en ? "📋 Copy Image Link" : "📋 复制图片链接"}</button>
<button type="button" class="btn" id="ub-btn-copy-json">${en ? "📋 Copy JSON Link" : "📋 复制 JSON 链接"}</button></div>
<div id="ub-translate-status" style="font-size:13px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:12px;display:none"></div>
<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:20px;padding-top:20px;border-top:1px solid var(--line)">
<a class="btn" id="ub-preview" href="/api/random" target="_blank">${en ? "👁️ Preview Image" : "👁️ 预览图片"}</a>
<a class="btn" id="ub-preview-json" href="/api/random?info=true" target="_blank">${en ? "📄 Preview JSON" : "📄 预览 JSON"}</a></div>
<div id="ub-result">${origin}/api/random</div>
<div id="ub-result-json">${origin}/api/random?info=true</div></div></main>
<script>${JS}</script></body></html>`);
}

  const site = siteName(env);
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const en = lang === "en";
  const favicon = escapeAttr(siteFaviconHref(env));
  const origin = escapeHtml(url.origin);
  const turnstile = renderTurnstileWidget(config, env, "feedback");
  const script = turnstileScript(config, env);
  return htmlResponse(`<!doctype html><html lang="zh-CN"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><link rel="icon" href="${favicon}"><title>${en ? "Feedback" : "问题反馈"} — ${escapeHtml(site)}</title>${commonPublicAssets()}${script}<style>h1{font-size:32px;font-weight:900;margin-bottom:8px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#e0e7ff,#a78bfa);-webkit-background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent;background-clip:text}.form-group{margin-bottom:20px}.form-group label{display:block;margin-bottom:8px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text)}.form-group input,.form-group select,.form-group textarea{width:100%;padding:12px;background:var(--glass);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;color:var(--text);font-size:15px;font-family:inherit}.form-group textarea{min-height:120px;resize:vertical}.form-group small{display:block;margin-top:6px;color:var(--muted);font-size:13px}.form-group input::placeholder,.form-group textarea::placeholder{color:var(--muted);opacity:.7}#feedbackForm button[type=submit]{background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--brand),var(--brand2));color:#fff;border:none;padding:14px 32px;border-radius:12px;font-size:16px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;transition:transform .2s,box-shadow .2s;box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(167,139,250,.3);width:auto}#feedbackForm button[type=submit]:hover{transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:0 8px 30px rgba(167,139,250,.5)}#feedbackForm button[type=submit]:active{transform:translateY(0)}.severity-badge{display:inline-block;padding:4px 12px;border-radius:999px;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;margin-left:8px}.sev-5{background:rgba(239,68,68,.2);color:#ef4444}.sev-4{background:rgba(249,115,22,.2);color:#f97316}.sev-3{background:rgba(234,179,8,.2);color:#eab308}.sev-2{background:rgba(59,130,246,.2);color:#3b82f6}.sev-1,.sev-0{background:rgba(148,163,184,.2);color:#94a3b8}</style></head><body>${renderBanner(config)}<main class="shell">${publicNav(request, env, "feedback")}<div class="card"><h1>${en ? async function handleBgImage(request, env, ctx, config) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  const wantInfo = url.searchParams.get("info") === "true";
  const bgAccess = await verifyBgAccessToken(request, env, config);
  if (!bgAccess.ok) return await handleBgAccessViolation(request, env, ctx, config, bgAccess.reason, wantInfo, bgAccess.ban !== false);
  // /api/bg 不走普通限速，但仍应尊重非 bg 触发的封禁。
  // 旧版本会因正常首页 cookie/token 兼容问题误封，合法 bg_token 通过后自动清掉这类 bg_* 临时封禁。
  const bgBan = await checkBan(request, env);
  if (!bgBan.ok) {
    const reason = bgBan.ban?.reason || "ip_banned";
    if (isBgAccessBanReason(reason)) {
      await clearBanByHash(env.DB, bgBan.ipHash);
    } else {
      if (wantInfo) return publicJsonResponse({ ok: false, error: "banned", reason }, 403);
      return publicSecurityErrorResponse(request, config, "banned", { reason, expiresAt: bgBan.ban?.expires_at || "" }, 403);
    }
  }
  const params = {
    query: normalizeQuery(url.searchParams.get("query") || config.home?.backgroundQuery || "wallpaper"),
    orientation: normalizeOrientation(url.searchParams.get("orientation")),
    source: (url.searchParams.get("source") || "random").toLowerCase(),
    color: normalizeColor(url.searchParams.get("color")),
    minWidth: normalizeDimension(url.searchParams.get("min_width") || url.searchParams.get("minWidth")),
    minHeight: normalizeDimension(url.searchParams.get("min_height") || url.searchParams.get("minHeight")),
    aspectRatio: normalizeAspectRatio(url.searchParams.get("aspect") || url.searchParams.get("aspect_ratio")),
    quality: normalizeQuality(url.searchParams.get("quality"), config.quality?.default),
    safeMode: true,
    expand: normalizeBooleanParam(url.searchParams.get("expand"), true),
    forceReload: url.searchParams.get("reload") === "true",
    hasExplicitQuery: false,
  };
  const valid = validatePublicInput({ query: params.query, orientation: params.orientation, source: params.source, color: params.color, aspect: params.aspectRatio });
  if (!valid.ok) {
    if (wantInfo) return publicJsonResponse({ ok: false, error: valid.reason }, 400);
    return renderPublicErrorPage({ request, config, status: 400, code: "invalid_request", title: "请求参数不可用", message: "这个图片请求的参数有问题，请检查图源、方向或关键词后再试。", detail: valid.reason, actionText: "去生成链接", actionHref: "/url-builder", site });
  }
  params.originalQuery = params.query;
  params.query = await translateQueryIfNeeded(params.query, env);
  const expansion = await expandQueryIfNeeded(params.query, env, config, params);
  params.expandedQuery = expansion.query;
  params.expansionTerms = expansion.terms || [];
  params.expanded = !!expansion.expanded;
  params.query = expansion.query;
  const result = await fetchWithFallback(params, env, ctx, config);
  if (!result.ok) {
    if (wantInfo) return publicJsonResponse({ ok: false, error: result.error }, result.status || 503);
    const errorType = classifyFetchError(result);
    return renderPublicErrorPage({ request, config, status: result.status || 503, code: errorType, title: errorType === "quota" ? "图片额度暂时用完" : "图源暂时不可用", message: errorType === "quota" ? "上游图片服务的调用额度可能已经耗尽，请稍后再试或切换其他图源。" : "当前图片源暂时没有返回可用图片，请稍后再试。", detail: result.error || "", actionText: "查看文档", actionHref: "/docs", site });
  }
  const image = result.image;
  if (wantInfo) {
    return publicJsonResponse({
      source: image.source || result.source,
      url: image.url,
      title: image.title || "",
      author: image.author || "Unknown",
      author_url: image.author_url || "",
      width: image.width,
      height: image.height,
    });
  }
  return redirectResponse(image.url, "public, max-age=5", {
    "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
    "X-Image-Source": image.source || result.source,
    "X-Image-Title": encodeURIComponent(image.title || "Image"),
    "X-Image-Author": encodeURIComponent(image.author || "Unknown"),
    "X-Author-Link": image.author_url || "",
  });
}

async function handleDailyImage(request, env, ctx, config) {
  const security = await applyPublicSecurity(request, env, ctx, config);
  if (security) return security;
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  const result = await getDailyImage(env, ctx, config, { orientation: url.searchParams.get("orientation") || "landscape", query: url.searchParams.get("query") || config.home?.dailyQuery });
  if (!result.ok) return publicApiErrorResponse({ error: result.error || "daily_unavailable", attempts: result.attempts || [] }, result.status || 503, { "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*" });
  const image = result.image;
  if (url.searchParams.get("info") === "true" || wantsJson(request)) return publicJsonResponse({ ...image, ok: true, daily_date: result.date, daily_orientation: result.orientation });
  return redirectResponse(image.url, "public, max-age=300", { "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*", "X-Image-Source": image.source || "daily", "X-Image-Title": encodeURIComponent(image.title || "Daily Image"), "X-Image-Author": encodeURIComponent(image.author || "Unknown") });
}

async function handleRandomImage(request, env, ctx, config) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const apiAuth = await authenticateApiKey(request, env);
  if (!apiAuth.ok) return apiAuth.response;
  const apiLimit = await applyApiKeyLimit(request, env, config, apiAuth.key);
  if (apiLimit) return apiLimit;
  const security = await applyPublicSecurity(request, env, ctx, config);
  if (security) return security;
  const startMs = Date.now();
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  const preset = await getPresetBySlug(env.DB, url.searchParams.get("preset"));
  const rawParams = mergePresetParams({
    query: url.searchParams.has("query") ? url.searchParams.get("query") : "",
    orientation: url.searchParams.get("orientation") || "",
    source: (url.searchParams.get("source") || "random").toLowerCase(),
    color: url.searchParams.get("color") || "",
    minWidth: url.searchParams.get("min_width") || url.searchParams.get("minWidth") || "",
    minHeight: url.searchParams.get("min_height") || url.searchParams.get("minHeight") || "",
    aspectRatio: url.searchParams.get("aspect") || url.searchParams.get("aspect_ratio") || "",
    quality: url.searchParams.get("quality") || "",
    expand: url.searchParams.has("expand") ? normalizeBooleanParam(url.searchParams.get("expand"), true) : undefined,
  }, preset);
  const hasExplicitQuery = (url.searchParams.has("query") && url.searchParams.get("query").trim() !== "") || !!preset?.params?.query;
  const params = {
    query: normalizeQuery(rawParams.query || "wallpaper"),
    orientation: normalizeOrientation(rawParams.orientation),
    source: (rawParams.source || "random").toLowerCase(),
    color: normalizeColor(rawParams.color),
    minWidth: normalizeDimension(rawParams.minWidth),
    minHeight: normalizeDimension(rawParams.minHeight),
    aspectRatio: normalizeAspectRatio(rawParams.aspectRatio),
    quality: normalizeQuality(rawParams.quality || url.searchParams.get("quality"), config.quality?.default),
    safeMode: normalizeBooleanParam(url.searchParams.get("safe"), true),
    expand: rawParams.expand !== undefined ? rawParams.expand : true,
    wantInfo: url.searchParams.get("info") === "true",
    forceReload: url.searchParams.get("reload") === "true",
    hasExplicitQuery,
    preset: preset?.slug || "",
    apiKeyId: apiAuth.key?.id || "",
  };
  const valid = validatePublicInput({ query: params.query, orientation: params.orientation, source: params.source, color: params.color, aspect: params.aspectRatio, preset: params.preset });
  if (!valid.ok) {
    ctx.waitUntil(appendLog(env.DB, "warn", "Rejected public input", { reason: valid.reason, ip: await hashIdentifier(getClientIp(request), env.ADMIN_PASSWORD) }));
    if (params.wantInfo || wantsJson(request)) return publicJsonResponse({ ok: false, error: valid.reason }, 400);
    return renderPublicErrorPage({ request, config, status: 400, code: "invalid_request", title: "请求参数不可用", message: "这个图片请求的参数有问题，请检查图源、方向或关键词后再试。", detail: valid.reason, actionText: "去生成链接", actionHref: "/url-builder", site });
  }
  if (params.hasExplicitQuery) {
    const moderation = await moderateContent(params.query, env);
    if (!moderation.ok) {
      ctx.waitUntil(appendLog(env.DB, "warn", "Moderation rejected query", { moderation, ip: await hashIdentifier(getClientIp(request), env.ADMIN_PASSWORD) }));
      if (params.wantInfo || wantsJson(request)) return publicJsonResponse({ ok: false, error: "content_rejected", moderation }, 400);
      return renderPublicErrorPage({ request, config, status: 400, code: "content_rejected", title: "关键词不可用", message: "这个关键词暂时不能用于搜图，请换一个更安全、更明确的关键词。", imageType: "inputRejected", actionText: "去生成链接", actionHref: "/url-builder", site });
    }
  }
  params.originalQuery = params.query;
  params.query = await translateQueryIfNeeded(params.query, env);
  const expansion = await expandQueryIfNeeded(params.query, env, config, params);
  params.expandedQuery = expansion.query;
  params.expansionTerms = expansion.terms || [];
  params.expanded = !!expansion.expanded;
  params.query = expansion.query;
  const ipHash = await hashIdentifier(getClientIp(request), env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "fallback");
  const result = await fetchWithFallback(params, env, ctx, config);
  if (!result.ok) {
    ctx.waitUntil(trackRequestAnalytics(env, request, params, { ok: false, source: params.source }, startMs, ipHash, result.status || 503));
    if (params.wantInfo || wantsJson(request)) return publicJsonResponse({ ok: false, error: result.error, attempts: result.attempts || [] }, result.status || 503);
    const errorType = classifyFetchError(result);
    return renderPublicErrorPage({ request, config, status: result.status || 503, code: errorType, title: errorType === "quota" ? "图片额度暂时用完" : "图源暂时不可用", message: errorType === "quota" ? "上游图片服务的调用额度可能已经耗尽，请稍后再试或切换其他图源。" : "当前图片源暂时没有返回可用图片，请稍后再试。", detail: result.error || "", actionText: "查看文档", actionHref: "/docs", site });
  }
  ctx.waitUntil(trackRequestAnalytics(env, request, params, result, startMs, ipHash, 200));
  const image = { ...result.image, cache_hit: result.cacheHit, cache_key: result.cacheKey, translated: params.query !== params.originalQuery, translated_query: params.query, original_query: params.originalQuery, preset: params.preset || undefined, color: params.color || undefined, min_width: params.minWidth || undefined, min_height: params.minHeight || undefined, aspect_ratio: params.aspectRatio || undefined, quality: params.quality, expanded: params.expanded, expanded_query: params.expandedQuery, expansion_terms: params.expansionTerms, api_key: apiAuth.key ? { id: apiAuth.key.id, name: apiAuth.key.name, prefix: apiAuth.key.prefix } : undefined };
  if (params.wantInfo) return publicJsonResponse(image);
  return redirectResponse(image.url, "public, max-age=5", {
    "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
    "X-Image-Source": image.source || result.source,
    "X-Image-Title": encodeURIComponent(image.title || "Image"),
    "X-Image-Author": encodeURIComponent(image.author || "Unknown"),
    "X-Author-Link": image.author_url || "",
  });
}

async function handleContentCheck(request, env, ctx, config) {
  const security = await applyPublicSecurity(request, env, ctx, config);
  if (security) return security;
  const contentType = request.headers.get("content-type") || "";
  let payload = {};
  let text = "";
  let turnstileToken = "";
  if (contentType.includes("application/json")) {
    payload = await request.clone().json().catch(() => ({}));
    text = payload.text || payload.query || "";
    turnstileToken = String(payload["cf-turnstile-response"] || payload.turnstileToken || "");
  } else {
    const form = await request.formData().catch(() => null);
    text = form ? (form.get("text") || form.get("query") || "") : await request.text();
    turnstileToken = form ? String(form.get("cf-turnstile-response") || form.get("turnstileToken") || "") : "";
  }
  if (turnstileEnabled(config, env, "checkContent")) {
    const verification = await verifyTurnstileToken(request, env, turnstileToken);
    if (!verification.ok) {
      ctx.waitUntil(appendLog(env.DB, "warn", "Content check Turnstile failed", { error: verification.error, codes: verification.codes || [], ip: await hashIdentifier(getClientIp(request), env.ADMIN_PASSWORD) }, env));
      return publicJsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: "turnstile_failed", message: "人机验证失败，请刷新后重试" }, 403);
    }
  }
  const moderation = await moderateContent(text, env);
  return publicJsonResponse(moderation.ok ? { ok: true, reason: moderation.suspicious ? "suspicious_external_failed" : "clean", moderation } : { ok: false, reason: moderation.reason, moderation });
}

async function handleFeedbackSubmit(request, env, ctx, config) {
  const security = await applyPublicSecurity(request, env, ctx, config);
  if (security) return security;
  const form = await request.formData();
  if (turnstileEnabled(config, env, "feedback")) {
    const verification = await verifyTurnstileToken(request, env, String(form.get("cf-turnstile-response") || ""));
    if (!verification.ok) {
      ctx.waitUntil(appendLog(env.DB, "warn", "Feedback Turnstile failed", { error: verification.error, codes: verification.codes || [], ip: await hashIdentifier(getClientIp(request), env.ADMIN_PASSWORD) }, env));
      return publicJsonResponse({ ok: false, error: "人机验证失败，请刷新后重试" }, 403);
    }
  }
  const type = String(form.get("type") || "other");
  const title = String(form.get("title") || "").trim().slice(0, 200);
  const content = String(form.get("content") || "").trim().slice(0, 2000);
  const contact = String(form.get("contact") || "").trim().slice(0, 200);
  if (!title || !content) return publicJsonResponse({ ok: false, error: "标题和内容不能为空" }, 400);
  const ipHash = await hashIdentifier(getClientIp(request), env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "fallback");
  const userAgent = String(request.headers.get("user-agent") || "").slice(0, 500);
  const result = await submitFeedback(env.DB, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, type, title, content, contact, ipHash, userAgent, env);
  if (result.ok) {
    ctx.waitUntil(sendNotification("feedback_received", { type, title: title.slice(0, 50), ip: ipHash.slice(0, 16), time: nowIso() }, env));
    return publicJsonResponse({ ok: true });
  }
  return publicJsonResponse({ ok: false, error: result.error || "提交失败" }, 500);
}

async function handlePublicPage(request, env, config) {
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  if (url.pathname === "/") return renderHome(request, url, env, config);
  if (url.pathname === "/featured") return renderFeatured(request, url, env, config);
  if (url.pathname === "/explore") return renderExplore(request, url, env, config);
  if (url.pathname === "/daily") return renderDaily(request, url, env, config);
  if (url.pathname.startsWith("/share/preset/")) return renderPresetShare(request, url, env, config, decodeURIComponent(url.pathname.split("/").pop() || ""));
  if (url.pathname === "/docs") return renderDocs(request, url, env, config);
  if (url.pathname === "/url-builder") return renderUrlBuilder(request, url, env, config);
  if (url.pathname === "/feedback") return renderFeedback(request, url, env, config);
  return null;
}

function renderAdminLayout({ token, active, title, body, csrfToken, flash, favicon = DEFAULT_FAVICON_DATA_URI, site = DEFAULT_SITE_NAME, lang = "zh", request }) {
  const groups = [
    { label: lang === "en" ? "Overview" : "概览", items: [["dashboard", "dashboard"], ["analytics", "analytics"]] },
    { label: lang === "en" ? "Resources" : "资源", items: [["images", "images"], ["presets", "presets"], ["monitor", "monitor"]] },
    { label: lang === "en" ? "Security" : "安全", items: [["security", "security"], ["moderation", "moderation"], ["api-keys", "apiKeys"]] },
    { label: lang === "en" ? "Notifications" : "通知", items: [["notifications", "notifications"]] },
    { label: lang === "en" ? "System" : "系统", items: [["settings", "settings"], ["updates", "updates"], ["feedback-management", "feedback"]] },
    { label: lang === "en" ? "Account" : "账户", items: [["account", "account"]] },
  ];
  const nav = groups.map(g => {
    const items = g.items.map(([key, labelKey]) => {
      const href = `/${escapeAttr(token)}/${key}${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}`;
      return `<a class="${active === key ? "active" : ""}" href="${href}">${_(labelKey, lang)}</a>`;
    }).join("");
    return `<div style="margin-bottom:8px"><div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;padding:8px 14px 4px">${escapeHtml(g.label)}</div>${items}</div>`;
  }).join("");
  const switchHref = langSwitchLink(request, lang);
  const switchText = _("langSwitch", lang);
  const themeLabels = lang === "en" ? { auto: "Auto", light: "Light", dark: "Dark" } : { auto: "自动", light: "浅色", dark: "深色" };
  const themeScript = `<script>function applyAdminTheme(mode){var root=document.documentElement;var prefer=window.matchMedia&&window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: light)').matches;var light=mode==='light'||(!mode||mode==='auto')&&prefer;root.classList.toggle('light',!!light);var labels=document.querySelectorAll('.theme-label');labels.forEach(function(el){el.textContent=(mode==='light'?(el.getAttribute('data-light')||'Light'):mode==='dark'?(el.getAttribute('data-dark')||'Dark'):(el.getAttribute('data-auto')||'Auto'))})}function toggleAdminTheme(){var cur=localStorage.getItem('omniwall_admin_theme')||'auto';var next=cur==='auto'?'light':cur==='light'?'dark':'auto';localStorage.setItem('omniwall_admin_theme',next);applyAdminTheme(next)}document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){var qp=new URLSearchParams(location.search);var lang=qp.get('lang');if(lang==='en'||lang==='zh')document.cookie='omniwall_lang='+lang+';path=/;max-age=31536000;samesite=lax';applyAdminTheme(localStorage.getItem('omniwall_admin_theme')||'auto');if(window.matchMedia)window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: light)').addEventListener('change',function(){if((localStorage.getItem('omniwall_admin_theme')||'auto')==='auto')applyAdminTheme('auto')})});</script>`;
  const logo = brandLogoHtml(favicon, site);
  return adminHtmlResponse(`<!doctype html><html lang="${lang === "en" ? "en" : "zh-CN"}"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><link rel="icon" href="${escapeAttr(favicon)}"><title>${escapeHtml(title)} — ${escapeHtml(site)}</title>${adminStyles()}${themeScript}</head><body><div class="layout"><aside class="side">${logo}${nav}<div class="admin-tools"><a class="tool-btn" href="${escapeAttr(switchHref)}">🌐 ${escapeHtml(switchText)}</a><button type="button" class="tool-btn" onclick="toggleAdminTheme()">🌓 <span class="theme-label" data-auto="${escapeAttr(themeLabels.auto)}" data-light="${escapeAttr(themeLabels.light)}" data-dark="${escapeAttr(themeLabels.dark)}">${escapeHtml(themeLabels.auto)}</span></button></div><form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/logout"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrfToken)}"><button class="logout-btn">${_("logout", lang)}</button></form></aside><main class="main"><h1>${escapeHtml(title)}</h1>${flash ? `<div class="flash">${escapeHtml(flash)}</div>` : ""}${body}</main></div></body></html>`);
}

async function renderLogin(request, env, ctx, token, message = "", config = {}) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const daily = await getDailyAdminPath(env.DB, env, ctx, request);
  if (token !== daily) return renderAdminErrorPage({ request, token, status: 403, title: "后台入口已过期", message: "这个管理入口已经过期或无效。", detail: "请通过 Telegram Bot 发送 /admin 命令获取今日有效入口。", actionText: "返回首页", actionHref: "/", site });
  const username = await getAdminUsername(env.DB, env);
  const totpState = await getTotpState(env.DB, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, env);
  const favicon = escapeAttr(siteFaviconHref(env));
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const l = (k) => _(k, lang);
  const bg = `${new URL(request.url).origin}/api/random?orientation=landscape`;
  const turnstileHtml = renderTurnstileWidget(config, env, "adminLogin");
  const loginMessage = String(message || "");
  const loginLogo = `<img src="${favicon}" alt="" style="width:46px;height:46px;border-radius:12px;object-fit:cover;box-shadow:0 0 0 1px var(--line)">`;
  const script = turnstileScript(config, env);
  const totpLabel = totpState.enabled
    ? `TOTP ${l("verifyCode")}（${l("totpEnabled")}，${lang === "en" ? "required" : "必填"}）`
    : `TOTP ${l("verifyCode")}（${l("totpDisabled")}，${lang === "en" ? "optional" : "可选"}）`;
  const totpRequired = totpState.enabled ? "required" : "";
  return adminHtmlResponse(`<!doctype html><html lang="${lang === "en" ? "en" : "zh-CN"}"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><link rel="icon" href="${favicon}"><title>${l("login")} — ${escapeHtml(site)}</title>${script}<style>
:root{color-scheme:dark;--bg:#050810;--text:#f1f5f9;--muted:#94a3b8;--line:rgba(148,163,184,.15);--brand:#8b5cf6;--brand2:#06b6d4;--danger:#ef4444}*{box-sizing:border-box}body{margin:0;min-height:100vh;display:grid;place-items:center;font-family:'Segoe UI',system-ui,sans-serif;color:var(--text);background:#050810 url('${escapeAttr(bg)}') center/cover no-repeat fixed}body::before{content:"";position:fixed;inset:0;background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(5,8,16,.88),rgba(15,23,42,.75));z-index:-1}.login-card{width:min(420px,calc(100% - 0 6px;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em}input{width:100%;padding:15px 18px;border-radius:16px;border:1px solid var(--line);background:rgba(15,23,42,.85);color:var(--text);font-size:15px;transition:all .3s}input:focus{outline:none;border-color:rgba(139,92,246,.5);box-shadow:0 0 0 4px rgba(139,92,246,.12),0 0 30px rgba(139,92,246,.1)}.flash{padding:14px 18px;border-radius:16px;background:rgba(239,68,68,.12);border:1px solid rgba(239,68,68,.25);color:#fca5a5;font-size:14px;margin-bottom:24px;animation:shake .5s}@keyframes
