</style></head><body><form class="login-card" method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/auth${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="lang" value="${lang}"><div class="brand">${loginLogo}</div><div class="title">${escapeHtml(site)}</div><div class="subtitle">${l("login")}</div>${loginMessage ? `<div class="flash">${escapeHtml(loginMessage)}</div>` : ""}<label>${l("username")}</label><input name="username" value="${escapeAttr(username)}" autocomplete="username" required><label>${l("passwordInfo")}</label><input name="password" type="password" autocomplete="current-password" required><label>${totpLabel}</label><input name="totp" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="one-time-code" placeholder="000000" maxlength="6" ${totpRequired}>${turnstileHtml}<button>${l("login")}</button><div class="hint">${lang === "en" ? "First login uses ADMIN_USERNAME; random daily path is generated afterward." : "首次登录使用 ADMIN_USERNAME；登录后生成每日随机入口。"}<br>${lang === "en" ? "Lost entry? Use Telegram Bot /admin command." : "入口丢失？请使用 Telegram Bot /admin 命令获取。"}</div></form></body></html>`);
}

async function handleAuth(request, env, ctx, token, config = {}) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const daily = await getDailyAdminPath(env.DB, env, ctx, request);
  if (token !== daily) return renderAdminErrorPage({ request, token, status: 403, title: "后台入口已过期", message: "这个管理入口已经过期或无效。", detail: "请通过 Telegram Bot 发送 /admin 命令获取今日有效入口。", actionText: "返回首页", actionHref: "/", site });
  const form = await request.formData();
  const username = String(form.get("username") || "");
  const password = String(form.get("password") || "");
  const expectedUser = await getAdminUsername(env.DB, env);
  const ipHash = await hashIdentifier(getClientIp(request), env.ADMIN_PASSWORD);
  if (turnstileEnabled(config, env, "adminLogin")) {
    const verification = await verifyTurnstileToken(request, env, String(form.get("cf-turnstile-response") || ""));
    if (!verification.ok) {
      ctx.waitUntil(appendLog(env.DB, "warn", "Admin Turnstile failed", { ip: ipHash, error: verification.error, codes: verification.codes || [] }));
      return renderLogin(request, env, ctx, token, "人机验证失败，请刷新后重试", config);
    }
  }
  if (username !== expectedUser || password !== env.ADMIN_PASSWORD) {
    ctx.waitUntil(appendLog(env.DB, "warn", "Admin login failed", { ip: ipHash, user: username }));
    ctx.waitUntil(sendNotification("login_failed", { ip: ipHash.slice(0, 16), user: username, time: nowIso() }, env));
    return renderLogin(request, env, ctx, token, "用户名或密码错误", config);
  }
  const totp = await getTotpState(env.DB, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, env);
  if (totp.enabled && !await verifyTotp(form.get("totp"), totp.secret)) {
    ctx.waitUntil(appendLog(env.DB, "warn", "Admin TOTP failed", { ip: ipHash, user: username }));
    ctx.waitUntil(sendNotification("login_failed", { ip: ipHash.slice(0, 16), user: username, reason: "totp", time: nowIso() }, env));
    return renderLogin(request, env, ctx, token, "TOTP 验证码错误", config);
  }
  let firstDone = null;
  try { const r = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM admin_config WHERE key = ?").bind(KV.CONFIG_FIRST_LOGIN_DONE).first(); firstDone = r ? r.value : null; } catch (_) {}
  const nextPath = firstDone ? daily : await rotateAdminPathAfterFirstLogin(env.DB, env, ctx, request);
  const exp = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + SESSION_TTL_SECONDS;
  const session = { sub: expectedUser, iat: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), exp, nonce: randomString(18) };
  const sessionToken = await signSession(session, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD);
  ctx.waitUntil(appendLog(env.DB, "info", "Admin login success", { ip: ipHash }));
  ctx.waitUntil(sendNotification("login_success", { ip: ipHash.slice(0, 16), user: username, time: nowIso() }, env));
  return redirectResponse(`/${nextPath}/dashboard`, "no-store", { "Set-Cookie": setSessionCookieHeader(sessionToken) });
}

async function renderDashboard(env, token, session, csrf, config, flash, request) {
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const l = (k) => _(k, lang);
  const logs = await getLogs(env.DB, 8, env);
  const quotaRows = await getQuotaRows(env.DB, env);
  const sourceCards = SOURCES.map(s => {
    const available = s === "unsplash" ? !!env.UNSPLASH_ACCESS_KEY : s === "pexels" ? !!env.PEXELS_API_KEY : s === "pixabay" ? !!env.PIXABAY_API_KEY : true;
    return `<div class="card"><h3>${escapeHtml(s)}</h3><p class="muted">${available ? `<span style="color:#22c55e">●</span> ${lang === "en" ? "Available" : "可用"}` : `<span style="color:#ef4444">●</span> ${lang === "en" ? "Not configured" : "未配置"}`}</p></div>`;
  }).join("");
  const logHtml = logs.map(l => `<div class="log">[${escapeHtml(l.ts || "-")}] ${escapeHtml(l.level || "info")} ${escapeHtml(l.message)}</div>`).join("") || `<p class="muted">${l("noLogs")}</p>`;
  const quotaHtml = quotaRows.map(q => {
    const used = Number(q.used) || 0;
    const limit = Number(q.limit) || 1;
    const pct = limit > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.round((used / limit) * 100)) : 0;
    const barColor = pct >= 90 ? "#ef4444" : pct >= 70 ? "#f59e0b" : "#22c55e";
    return `<tr><td>${escapeHtml(q.source)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(q.used ?? "-")}/${escapeHtml(q.limit ?? "-")}</td><td><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px"><div style="flex:1;height:8px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.08);border-radius:4px;overflow:hidden"><div style="width:${pct}%;height:100%;background:${barColor};border-radius:4px"></div></div><span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);min-width:40px">${escapeHtml(q.remaining ?? "-")}</span></div></td><td>${escapeHtml(q.updated_at || "-")}</td></tr>`;
  }).join("");
  const body = `<section class="grid">${sourceCards}<div class="card"><h3>${l("cacheTtl")}</h3><p>${escapeHtml(config.cacheTtl)} ${lang === "en" ? "seconds" : "秒"}</p></div></section><section class="card"><h2>${l("apiQuota")}</h2><table><tr><th>${l("source")}</th><th>${l("usedTotal")}</th><th>${l("remaining")}</th><th>${l("updatedAt")}</th></tr>${quotaHtml}</table></section><section class="card"><h2>${l("recentLogs")}</h2>${logHtml}</section><section class="card"><h2>${l("quickLinks")}</h2><div class="toolbar"><a class="btn" href="/api/random" target="_blank">${l("randomImage")}</a><a class="btn" href="/api/random?info=true" target="_blank">${l("viewJson")}</a><a class="btn" href="/docs${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}" target="_blank">${l("docs")}</a></div></section>`;
  return renderAdminLayout({ token, active: "dashboard", title: l("dashboard"), body, csrfToken: csrf, flash, favicon: siteFaviconHref(env), site: siteName(env), lang, request });
}

async function renderImages(env, token, session, csrf, config, flash, request) {
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const l = (k) => _(k, lang);
  const display = config.cacheTtl % 3600 === 0 ? config.cacheTtl / 3600 : config.cacheTtl % 60 === 0 ? config.cacheTtl / 60 : config.cacheTtl;
  const unit = config.cacheTtl % 3600 === 0 ? 3600 : config.cacheTtl % 60 === 0 ? 60 : 1;
  const opt = v => unit === v ? "selected" : "";
  let keys = [];
  try {
    const rows = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT cache_key, created_at FROM image_cache ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 60").all();
    keys = (rows.results || []).map(r => ({ name: r.cache_key, created_at: r.created_at || "" }));
  } catch (_) {}
  let sections = "";
  for (const key of keys) {
    const pool = await getCachedPool(env.DB, key.name, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, env);
    const images = pool?.images || [];
    const previewImages = shuffle(images).slice(0, Math.min(18, images.length));
    let cards = "";
    for (const img of previewImages) {
      const dec = await decryptCachedImage(img, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD);
      if (dec._decryptFailed || !dec.url) continue;
      const imgUrl = safeUrl(dec.url, "");
      cards += `<div class="img-card"><img src="${escapeAttr(imgUrl)}" loading="lazy" alt="${escapeAttr(dec.title || "image")}"><div><strong>${escapeHtml(dec.title || "Image")}</strong><br><span class="muted">${escapeHtml(dec.source || pool.source || "-")} · ${escapeHtml(dec.author || "Unknown")}</span><br><a href="${escapeAttr(imgUrl)}" target="_blank">${l("viewLarge")}</a></div></div>`;
    }
    sections += `<div class="card"><h3>${escapeHtml(key.name)} <span class="muted">(${images.length} ${lang === "en" ? "images" : "张"})</span></h3><p class="muted" style="font-size:12px;margin-top:-4px;margin-bottom:14px">${lang === "en" ? "Updated" : "更新时间"}：${escapeHtml(key.created_at || "-")}</p><div class="preview-grid">${cards || l("noPreview")}</div></div>`;
  }
  const body = `<div class="grid"><form class="card" method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/cache/settings${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><h2>${lang === "en" ? "Cache Settings" : "缓存设置"}</h2><p class="muted" style="font-size:13px;margin:0 0 12px">${lang === "en" ? "Control image pool refresh and homepage background override." : "管理图片池刷新周期，以及首页背景自定义覆盖。"}</p><label>${l("cacheRefresh")}</label><div style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-end"><input name="cacheTime" type="number" value="${escapeAttr(display)}" style="flex:1"><select name="cacheUnit" style="width:140px"><option value="1" ${opt(1)}>${l("second")}</option><option value="60" ${opt(60)}>${l("minute")}</option><option value="3600" ${opt(3600)}>${l("hour")}</option></select></div><label>${l("customBg")}</label><input name="customBg" value="${escapeAttr(config.customBg)}" placeholder="https://example.com/bg.jpg"><button>${l("save")}</button></form><div class="card"><h2>${lang === "en" ? "Cache Maintenance" : "缓存维护"}</h2><form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/cache/clear${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><button class="warn">${l("clearAllCache")}</button></form><form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/cache/clear-source${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}" style="margin-top:20px"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><div style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-end"><div style="flex:1"><label>${l("clearBySource")}</label><select name="source"><option value="unsplash">Unsplash</option><option value="pexels">Pexels</option><option value="pixabay">Pixabay</option><option value="bing">Bing</option></select></div><button>${l("clearBySource")}</button></div></form></div></div>${sections || `<div class="card"><p class="muted">${l("noCache")}</p></div>`}`;
  return renderAdminLayout({ token, active: "images", title: lang === "en" ? "Images / Cache" : "图片与缓存", body, csrfToken: csrf, flash, favicon: siteFaviconHref(env), site: siteName(env), lang, request });
}

async function renderSecurity(env, token, session, csrf, config, flash, request) {
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const l = (k) => _(k, lang);
  const checked = v => v ? "checked" : "";
  const turnstile = { ...DEFAULT_TURNSTILE_CONFIG, ...(config.turnstile || {}) };
  const bgAccess = { ...DEFAULT_BG_ACCESS, ...(config.bgAccess || {}) };
  const turnstileReady = !!(env.TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY && env.TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY);
  const banRows = [];
  try {
    const rows = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT ip_hash, reason, expires_at FROM bans ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 100").all();
    for (const b of (rows.results || [])) {
      banRows.push(`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(String(b.ip_hash || "").slice(0, 16))}</td><td>${escapeHtml(b.reason || "-")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(b.expires_at || "-")}</td><td><form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/security/unban${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><input type="hidden" name="key" value="ban:${escapeAttr(b.ip_hash)}"><button class="danger">${l("unban")}</button></form></td></tr>`);
    }
  } catch (_) {}
  let wlEntries = [];
  try { const r = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT ip_hash, ip, added_at FROM whitelist ORDER BY added_at DESC").all(); wlEntries = (r.results || []).map(w => ({ ipHash: w.ip_hash, ip: w.ip || "", added_at: w.added_at || "" })); } catch (_) {}
  const wlRows = wlEntries.map(w => `<tr><td>${escapeHtml(w.ip || "-")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(String(w.ipHash || "").slice(0, 16))}</td><td>${escapeHtml(w.added_at || "-")}</td></tr>`).join("");
  const body = `<div class="grid"><form class="card" method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/security/rate-limits${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><h2>${l("rateLimits")}</h2><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><label>${l("perMinute")}</label><input name="minute" type="number" value="${escapeAttr(config.rateLimits.minute)}"><label>${l("perHour")}</label><input name="hour" type="number" value="${escapeAttr(config.rateLimits.hour)}"><label>${l("perDay")}</label><input name="day" type="number" value="${escapeAttr(config.rateLimits.day)}"><button>${l("save")}</button></form><form class="card" method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/security/ban${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><h2>${l("addBan")}</h2><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><label>${l("ipAddress")}</label><input name="ip" placeholder="1.2.3.4"><label>${l("reason")}</label><input name="reason" value="manual"><label>${l("banSeconds")}</label><input name="seconds" type="number" value="3600"><button class="danger">${l("ban")}</button></form></div>
<form class="card" method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/security/turnstile${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><h2>${lang === "en" ? "Human Verification" : "人机验证"}</h2><p class="muted" style="font-size:13px;margin:0 0 12px">${turnstileReady ? (lang === "en" ? "Turnstile keys are configured in Worker variables." : "已检测到 Worker 变量中的 Turnstile Site Key 和 Secret Key。") : (lang === "en" ? "Set TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY and TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY first; switches are saved but inactive until then." : "请先在 Worker 变量中配置 TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY 和 TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY；开关会保存，但未配置密钥前不会生效。")}</p><label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" name="turnstile_enabled" style="width:auto;margin:0" ${checked(turnstile.enabled)}> ${lang === "en" ? "Enable Cloudflare Turnstile" : "启用 Cloudflare Turnstile"}</label><label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" name="turnstile_feedback" style="width:auto;margin:0" ${checked(turnstile.protectFeedback)}> ${lang === "en" ? "Protect feedback form" : "保护用户反馈提交"}</label><label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" name="turnstile_admin_login" style="width:auto;margin:0" ${checked(turnstile.protectAdminLogin)}> ${lang === "en" ? "Protect admin login" : "保护后台登录"}</label><label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" name="turnstile_check_content" style="width:auto;margin:0" ${checked(turnstile.protectContentCheck)}> ${lang === "en" ? "Token TTL (seconds)" : "凭证有效期（秒）"}</label><input name="bg_ttl" type="number" min="60" value="${escapeAttr(bgAccess.ttlSeconds)}"></div><div><label>${lang === "en" ? "Ban min (seconds)" : "封禁最短（秒）"}</label><input name="bg_ban_min" type="number" min="60" value="${escapeAttr(bgAccess.banMinSeconds)}"></div><div><label>${lang === "en" ? "Ban max (seconds)" : "封禁最长（秒）"}</label><input name="bg_ban_max" type="number" min="60" value="${escapeAttr(bgAccess.banMaxSeconds)}"></div></div><label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" name="bg_strict_hotlink_ban" style="width:auto;margin:0" ${checked(bgAccess.strictHotlinkBan)}> ${lang === "en" ? "Also ban obvious cross-site image hotlinking" : "对明显跨站图片盗链也执行封禁"}</label><button>${l("save")}</button></form>
<div class="card"><h2>${lang === "en" ? "Whitelist" : "白名单"}</h2><form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/security/whitelist/add${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><div style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-end"><div style="flex:1"><label>${lang === "en" ? "IP Address" : "IP 地址"}</label><input name="ip" placeholder="1.2.3.4"></div><button>${lang === "en" ? "Add" : "添加"}</button></div></form><p class="muted" style="font-size:13px;margin-top:12px">${lang === "en" ? "Whitelisted IPs bypass rate limits and auto-ban." : "白名单 IP 不受限速和自动封禁限制。"}</p>${wlRows ? `<table style="margin-top:14px"><tr><th>${lang === "en" ? "IP" : "IP"}</th><th>${lang === "en" ? "Hash" : "哈希"}</th><th>${lang === "en" ? "Added" : "添加时间"}</th></tr>${wlRows}</table>` : ""}</div>
<div class="card"><h2>${l("banList")}</h2><table><tr><th>IP Hash</th><th>${l("reason")}</th><th>${lang === "en" ? "Expires" : "过期"}</th><th>${lang === "en" ? "Action" : "操作"}</th></tr>${banRows.join("") || `<tr><td colspan="4" class="muted">${l("noBans")}</td></tr>`}</table></div>`;
  return renderAdminLayout({ token, active: "security", title: l("security"), body, csrfToken: csrf, flash, favicon: siteFaviconHref(env), site: siteName(env), lang, request });
}

async function renderModeration(env, token, session, csrf, config, flash, request) {
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const l = (k) => _(k, lang);
  const keywords = await getKeywords(env.DB, env);
  const keywordRows = keywords.map((k, i) => `<tr><td>${escapeHtml(k.word || k)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(k.reason || "-")}</td><td><form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/security/keywords/delete${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><input type="hidden" name="index" value="${i}"><button class="danger" style="padding:6px 12px;font-size:12px">${lang === "en" ? "Delete" : "删除"}</button></form></td></tr>`).join("");
  const apiStatus = [
    { name: "OpenAI Moderation", key: env.OPENAI_API_KEY },
    { name: "Google Perspective", key: env.PERSPECTIVE_API_KEY },
    { name: "Azure Content Safety", key: env.AZURE_CONTENT_SAFETY_ENDPOINT && env.AZURE_CONTENT_SAFETY_KEY },
  ].map(a => `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin:8px 0"><span style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;background:${a.key ? '#22c55e' : '#6b7280'};box-shadow:0 0 8px ${a.key ? 'rgba(34,197,94,.4)' : 'transparent'}"></span><span>${a.name}</span><span style="margin-left:auto;font-size:13px;color:${a.key ? '#22c55e' : 'var(--muted)'}">${a.key ? (lang === "en" ? "Connected" : "已接入") : (lang === "en" ? "Not configured" : "未配置")}</span></div>`).join("");
  const body = `<div class="card"><h2>${lang === "en" ? "Content Moderation" : "内容审核"}</h2><h3 style="font-size:14px;margin:16px 0 8px;color:var(--text)">${lang === "en" ? "External API Status" : "外部 API 状态"}</h3><div style="margin:12px 0">${apiStatus}</div><h3 style="font-size:14px;margin:16px 0 8px;color:var(--text)">${lang === "en" ? "Local Detection Rules" : "本地检测规则"}</h3><p class="muted" style="font-size:13px;line-height:1.8">${l("moderationDesc")}</p></div>
<div class="card"><h2>${lang === "en" ? "Keyword Blacklist" : "关键词黑名单"}</h2><p class="muted" style="font-size:13px;margin:0 0 12px">${lang === "en" ? `Built-in offline library active (${BUILTIN_KEYWORDS.length} baseline words, always enforced). Add custom words below.` : `内置离线词库已启用（${BUILTIN_KEYWORDS.length} 个基线词，始终生效）。可在下方添加自定义词。`}</p><form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/security/keywords/add${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><div style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-end"><div style="flex:1"><label>${lang === "en" ? "Keyword" : "关键词"}</label><input name="word" placeholder="${lang === "en" ? "Enter keyword" : "输入关键词"}"></div><div style="flex:1"><label>${lang === "en" ? "Reason (optional)" : "原因（可选）"}</label><input name="reason" placeholder="${lang === "en" ? "spam, abuse..." : "垃圾信息、滥用..."}"></div><button>${lang === "en" ? "Add" : "添加"}</button></div></form><table style="margin-top:16px"><tr><th>${lang === "en" ? "Keyword" : "关键词"}</th><th>${lang === "en" ? "Reason" : "原因"}</th><th>${lang === "en" ? "Action" : "操作"}</th></tr>${keywordRows || `<tr><td colspan="3" class="muted">${lang === "en" ? "No keywords" : "暂无关键词"}</td></tr>`}</table></div>`;
  return renderAdminLayout({ token, active: "moderation", title: l("moderation"), body, csrfToken: csrf, flash, favicon: siteFaviconHref(env), site: siteName(env), lang, request });
}

async function renderUpdates(env, token, session, csrf, config, flash, request) {
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const l = (k) => _(k, lang);
  let changelog = [];
  try {
    const rows = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT date, version, title, content FROM changelog ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 100").all();
    changelog = (rows.results || []).map(r => r);
  } catch (_) {}
  const langQs = lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : "";
  const rows = changelog.map((item, i) => `<tr><td style="white-space:nowrap">${escapeHtml(item.date)}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(item.version || "")}</strong><br>${escapeHtml(item.title)}</td><td style="max-width:300px;white-space:pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(item.content).slice(0, 200)}</td><td><form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/updates/delete${langQs}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><input type="hidden" name="index" value="${i}"><button class="danger" style="padding:6px 12px;font-size:12px">${lang === "en" ? "Delete" : "删除"}</button></form></td></tr>`).join("");
  const body = `<form class="card" method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/updates/add${langQs}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><h2>${lang === "en" ? "Add Update" : "添加更新记录"}</h2><div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:16px"><div><label>${lang === "en" ? "Date" : "日期"}</label><input name="date" type="date" value="${escapeHtml(todayUtc8())}"></div><div><label>${lang === "en" ? "Version" : "版本号"}</label><input name="version" placeholder="v2.1.0"></div></div><label>${lang === "en" ? "Title" : "标题"}</label><input name="title" placeholder="${lang === "en" ? "New feature summary" : "新功能概要"}"><label>${lang === "en" ? "Content" : "内容"}</label><textarea name="content" rows="4" placeholder="${lang === "en" ? "Describe what changed..." : "描述本次更新内容..."}"></textarea><button>${lang === "en" ? "Publish" : "发布"}</button></form>
<div class="card"><h2>${lang === "en" ? "Update History" : "更新历史"}</h2><table><tr><th>${lang === "en" ? "Date" : "日期"}</th><th>${lang === "en" ? "Version / Title" : "版本 / 标题"}</th><th>${lang === "en" ? "Content" : "内容"}</th><th>${lang === "en" ? "Action" : "操作"}</th></tr>${rows || `<tr><td colspan="4" class="muted">${lang === "en" ? "No updates yet" : "暂无更新记录"}</td></tr>`}</table></div>`;
  return renderAdminLayout({ token, active: "updates", title: l("updates"), body, csrfToken: csrf, flash, favicon: siteFaviconHref(env), site: siteName(env), lang, request });
}

async function renderNotifications(env, token, session, csrf, config, flash, request) {
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const l = (k) => _(k, lang);
  const configsByChannel = {
    telegram: await getEncryptedJson(env.DB, "notify:telegram", env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, {}, env),
    feishu: await getEncryptedJson(env.DB, "notify:feishu", env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, {}, env),
    dingtalk: await getEncryptedJson(env.DB, "notify:dingtalk", env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, {}, env),
    email: await getEncryptedJson(env.DB, "notify:email", env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, {}, env),
  };
  const { telegram, feishu, dingtalk, email } = configsByChannel;
  const history = await getNotificationHistory(env.DB, 30, env);
  const statusBadge = (status) => {
    const color = status === "sent" ? "#22c55e" : status === "deduped" ? "#38bdf8" : status === "skipped" ? "#94a3b8" : "#f59e0b";
    return `<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:999px;background:${color}22;color:${color};font-size:12px;font-weight:700">● ${escapeHtml(status)}</span>`;
  };
  const historyRows = history.map(item => `<tr><td>${escapeHtml(item.ts)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(item.eventType)}</td><td>${statusBadge(item.status)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(JSON.stringify(item.payload || {}).slice(0, 180))}</td></tr>`).join("");
  const checked = v => v ? "checked" : "";
  const selected = (a, b) => a === b ? "selected" : "";
  const has = v => String(v || "").trim() ? (lang === "en" ? "configured" : "已设置") : (lang === "en" ? "not set" : "未设置");
  const keep = v => String(v || "").trim() ? (lang === "en" ? "Configured; leave blank to keep current value" : "已设置，留空则保持当前值") : (lang === "en" ? "Not configured; enter a value to set" : "未设置，填写后保存");
  const clearLabel = lang === "en" ? "Clear saved value" : "清空已保存的值";
  const secretInput = (name, placeholder, current, clearName) => `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:4px 0 8px"><span class="muted" style="font-size:12px">${escapeHtml(has(current))}</span>${clearName ? `<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;margin:0;font-size:12px"><input type="checkbox" name="${escapeAttr(clearName)}" style="width:auto;margin:0"> ${escapeHtml(clearLabel)}</label>` : ""}</div><input name="${escapeAttr(name)}" type="password" value="" autocomplete="new-password" placeholder="${escapeAttr(placeholder || keep(current))}">`;
  const sensitiveUrl = (name, placeholder, current, clearName) => `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:4px 0 8px"><span class="muted" style="font-size:12px">${escapeHtml(has(current))}</span><label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;margin:0;font-size:12px"><input type="checkbox" name="${escapeAttr(clearName)}" style="width:auto;margin:0"> ${escapeHtml(clearLabel)}</label></div><input name="${escapeAttr(name)}" type="password" value="" autocomplete="new-password" placeholder="${escapeAttr(placeholder || keep(current))}">`;
  const eventBoxes = (prefix, cfg) => `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(180px,1fr));gap:8px;margin:8px 0 16px">${NOTIFICATION_EVENTS.filter(([key]) => key !== "test").map(([key, zh, en]) => `<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin:0;font-size:12px"><input type="checkbox" name="${prefix}_events" value="${key}" style="width:auto;margin:0" ${checked(!Array.isArray(cfg.events) || cfg.events.includes(key))}> ${escapeHtml(lang === "en" ? en : zh)}</label>`).join("")}</div>`;
  const channelLabel = { telegram: "Telegram", feishu: lang === "en" ? "Feishu/Lark" : "飞书/Lark", dingtalk: lang === "en" ? "DingTalk" : "钉钉", email: "Email" };
  const enabledBadge = v => `<span style="padding:3px 8px;border-radius:999px;font-size:12px;background:${v ? "rgba(34,197,94,.14);color:#86efac" : "rgba(148,163,184,.14);color:var(--muted)"}">${escapeHtml(v ? (lang === "en" ? "enabled" : "已启用") : (lang === "en" ? "disabled" : "未启用"))}</span>`;
  const matrixRows = NOTIFICATION_CHANNELS.map(ch => {
    const cfg = configsByChannel[ch] || {};
    const outboundReady = ch === "telegram" ? !!(cfg.botToken && cfg.chatId) : ch === "email" ? !!(cfg.provider && (cfg.apiKey || cfg.webhookUrl)) : !!cfg.webhookUrl;
    const signed = ch === "feishu" || ch === "dingtalk" ? !!cfg.secret : ch === "telegram" ? !!cfg.botToken : !!cfg.apiKey;
    const events = Array.isArray(cfg.events) ? cfg.events.length : NOTIFICATION_EVENTS.length - 1;
    return `<tr><td>${escapeHtml(channelLabel[ch])}</td><td>${enabledBadge(cfg.enabled)}</td><td>${outboundReady ? "✅" : "—"}</td><td>${signed ? "✅" : "—"}</td><td>${ch === "telegram" ? "✅" : "—"}</td><td>${events}</td><td><form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/notifications/test${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><input type="hidden" name="channel" value="${escapeAttr(ch)}"><button style="padding:8px 12px;font-size:12px">${lang === "en" ? "Test" : "测试"}</button></form></td></tr>`;
  }).join("");
  const body = `<div class="card"><h2>${lang === "en" ? "Channel Capability Matrix" : "渠道能力矩阵"}</h2><table><tr><th>${lang === "en" ? "Channel" : "渠道"}</th><th>${l("status")}</th><th>${lang === "en" ? "Outbound" : "出站通知"}</th><th>${lang === "en" ? "Signed / Secret" : "加签/密钥"}</th><th>${lang === "en" ? "Bot commands" : "Bot 命令"}</th><th>${lang === "en" ? "Events" : "订阅事件"}</th><th>${lang === "en" ? "Test" : "测试"}</th></tr>${matrixRows}</table></div><form class="card" method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/notifications/save${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><h2>${l("telegram")}</h2><label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" name="telegram_enabled" style="width:auto;margin:0" ${checked(telegram.enabled)}> ${l("enabled")}</label><label>${l("botToken")}</label>${secretInput("telegram_botToken", "1234567890:ABCDEF...", telegram.botToken, "telegram_botToken_clear")}<label>${l("chatId")}</label><input name="telegram_chatId" value="${escapeAttr(telegram.chatId || "")}" placeholder="123456789"><label>${lang === "en" ? "Subscribed events" : "订阅事件"}</label>${eventBoxes("telegram", telegram)}<h2>${l("feishu")}</h2><label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" name="feishu_enabled" style="width:auto;margin:0" ${checked(feishu.enabled)}> ${l("enabled")}</label><label>${l("webhookUrl")}</label>${sensitiveUrl("feishu_webhookUrl", "https://open.feishu.cn/open-apis/bot/v2/hook/...", feishu.webhookUrl, "feishu_webhookUrl_clear")}<label>${l("secret")}</label>${secretInput("feishu_secret", lang === "en" ? "Feishu/Lark bot signing secret" : "飞书/海外 Lark 机器人加签 Secret", feishu.secret, "feishu_secret_clear")}<label>${lang === "en" ? "Subscribed events" : "订阅事件"}</label>${eventBoxes("feishu", feishu)}<h2>${l("dingtalk")}</h2><label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" name="dingtalk_enabled" style="width:auto;margin:0" ${checked(dingtalk.enabled)}> ${l("enabled")}</label><label>${l("webhookUrl")}</label>${sensitiveUrl("dingtalk_webhookUrl", "https://oapi.dingtalk.com/robot/send?access_token=...", dingtalk.webhookUrl, "dingtalk_webhookUrl_clear")}<label>${l("secret")}</label>${secretInput("dingtalk_secret", "SECxxxx...", dingtalk.secret, "dingtalk_secret_clear")}<label>${lang === "en" ? "Subscribed events" : "订阅事件"}</label>${eventBoxes("dingtalk", dingtalk)}<h2>${l("email")}</h2><label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" name="email_enabled" style="width:auto;margin:0" ${checked(email.enabled)}> ${l("enabled")}</label><label>${l("provider")}</label><select name="email_provider"><option value="resend" ${selected(email.provider, "resend")}>Resend</option><option value="sendgrid" ${selected(email.provider, "sendgrid")}>SendGrid</option><option value="webhook" ${selected(email.provider, "webhook")}>${lang === "en" ? "Custom Webhook" : "自定义 Webhook"}</option></select><label>${l("apiKey")}</label>${secretInput("email_apiKey", "re_xxx / SG.xxx", email.apiKey, "email_apiKey_clear")}<label>${l("from")}</label><input name="email_from" value="${escapeAttr(email.from || "")}" placeholder="noreply@example.com"><label>${l("to")}</label><input name="email_to" value="${escapeAttr(email.to || "")}" placeholder="admin@example.com"><label>Webhook URL（provider=webhook）</label>${sensitiveUrl("email_webhookUrl", "https://example.com/webhook", email.webhookUrl, "email_webhookUrl_clear")}<label>${lang === "en" ? "Subscribed events" : "订阅事件"}</label>${eventBoxes("email", email)}<p class="muted" style="font-size:13px;margin:0 0 16px">${lang === "en" ? "Tokens, webhook URLs, API keys, and signing secrets are not shown after saving. Leave blank to keep the saved value." : "Token、Webhook URL、API Key 和加签 Secret 保存后不再回显；留空会保留已保存的值。"}</p><div style="display:flex;gap:12px;margin-top:8px;flex-wrap:wrap"><button>${l("saveNotifyConfig")}</button></div></form><form class="card" method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/notifications/test${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><h2>${l("sendTest")}</h2><p class="muted" style="margin-bottom:16px">${lang === "en" ? "Send a test notification to all enabled channels, or use the matrix above to test one channel." : "向所有已启用的渠道发送测试通知，也可以在上方矩阵中单独测试某个渠道。"}</p><button>${l("sendTest")}</button></form><div class="card"><h2>${l("notifyHistory")}</h2><p class="muted" style="font-size:13px;margin:0 0 12px">${lang === "en" ? "Duplicate noisy events are cooled down automatically. Status deduped means the event was recorded but not sent again." : "重复噪音事件会自动冷却去重。状态 deduped 表示事件已记录，但没有再次发送到消息渠道。"}</p><table><tr><th>${l("time")}</th><th>${l("event")}</th><th>${l("status")}</th><th>${l("content")}</th></tr>${historyRows || `<tr><td colspan="4" class="muted">${l("noHistory")}</td></tr>`}</table></div>`;
  return renderAdminLayout({ token, active: "notifications", title: l("notifications"), body, csrfToken: csrf, flash, favicon: siteFaviconHref(env), site: siteName(env), lang, request });
}

async function renderApiKeys(env, token, session, csrf, config, flash, request, createdKey = "") {
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const en = lang === "en";
  const keys = await listApiKeys(env);
  const rows = keys.map(k => `<tr><td><strong>${escapeHtml(k.name || "API Key")}</strong><br><code>${escapeHtml(k.key_prefix || "ow_")}••••</code></td><td>${escapeHtml(k.status || "active")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(k.minute_limit || DEFAULT_API_KEY_LIMITS.minute)}/${escapeHtml(k.hour_limit || DEFAULT_API_KEY_LIMITS.hour)}/${escapeHtml(k.day_limit || DEFAULT_API_KEY_LIMITS.day)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(k.last_used_at || "-")}</td><td><form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/api-keys/toggle${en ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><input type="hidden" name="id" value="${escapeAttr(k.id)}"><input type="hidden" name="status" value="${k.status === "active" ? "disabled" : "active"}"><button class="${k.status === "active" ? "warn" : ""}" style="padding:7px 12px;font-size:12px">${k.status === "active" ? (en ? "Disable" : "禁用") : (en ? "Enable" : "启用")}</button></form></td><td><form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/api-keys/delete${en ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><input type="hidden" name="id" value="${escapeAttr(k.id)}"><button class="danger" style="padding:7px 12px;font-size:12px">${en ? "Delete" : "删除"}</button></form></td></tr>`).join("");
  const createdBox = createdKey ? `<div class="flash"><strong>${en ? "Copy this API key now:" : "请立即复制这个 API Key："}</strong><br><code style="word-break:break-all">${escapeHtml(createdKey)}</code><p style="margin:8px 0 0">${en ? "It is only shown once and is not stored in plaintext." : "它只显示一次，系统不会保存明文。"}</p></div>` : "";
  const body = `${createdBox}<form class="card" method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/api-keys/create${en ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><h2>${en ? "Create API Key" : "创建 API Key"}</h2><label>${en ? "Name" : "名称"}</label><input name="name" placeholder="blog / client"><div class="grid"><div><label>${en ? "Per minute" : "每分钟"}</label><input name="minute" type="number" value="${DEFAULT_API_KEY_LIMITS.minute}"></div><div><label>${en ? "Per hour" : "每小时"}</label><input name="hour" type="number" value="${DEFAULT_API_KEY_LIMITS.hour}"></div><div><label>${en ? "Per day" : "每天"}</label><input name="day" type="number" value="${DEFAULT_API_KEY_LIMITS.day}"></div></div><button>${en ? "Create" : "创建"}</button><p class="muted">${en ? "Use Authorization: Bearer ow_xxx or X-API-Key. Plaintext keys are only shown once." : "支持 Authorization: Bearer ow_xxx 或 X-API-Key。明文 Key 只显示一次。"}</p></form><div class="card"><h2>${en ? "Keys" : "Key 列表"}</h2><table><tr><th>${en ? "Name" : "名称"}</th><th>${en ? "Status" : "状态"}</th><th>${en ? "Limits m/h/d" : "限额 分/时/天"}</th><th>${en ? "Last used" : "最近使用"}</th><th>${en ? "Toggle" : "启停"}</th><th>${en ? "Delete" : "删除"}</th></tr>${rows || `<tr><td colspan="6" class="muted">${en ? "No keys yet" : "暂无 Key"}</td></tr>`}</table></div>`;
  return renderAdminLayout({ token, active: "api-keys", title: en ? "API Keys" : "API Key 管理", body, csrfToken: csrf, flash, favicon: siteFaviconHref(env), site: siteName(env), lang, request });
}

  const lang = getLang(request);
  const l = (k) => _(k, lang);
  const typeOpt = v => config.bannerType === v ? "selected" : "";
  const bannerText = Array.isArray(config.bannerContent) ? config.bannerContent.join(" ||| ") : (config.bannerContent || "");
  const home = normalizeHomeConfig(config.home || DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG);
  const body = `<form class="card" method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/home-settings${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><h2>${lang === "en" ? Separate rotating titles by line or |||." : "无需改源码即可自定义首页。轮播标题可按行或用 ||| 分隔。"}</p><div class="grid"><div><label>中文标题轮播</label><textarea name="titlesZh" rows="4">${escapeHtml(home.titlesZh.join("\n"))}</textarea></div><div><label>English rotating titles</label><textarea name="titlesEn" rows="4">${escapeHtml(home.titlesEn.join("\n"))}</textarea></div></div><label>中文副标题</label><input name="heroZh" value="${escapeAttr(home.heroZh)}"><label>English subtitle</label><input name="heroEn" value="${escapeAttr(home.heroEn)}"><div class="grid"><div><label>中文随机按钮</label><input name="randomZh" value="${escapeAttr(home.randomZh)}"></div><div><label>English random button</label><input name="randomEn" value="${escapeAttr(home.randomEn)}"></div><div><label>中文生成器按钮</label><input name="builderZh" value="${escapeAttr(home.builderZh)}"></div><div><label>English builder button</label><input name="builderEn" value="${escapeAttr(home.builderEn)}"></div></div><div class="grid"><div><label>${lang === "en" ? "Daily pick keyword" : "每日精选关键词"}</label><input name="dailyQuery" value="${escapeAttr(home.dailyQuery)}"></div></div><button>${l("save")}</button></form><form class="card" method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/settings${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><h2>${lang === "en" ? "Site Notice" : "站点通知"}</h2><p class="muted" style="font-size:13px;margin:0 0 12px">${lang === "en" ? "Displayed as a slim notice bar on public pages. Use ||| to separate carousel messages." : "显示在用户页面顶部的细长通知条。用 ||| 分隔多条消息可轮播。"}</p><label>${l("bannerMarkdown")}</label><textarea name="bannerContent" rows="6" placeholder="${lang === "en" ? "Use ||| to separate multiple messages for carousel" : "用 ||| 分隔多条消息，实现轮播效果"}">${escapeHtml(bannerText)}</textarea><label>${l("bannerMode")}</label><select name="bannerType"><option value="fixed" ${typeOpt("fixed")}>${l("fixed")}</option><option value="dismissible" ${typeOpt("dismissible")}>${l("dismissible")}</option><option value="toast" ${typeOpt("toast")}>${l("toast")}</option></select><button>${l("save")}</button></form><form class="card" method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/logs/clear${lang === "en" ? "Clear all error logs stored in D1. Image cache settings are now in Images / Cache; security options are in Security." : "清空 D1 中存储的错误日志。图片缓存设置已移到“图片管理”，安全相关设置已移到“安全设置”。"}</p><button class="danger">${l("clearLogs")}</button></form>`;
  return renderAdminLayout({ token, active: "settings", title: l("settings"), body, csrfToken: csrf, flash, favicon: siteFaviconHref(env), site: siteName(env), lang, request });
