async function renderFeedbackManagement(env, token, session, csrf, config, flash, request) {
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const l = (k) => _(k, lang);
  const statusFilter = new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("status") || "all";
  const feedbackList = statusFilter === "all" ? await getFeedbackList(env.DB, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, 100, null, env) : await getFeedbackList(env.DB, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, 100, statusFilter, env);
  const typeMap = { bug: l("feedbackBug"), security: l("feedbackSecurity"), feature: l("feedbackFeature"), other: l("feedbackOther") };
  const statusMap = { pending: l("feedbackPending"), in_progress: l("feedbackInProgress"), resolved: l("feedbackResolved"), closed: l("feedbackClosed") };
  const severityBadge = (sev) => `<span class="severity-badge sev-${sev}">${["Low", "Low", "Medium", "High", "Critical", "Critical"][sev] || "N/A"}</span>`;
  const rows = feedbackList.map(f => {
    const excerpt = escapeHtml(f.content.slice(0, 80)) + (f.content.length > 80 ? "..." : "");
    return `<tr><td><strong>${escapeHtml(typeMap[f.type] || f.type)}</strong>${severityBadge(f.severity)}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(f.title)}</strong><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:var(--muted)">${excerpt}</span></td><td>${escapeHtml(statusMap[f.status] || f.status)}</td><td style="white-space:nowrap">${escapeHtml(f.created_at || "-")}</td><td><a href="/${escapeAttr(token)}/feedback/${f.id}${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}" style="color:var(--brand);text-decoration:none;font-size:13px">${lang === "en" ? "View" : "查看"} →</a></td></tr>`;
  }).join("");
  const filterLinks = ["all", "pending", "in_progress", "resolved", "closed"].map(s => {
    const label = s === "all" ? (lang === "en" ? "All" : "全部") : (statusMap[s] || s);
    const active = statusFilter === s ? ' style="background:var(--brand);color:#fff"' : "";
    return `<a href="/${escapeAttr(token)}/feedback-management?status=${s}${lang === "en" ? "&lang=en" : ""}"${active}>${label}</a>`;
  }).join(" ");
  const body = `<div style="margin-bottom:20px;display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">${filterLinks}</div><div class="card"><h2>${l("feedbackManagement")}</h2><table><tr><th>${l("feedbackType")}</th><th>${l("feedbackTitle")}</th><th>${l("feedbackStatus")}</th><th>${l("feedbackCreatedAt")}</th><th>${lang === "en" ? "Action" : "操作"}</th></tr>${rows || `<tr><td colspan="5" class="muted">${l("noFeedback")}</td></tr>`}</table></div>`;
  return renderAdminLayout({ token, active: "feedback-management", title: l("feedbackManagement"), body, csrfToken: csrf, flash, favicon: siteFaviconHref(env), site: siteName(env), lang, request });
}

async function renderFeedbackDetail(env, token, session, csrf, config, flash, request, feedbackId) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const l = (k) => _(k, lang);
  const feedback = await getFeedbackById(env.DB, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, parseInt(feedbackId, 10));
  if (!feedback) return renderAdminErrorPage({ request, token, status: 404, title: lang === "en" ? "Feedback not found" : "反馈不存在", message: lang === "en" ? "This feedback item may have been deleted or the link is invalid." : "这条反馈可能已被删除，或当前链接无效。", actionText: lang === "en" ? "Back to feedback" : "返回反馈列表", actionHref: `/${token}/feedback-management${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}`, site });
  const typeMap = { bug: l("feedbackBug"), security: l("feedbackSecurity"), feature: l("feedbackFeature"), other: l("feedbackOther") };
  const statusMap = { pending: l("feedbackPending"), in_progress: l("feedbackInProgress"), resolved: l("feedbackResolved"), closed: l("feedbackClosed") };
  const severityBadge = (sev) => `<span class="severity-badge sev-${sev}">${["Low", "Low", "Medium", "High", "Critical", "Critical"][sev] || "N/A"}</span>`;
  const langQs = lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : "";
  const body = `<div class="card"><a href="/${escapeAttr(token)}/feedback-management${langQs}" style="color:var(--brand);text-decoration:none">← ${lang === "en" ? "Back to List" : "返回列表"}</a><h2 style="margin-top:16px">${l("feedbackDetail")}</h2><div style="margin:20px 0;padding:16px;background:rgba(0,0,0,.2);border-radius:12px;border:1px solid var(--line)"><div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(200px,1fr));gap:16px;margin-bottom:16px"><div><strong style="color:var(--muted);font-size:13px">${l("feedbackType")}</strong><div>${escapeHtml(typeMap[feedback.type] || feedback.type)}${severityBadge(feedback.severity)}</div></div><div><strong style="color:var(--muted);font-size:13px">${l("feedbackStatus")}</strong><div>${escapeHtml(statusMap[feedback.status] || feedback.status)}</div></div><div><strong style="color:var(--muted);font-size:13px">${l("feedbackCreatedAt")}</strong><div>${escapeHtml(feedback.created_at || "-")}</div></div></div><div style="margin-bottom:16px"><strong style="color:var(--muted);font-size:13px">${l("feedbackTitle")}</strong><div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:600;margin-top:4px">${escapeHtml(feedback.title)}</div></div><div style="margin-bottom:16px"><strong style="color:var(--muted);font-size:13px">${l("feedbackContent")}</strong><div style="margin-top:4px;white-space:pre-wrap;line-height:1.6">${escapeHtml(feedback.content)}</div></div>${feedback.contact ? `<div style="margin-bottom:16px"><strong style="color:var(--muted);font-size:13px">${l("feedbackContact")}</strong><div style="margin-top:4px">${escapeHtml(feedback.contact)}</div></div>` : ""}<div><strong style="color:var(--muted);font-size:13px">IP Hash</strong><div style="font-family:monospace;font-size:13px;margin-top:4px">${escapeHtml(String(feedback.ip_hash || "").slice(0, 16))}</div></div></div><form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/feedback/update${langQs}" class="card" style="margin-top:20px"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><input type="hidden" name="id" value="${feedback.id}"><h3>${lang === "en" ? "Update Status" : "更新状态"}</h3><label>${l("feedbackStatus")}</label><select name="status" required><option value="pending" ${feedback.status === "pending" ? "selected" : ""}>${l("feedbackPending")}</option><option value="in_progress" ${feedback.status === "in_progress" ? "selected" : ""}>${l("feedbackInProgress")}</option><option value="resolved" ${feedback.status === "resolved" ? "selected" : ""}>${l("feedbackResolved")}</option><option value="closed" ${feedback.status === "closed" ? "selected" : ""}>${l("feedbackClosed")}</option></select><label>${l("adminNote")}</label><textarea name="admin_note" rows="3" placeholder="${lang === "en" ? "Internal notes..." : "内部备注..."}">${escapeHtml(feedback.admin_note || "")}</textarea><button>${lang === "en" ? "Update" : "更新"}</button></form></div>`;
  return renderAdminLayout({ token, active: "feedback-management", title: l("feedbackDetail"), body, csrfToken: csrf, flash, favicon: siteFaviconHref(env), site: siteName(env), lang, request });
}

async function renderAccount(env, token, session, csrf, config, flash, request) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const l = (k) => _(k, lang);
  const username = await getAdminUsername(env.DB, env);
  const totp = await getTotpState(env.DB, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, env);
  let setupSecret;
  try { const r = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM admin_config WHERE key = 'totp_setup_secret'").first(); setupSecret = r?.value; } catch (_) {}
  if (!totp.enabled && !setupSecret) {
    setupSecret = generateTotpSecret();
    try { await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO admin_config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES ('totp_setup_secret', ?, datetime('now'))").bind(setupSecret).run(); } catch (_) {}
  }
  const issuer = site;
  const otpauth = setupSecret ? `otpauth://totp/${encodeURIComponent(issuer)}:${encodeURIComponent(username)}?secret=${encodeURIComponent(setupSecret)}&issuer=${encodeURIComponent(issuer)}&algorithm=SHA1&digits=6&period=30` : "";
  const qrUrl = otpauth ? `https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=180x180&data=${encodeURIComponent(otpauth)}` : "";
  const totpStatusBadge = totp.enabled
    ? `<span style="display:inline-block;padding:4px 12px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(34,197,94,.15);color:#22c55e;font-size:13px;font-weight:600">${l("totpEnabled")}</span>`
    : `<span style="display:inline-block;padding:4px 12px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(148,163,184,.15);color:var(--muted);font-size:13px;font-weight:600">${l("totpDisabled")}</span>`;
  const body = `<form class="card" method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/account/username${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><h2>${l("account")}</h2><label>${l("username")}</label><input name="username" value="${escapeAttr(username)}"><button>${l("changeUsername")}</button></form>
<div class="card"><h2>${l("passwordInfo")}</h2><p class="muted">${l("passwordDesc")}</p><p style="margin-top:16px">${l("currentUser")}：<strong>${escapeHtml(session.sub)}</strong></p></div>
<div class="card"><h2>${l("totp")}</h2><p style="margin-bottom:20px">${lang === "en" ? "Status" : "状态"}：${totpStatusBadge}</p>${!totp.enabled ? `<div style="background:rgba(0,0,0,.25);border-radius:16px;padding:24px;text-align:center;margin-bottom:20px"><p class="muted">${l("totpScan")}</p><img src="${escapeAttr(qrUrl)}" alt="TOTP QR" style="background:#fff;padding:12px;border-radius:16px;margin:16px 0;box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.3)"><div style="font-family:ui-monospace,monospace;font-size:14px;background:rgba(0,0,0,.3);padding:10px 16px;border-radius:10px;display:inline-block;color:#c4b5fd">${escapeHtml(setupSecret)}</div></div><form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/account/totp-enable${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><label>${l("verifyCode")}</label><input name="totp" inputmode="numeric" placeholder="000000" maxlength="6"><button>${l("enableTotp")}</button></form>` : `<form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/account/totp-disable${lang === "en" ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><label>${l("verifyCode")}</label><input name="totp" inputmode="numeric" placeholder="000000" maxlength="6"><button class="danger">${l("disableTotp")}</button></form>`}</div>`;
  return renderAdminLayout({ token, active: "account", title: l("account"), body, csrfToken: csrf, flash, favicon: siteFaviconHref(env), site: siteName(env), lang, request });
}

async function renderMonitor(env, token, session, csrf, config, flash, request) {
  const lang = getLang(request);
  const l = (k) => _(k, lang);
  const en = lang === "en";
  const routing = { ...DEFAULT_SOURCE_ROUTING, ...(config.sourceRouting || {}) };
  const orderValue = (routing.priority || DEFAULT_SOURCE_ROUTING.priority).join(",");
  const checked = v => v ? "checked" : "";
  const body = `<div class="card"><h2>${l("monitor")}</h2><p class="muted" style="font-size:13px;margin:0 0 16px">${en ? "Each image source is pinged every 5 minutes (via Cron) and on first view. Random routing can use these health samples to prefer faster healthy sources." : "每个图源每 5 分钟由 Cron 检测一次（首次访问也会触发）。混合随机路由可根据这些健康采样优先选择更快、更健康的图源。"}</p><form method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/monitor/ping${en ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><button>${en ? "Ping Now" : "立即检测"}</button></form></div>
<form class="card" method="POST" action="/${escapeAttr(token)}/api/monitor/routing${en ? "?lang=en" : ""}"><input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="${escapeAttr(csrf)}"><h2>${en ? "Health-aware routing" : "健康路由"}</h2><p class="muted" style="font-size:13px;margin:0 0 12px">${en ? "Set source priority for source=random. Use comma-separated source names." : "设置 source=random 时的图源优先级。用英文逗号分隔图源名称。"}</p><label>${en ? "Priority" : "优先级"}</label><input name="priority" value="${escapeAttr(orderValue)}" placeholder="unsplash,pexels,pixabay,bing"><label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" name="healthAware" style="width:auto;margin:0" ${checked(routing.healthAware !== false)}> ${en ? "Prefer healthy / low-latency sources" : "优先健康、低延迟图源"}</label><label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" name="skipUnhealthy" style="width:auto;margin:0" ${checked(routing.skipUnhealthy)}> ${en ? "Skip currently unhealthy sources when possible" : "尽量跳过当前异常图源"}</label><label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" name="quotaAware" style="width:auto;margin:0" ${checked(routing.quotaAware !== false)}> ${en ? "Prefer sources with more quota remaining" : "优先剩余额度更多的图源"}</label><label>${en ? "Failure cooldown seconds" : "失败冷却秒数"}</label><input name="failureCooldownSeconds" type="number" value="${escapeAttr(routing.failureCooldownSeconds || DEFAULT_SOURCE_ROUTING.failureCooldownSeconds)}"><button>${l("save")}</button></form>
<div class="card">${monitorWidget("adminMonitor", lang)}</div>`;
  return renderAdminLayout({ token, active: "monitor", title: l("monitor"), body, csrfToken: csrf, flash, favicon: siteFaviconHref(env), site: siteName(env), lang, request });
}

async function handleAdminApi(request, env, ctx, token, session) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const form = await request.formData();
  const csrf = String(form.get("csrf") || request.headers.get("X-CSRF-Token") || "");
  if (!await verifyCsrfToken(session, csrf, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD)) return renderAdminErrorPage({ request, token, status: 403, title: "页面已过期", message: "安全校验没有通过，通常是页面停留太久或重复提交导致。", detail: "请返回后台刷新页面后再试。", actionText: "返回后台", actionHref: `/${token}/dashboard`, site });
  const path = new URL(request.url).pathname;
  let msg = "操作成功";
  invalidateRuntimeConfigCache();
  if (path.endsWith("/api/home-settings")) {
    const homeConfig = normalizeHomeConfig({
      titlesZh: splitLines(form.get("titlesZh"), DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.titlesZh, 6),
      titlesEn: splitLines(form.get("titlesEn"), DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.titlesEn, 6),
      heroZh: form.get("heroZh"), heroEn: form.get("heroEn"),
      randomZh: form.get("randomZh"), randomEn: form.get("randomEn"),
      builderZh: form.get("builderZh"), builderEn: form.get("builderEn"),
      docsZh: DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.docsZh, docsEn: DEFAULT_HOME_CONFIG.docsEn,
      backgroundQuery: form.get("backgroundQuery"), dailyQuery: form.get("dailyQuery"),
    });
    await d1SetConfig(env, KV.CONFIG_HOME, JSON.stringify(homeConfig));
    msg = "首页内容已保存";
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/settings")) {
    const banner = String(form.get("bannerContent") || "").slice(0, 2000);
    const bannerCheck = detectDangerousInput(banner);
    if (!bannerCheck.ok) return redirectResponse(`/${token}/settings?msg=${encodeURIComponent("公告内容包含不安全输入，请修改后再保存")}`, "no-store");
    await d1SetConfig(env, KV.CONFIG_BANNER_CONTENT, banner);
    await d1SetConfig(env, KV.CONFIG_BANNER_TYPE, String(form.get("bannerType") || "fixed"));
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/cache/settings")) {
    const ttl = Math.max(60, (parseInt(form.get("cacheTime"), 10) || 1) * (parseInt(form.get("cacheUnit"), 10) || 1));
    await d1SetConfig(env, KV.CONFIG_CACHE_TTL, ttl);
    await d1SetConfig(env, KV.CONFIG_CUSTOM_BG, safeUrl(form.get("customBg"), ""));
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/security/turnstile")) {
    const turnstileConfig = {
      enabled: form.get("turnstile_enabled") === "on",
      protectFeedback: form.get("turnstile_feedback") === "on",
      protectAdminLogin: form.get("turnstile_admin_login") === "on",
      protectContentCheck: form.get("turnstile_check_content") === "on",
    };
    await d1SetConfig(env, KV.CONFIG_TURNSTILE, JSON.stringify(turnstileConfig));
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/security/bg-access")) {
    const banMin = Math.max(60, parseInt(form.get("bg_ban_min"), 10) || DEFAULT_BG_ACCESS.banMinSeconds);
    const banMax = Math.max(banMin, parseInt(form.get("bg_ban_max"), 10) || DEFAULT_BG_ACCESS.banMaxSeconds);
    const bgAccessConfig = {
      enabled: form.get("bg_access_enabled") === "on",
      ttlSeconds: Math.max(60, parseInt(form.get("bg_ttl"), 10) || DEFAULT_BG_ACCESS.ttlSeconds),
      banMinSeconds: banMin,
      banMaxSeconds: banMax,
      strictHotlinkBan: form.get("bg_strict_hotlink_ban") === "on",
    };
    await d1SetConfig(env, KV.CONFIG_BG_ACCESS, JSON.stringify(bgAccessConfig));
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/cache/clear")) {
    const r = await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM image_cache").run();
    msg = `已清理 ${Number(r.meta?.changes || 0)} 个缓存键`;
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/cache/clear-source")) {
    const source = String(form.get("source") || "");
    const pattern = source === "bing" ? "cache:bing_daily:%" : `cache:${source}:%`;
    const r = await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM image_cache WHERE cache_key LIKE ?").bind(pattern).run();
    msg = `已清理 ${Number(r.meta?.changes || 0)} 个 ${source} 缓存`;
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/logs/clear")) {
    await clearLogs(env.DB, env);
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/monitor/ping")) {
    ctx.waitUntil(pingSources(env));
    msg = "已在后台触发图源检测，稍候刷新查看";
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/monitor/routing")) {
    const priority = String(form.get("priority") || "").split(",").map(s => s.trim().toLowerCase()).filter(s => SOURCES.includes(s));
    const routingConfig = { priority: priority.length ? priority : DEFAULT_SOURCE_ROUTING.priority, healthAware: form.get("healthAware") === "on", skipUnhealthy: form.get("skipUnhealthy") === "on", quotaAware: form.get("quotaAware") === "on", failureCooldownSeconds: Math.max(60, parseInt(form.get("failureCooldownSeconds"), 10) || DEFAULT_SOURCE_ROUTING.failureCooldownSeconds) };
    await d1SetConfig(env, KV.CONFIG_SOURCE_ROUTING, JSON.stringify(routingConfig));
    msg = "图源路由策略已保存";
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/presets/add")) {
    const slug = normalizeSlug(form.get("slug") || form.get("name"));
    const name = String(form.get("name") || slug).trim().slice(0, 120);
    const params = { query: normalizeQuery(form.get("query") || "wallpaper"), orientation: normalizeOrientation(form.get("orientation")), source: String(form.get("source") || "random").toLowerCase(), color: normalizeColor(form.get("color")), expand: true };
    if (!slug || !name) return redirectResponse(`/${token}/presets?msg=${encodeURIComponent("Slug 和名称不能为空")}`, "no-store");
    await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO presets (slug, name, description, params_json, is_public, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 1, datetime('now'))").bind(slug, name, String(form.get("description") || "").slice(0, 300), JSON.stringify(params)).run();
    msg = "预设已保存";
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/presets/delete")) {
    const slug = normalizeSlug(form.get("slug"));
    if (slug && !BUILTIN_PRESETS.some(p => p.slug === slug)) await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM presets WHERE slug = ?").bind(slug).run();
    msg = "预设已删除";
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/api-keys/create")) {
    const key = await createApiKey(env, form.get("name"), { minute: Math.max(1, parseInt(form.get("minute"), 10) || DEFAULT_API_KEY_LIMITS.minute), hour: Math.max(1, parseInt(form.get("hour"), 10) || DEFAULT_API_KEY_LIMITS.hour), day: Math.max(1, parseInt(form.get("day"), 10) || DEFAULT_API_KEY_LIMITS.day) });
    return renderApiKeys(env, token, session, csrf, config, "", request, key);
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/api-keys/toggle")) {
    const status = String(form.get("status") || "disabled") === "active" ? "active" : "disabled";
    await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE api_keys SET status = ?, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?").bind(status, parseInt(form.get("id"), 10)).run();
    msg = "API Key 已更新";
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/api-keys/delete")) {
    await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM api_keys WHERE id = ?").bind(parseInt(form.get("id"), 10)).run();
    msg = "API Key 已删除";
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/security/rate-limits")) {
    const rl = {
      minute: Math.max(1, parseInt(form.get("minute"), 10) || DEFAULT_RATE_LIMITS.minute),
      hour: Math.max(1, parseInt(form.get("hour"), 10) || DEFAULT_RATE_LIMITS.hour),
      day: Math.max(1, parseInt(form.get("day"), 10) || DEFAULT_RATE_LIMITS.day),
    };
    await d1SetConfig(env, KV.CONFIG_RATE_LIMITS, JSON.stringify(rl));
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/security/ban")) {
    const ip = String(form.get("ip") || "").trim();
    const seconds = Math.max(60, parseInt(form.get("seconds"), 10) || 3600);
    const ipHash = await hashIdentifier(ip, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD);
    await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO bans (ip_hash, reason, level, created_at, expires_at) VALUES (?, ?, 1, ?, ?)").bind(ipHash, String(form.get("reason") || "manual"), nowIso(), new Date(Date.now() + seconds * 1000).toISOString()).run();
    ctx.waitUntil(sendNotification("ip_banned", { ip: ipHash.slice(0, 16), reason: form.get("reason") || "manual", seconds }, env));
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/security/unban")) {
    const key = String(form.get("key") || "");
    if (key.startsWith("ban:")) {
      await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM bans WHERE ip_hash = ?").bind(key.replace("ban:", "")).run();
    }
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/security/keywords/add")) {
    const word = String(form.get("word") || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (word) {
      try { await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO keywords (word, reason) VALUES (?, ?)").bind(word, String(form.get("reason") || "").trim()).run(); } catch (_) {}
    }
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/security/keywords/delete")) {
    const idx = parseInt(form.get("index"), 10);
    try {
      const rows = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id FROM keywords ORDER BY id ASC").all();
      if (rows.results && rows.results[idx]) await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM keywords WHERE id = ?").bind(rows.results[idx].id).run();
    } catch (_) {}
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/security/whitelist/add")) {
    const wlIp = String(form.get("ip") || "").trim();
    if (wlIp) {
      const wlHash = await hashIdentifier(wlIp, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "fallback");
      try { await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO whitelist (ip_hash, ip, added_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)").bind(wlHash, wlIp, nowIso()).run(); } catch (_) {}
    }
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/updates/add")) {
    await env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO changelog (date, version, title, content) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)").bind(String(form.get("date") || todayUtc8()), String(form.get("version") || ""), String(form.get("title") || ""), String(form.get("content") || "")).run();
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/updates/delete")) {
    const uIdx = parseInt(form.get("index"), 10);
    const rows = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id FROM changelog ORDER BY id DESC").all();
    if (rows.results && rows.results[uIdx]) await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM changelog WHERE id = ?").bind(rows.results[uIdx].id).run();
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/notifications/save")) {
    const [oldTelegram, oldFeishu, oldDingTalk, oldEmail] = await Promise.all([
      getEncryptedJson(env.DB, "notify:telegram", env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, {}, env),
      getEncryptedJson(env.DB, "notify:feishu", env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, {}, env),
      getEncryptedJson(env.DB, "notify:dingtalk", env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, {}, env),
      getEncryptedJson(env.DB, "notify:email", env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, {}, env),
    ]);
    await setEncryptedJson(env.DB, "notify:telegram", { enabled: form.get("telegram_enabled") === "on", botToken: keepOrUpdateSecret(form, "telegram_botToken", oldTelegram.botToken), chatId: String(form.get("telegram_chatId") || ""), events: readCheckedValues(form, "telegram_events") }, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, env);
    await setEncryptedJson(env.DB, "notify:feishu", { enabled: form.get("feishu_enabled") === "on", webhookUrl: keepOrUpdateUrl(form, "feishu_webhookUrl", oldFeishu.webhookUrl, "feishu"), secret: keepOrUpdateSecret(form, "feishu_secret", oldFeishu.secret), events: readCheckedValues(form, "feishu_events") }, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, env);
    await setEncryptedJson(env.DB, "notify:dingtalk", { enabled: form.get("dingtalk_enabled") === "on", webhookUrl: keepOrUpdateUrl(form, "dingtalk_webhookUrl", oldDingTalk.webhookUrl, "dingtalk"), secret: keepOrUpdateSecret(form, "dingtalk_secret", oldDingTalk.secret), events: readCheckedValues(form, "dingtalk_events") }, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, env);
    await setEncryptedJson(env.DB, "notify:email", { enabled: form.get("email_enabled") === "on", provider: String(form.get("email_provider") || "resend"), apiKey: keepOrUpdateSecret(form, "email_apiKey", oldEmail.apiKey), from: String(form.get("email_from") || ""), to: String(form.get("email_to") || ""), webhookUrl: keepOrUpdateUrl(form, "email_webhookUrl", oldEmail.webhookUrl, "webhook"), events: readCheckedValues(form, "email_events") }, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, env);
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/notifications/test")) {
    const channel = String(form.get("channel") || "").trim();
    const channels = NOTIFICATION_CHANNELS.includes(channel) ? [channel] : null;
    await sendNotification("test", { time: nowIso(), message: "Multi-Source Image Host notification test", target: channel || "all" }, env, { channels, force: true });
    msg = channel ? `测试通知已发送到 ${channel}` : "测试通知已发送（如渠道已启用）";
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/account/username")) {
    const newUser = String(form.get("username") || "admin").trim() || "admin";
    await d1SetConfig(env, KV.CONFIG_ADMIN_USERNAME, newUser);
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/account/totp-enable")) {
    let setupSecret;
    try { const r = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM admin_config WHERE key = 'totp_setup_secret'").first(); setupSecret = r?.value; } catch (_) {}
    if (!setupSecret || !await verifyTotp(form.get("totp"), setupSecret)) return redirectResponse(`/${token}/account?msg=${encodeURIComponent("TOTP 验证码错误，请检查后重试")}`, "no-store");
    await setTotpState(env.DB, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, true, setupSecret, env);
    try { await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM admin_config WHERE key = 'totp_setup_secret'").run(); } catch (_) {}
    ctx.waitUntil(sendNotification("totp_enabled", { user: session.sub, time: nowIso() }, env));
    msg = "TOTP 已启用";
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/account/totp-disable")) {
    const totp = await getTotpState(env.DB, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, env);
    if (!totp.enabled || !await verifyTotp(form.get("totp"), totp.secret)) return redirectResponse(`/${token}/account?msg=${encodeURIComponent("TOTP 验证码错误，请检查后重试")}`, "no-store");
    await setTotpState(env.DB, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, false, "", env);
    msg = "TOTP 已禁用";
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/feedback/update")) {
    const lang = getLang(request);
    const id = parseInt(form.get("id"), 10);
    const status = String(form.get("status") || "pending");
    const adminNote = String(form.get("admin_note") || "").slice(0, 1000);
    const langQs = lang === "en" ? "&lang=en" : "";
    if (!id || !["pending", "in_progress", "resolved", "closed"].includes(status)) {
      return redirectResponse(`/${token}/feedback-management?msg=${encodeURIComponent("Invalid parameters")}${langQs}`, "no-store");
    }
    const result = await updateFeedbackStatus(env.DB, id, status, adminNote, env);
    if (result.ok) {
      msg = lang === "en" ? "Feedback updated" : "反馈已更新";
      return redirectResponse(`/${token}/feedback/${id}?msg=${encodeURIComponent(msg)}${langQs}`, "no-store");
    }
    return redirectResponse(`/${token}/feedback/${id}?msg=${encodeURIComponent(result.error || "Update failed")}${langQs}`, "no-store");
  } else if (path.endsWith("/api/logout")) {
    return redirectResponse(`/${token}/login`, "no-store", { "Set-Cookie": clearSessionCookieHeader() });
  } else {
    return renderAdminErrorPage({ request, token, status: 404, title: "未知后台操作", message: "这个后台操作不存在，或当前版本不支持。", actionText: "返回后台", actionHref: `/${token}/dashboard`, site });
  }
  // Precise API path → admin page redirect mapping (avoids false matches from includes()).
  const redirectMap = [
    [/\/api\/api-keys\//, "api-keys"],
    [/\/api\/home-settings/, "settings"],
    [/\/api\/monitor\//, "monitor"],
    [/\/api\/presets\//, "presets"],
    [/\/api\/security\/keywords\/|\/api\/moderation\//, "moderation"],
    [/\/api\/security\//, "security"],
    [/\/api\/notifications\//, "notifications"],
    [/\/api\/account\//, "account"],
    [/\/api\/updates\//, "updates"],
    [/\/api\/cache\/settings/, "images"],
    [/\/api\/settings$|\/api\/logs\//, "settings"],
  ];
  const target = redirectMap.find(([re]) => re.test(path))?.[1] || "images";
  return redirectResponse(`/${token}/${target}?msg=${encodeURIComponent(msg)}`, "no-store");
}

async function handleAdminRoute(request, env, ctx, config) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  const parts = url.pathname.split("/").filter(Boolean);
  if (parts.length < 2) return null;
  const [token, page] = parts;
  if (page === "login" && request.method === "GET") return renderLogin(request, env, ctx, token, "", config);
  if (page === "auth" && request.method === "POST") return handleAuth(request, env, ctx, token, config);
  if (page !== "api" && !["dashboard", "analytics", "images", "presets", "security", "moderation", "api-keys", "notifications", "settings", "updates", "account", "monitor", "feedback", "feedback-management"].includes(page)) return null;
  const auth = await requireAdmin(request, env, ctx, token);
  if (auth.status === 403) return renderAdminErrorPage({ request, token, status: 403, title: "后台入口已过期", message: "这个管理入口已经过期或无效。", detail: "请通过 Telegram Bot 发送 /admin 命令获取今日有效入口。", actionText: "返回首页", actionHref: "/", site });
  if (!auth.ok) return redirectResponse(`/${token}/login`, "no-store");
  if (page === "api" && request.method === "POST") return handleAdminApi(request, env, ctx, token, auth.session);
  const csrf = await makeCsrfToken(auth.session, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD);
  const flash = url.searchParams.get("msg") || "";
  if (page === "dashboard") return renderDashboard(env, token, auth.session, csrf, config, flash, request);
  if (page === "analytics") return renderAnalytics(env, token, auth.session, csrf, config, flash, request);
  if (page === "images") return renderImages(env, token, auth.session, csrf, config, flash, request);
  if (page === "presets") return renderPresets(env, token, auth.session, csrf, config, flash, request);
  if (page === "security") return renderSecurity(env, token, auth.session, csrf, config, flash, request);
  if (page === "moderation") return renderModeration(env, token, auth.session, csrf, config, flash, request);
  if (page === "api-keys") return renderApiKeys(env, token, auth.session, csrf, config, flash, request);
  if (page === "notifications") return renderNotifications(env, token, auth.session, csrf, config, flash, request);
  if (page === "settings") return renderSettings(env, token, auth.session, csrf, config, flash, request);
  if (page === "updates") return renderUpdates(env, token, auth.session, csrf, config, flash, request);
  if (page === "account") return renderAccount(env, token, auth.session, csrf, config, flash, request);
  if (page === "feedback-management") return renderFeedbackManagement(env, token, auth.session, csrf, config, flash, request);
  if (page === "feedback" && parts[2]) return renderFeedbackDetail(env, token, auth.session, csrf, config, flash, request, parts[2]);
  if (page === "monitor") return renderMonitor(env, token, auth.session, csrf, config, flash, request);
  return renderAdminErrorPage({ request, token, status: 405, title: "请求方式不支持", message: "当前后台页面不支持这个请求方式。", detail: "请从后台页面内的按钮或表单重新操作。", actionText: "返回后台", actionHref: `/${token}/dashboard`, site });
}

function handleOptions() {
  return new Response(null, { headers: { "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*", "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET,POST,OPTIONS", "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "content-type, authorization, x-api-key" } });
}

async function buildBotCommandReply(text, request, env, ctx) {
  const site = siteName(env);
  const cmd = String(text || "").trim().split(" ")[0].split("@")[0];
  if (cmd === "/admin" || cmd === "/today" || cmd === "/path") {
    const daily = await getDailyAdminPath(env.DB, env, ctx, request);
    const origin = getPublicOrigin(request, env);
    return `🔑 今日管理入口\n${buildAdminUrl(origin, daily)}\n有效期至 ${tomorrowCstDateText()} 00:00 CST`;
  }
  if (cmd === "/status" || cmd === "/quota") {
    const rows = await getQuotaRows(env.DB, env);
    return "📊 API 配额\n" + rows.map(q => `${q.source}: ${q.used ?? "-"}/${q.limit ?? "-"} (${q.remaining ?? "-"} 剩余)`).join("\n");
  }
  if (cmd === "/daily") {
    const origin = getPublicOrigin(request, env);
    const parts = String(text || "").trim().split(/\s+/);
    const orientation = normalizeOrientation(parts[1] || "landscape");
    const result = await getDailyImage(env, ctx, await loadRuntimeConfig(env.DB, env), { orientation });
    if (!result.ok) return `❌ 每日精选暂不可用：${result.error || "unknown"}`;
    const img = result.image || {};
    return `🌅 每日精选 ${result.date}\n${img.title || "Daily Image"}\n${img.author ? "作者：" + img.author + "\n" : ""}${origin}/api/daily?orientation=${encodeURIComponent(result.orientation)}\n原图：${img.url}`;
  }
  if (cmd === "/random") {
    const origin = getPublicOrigin(request, env);
    const query = String(text || "").trim().split(/\s+/).slice(1).join(" ").trim() || "wallpaper";
    const cfg = await loadRuntimeConfig(env.DB, env);
    const params = { query: normalizeQuery(query), orientation: "landscape", source: "random", color: "", minWidth: 0, minHeight: 0, aspectRatio: "", safeMode: true, expand: true, forceReload: false, hasExplicitQuery: true, quality: "high" };
    const result = await fetchWithFallback(params, env, ctx, cfg);
    if (!result.ok) return `❌ 随机图片暂不可用：${result.error || "unknown"}`;
    const img = result.image || {};
    const api = `${origin}/api/random?query=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&quality=high`;
    return `🎲 随机图片\n关键词：${query}\n${img.title || "Image"}\n${img.author ? "作者：" + img.author + "\n" : ""}${api}\n原图：${img.url}`;
  }
  if (cmd === "/reset") {
    const totpState = await getTotpState(env.DB, env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, env);
    if (!totpState.enabled) return "❌ TOTP 未启用，无法通过 Bot 重置入口。请直接登录后台操作。";
    const parts = String(text || "").trim().split(/\s+/);
    const code = parts[1] || "";
    if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(code)) return "⚠️ 用法：/reset 123456\n请输入 6 位 TOTP 验证码以重置当日管理入口。";
    if (!await verifyTotp(code, totpState.secret)) return "❌ TOTP 验证码错误，请检查后重试。";
    const newPath = await rotateAdminPathAfterFirstLogin(env.DB, env, ctx, request);
    const origin = getPublicOrigin(request, env);
    return `✅ 管理入口已重置\n🔑 新入口：${buildAdminUrl(origin, newPath)}\n有效期至 ${tomorrowCstDateText()} 00:00 CST`;
  }
  if (cmd === "/help" || cmd === "/start") {
    return `🤖 ${site} Bot 命令
/admin — 今日管理入口
/status — API 配额状态
/daily [landscape|portrait|squarish] — 每日精选
/random 关键词 — 返回随机图片
/reset 123456 — 重置入口（需 TOTP 验证码）
/help — 显示此帮助`;
  }
  return "";
}

async function handleTelegramWebhook(request, env, ctx) {
  try {
    const update = await request.json();
    let msg = update.message;
    if (!msg && update.edited_message) msg = update.edited_message;
    if (!msg || !msg.text) return jsonResponse({ ok: true });
    const chatId = msg.chat?.id;
    const tgConfig = await getEncryptedJson(env.DB, "notify:telegram", env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, {}, env);
    if (!tgConfig.enabled || !tgConfig.botToken || !chatId) return jsonResponse({ ok: true });
    // 只回复已配置的 Chat ID
    if (String(tgConfig.chatId) !== String(chatId)) return jsonResponse({ ok: true });
    const reply = await buildBotCommandReply(String(msg.text).trim(), request, env, ctx);
    if (reply) {
      ctx.waitUntil(fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${tgConfig.botToken}/sendMessage`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ chat_id: chatId, text: reply }),
      }).catch((err) => {
        appendLog(env.DB, "error", "Telegram bot reply failed", { error: err.message }).catch(() => {});
      }));
    }
    return jsonResponse({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    ctx.waitUntil(appendLog(env.DB, "error", "Telegram webhook error", { error: err.message }).catch(() => {}));
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, error: err.message }, 500);
  }
}

async function handleRequest(request, env, ctx) {
  await ensureD1(env);
  const site = siteName(env);
  if (!env.DB) return renderPublicErrorPage({ request, status: 500, code: "missing_d1", title: "D1 数据库未绑定", message: `${site} 需要 Cloudflare D1 绑定才能运行。`, detail: "请在 Worker 设置中绑定名为 DB 的 D1 数据库，然后重新部署。", imageType: "generic", actionText: "查看文档", actionHref: "/docs", site });
  if (!env.ADMIN_PASSWORD) return renderPublicErrorPage({ request, status: 500, code: "missing_secret", title: "ADMIN_PASSWORD 未配置", message: `${site} 需要 ADMIN_PASSWORD Secret 才能保护后台和加密数据。`, detail: "请在 Cloudflare Worker Secrets 中添加 ADMIN_PASSWORD。", imageType: "generic", actionText: "查看文档", actionHref: "/docs", site });
  const config = await loadRuntimeConfig(env.DB, env);
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  if (request.method === "OPTIONS") return handleOptions();
  if (url.pathname === "/api/telegram-webhook" && request.method === "POST") return handleTelegramWebhook(request, env, ctx);
  if (request.method === "GET" && (url.pathname === "/api/random" || url.pathname === "/random" || (url.pathname === "/" && url.searchParams.has("query")))) return handleRandomImage(request, env, ctx, config);
  if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/api/daily") return handleDailyImage(request, env, ctx, config);
  if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/api/bg") return handleBgImage(request, env, ctx, config);
  if (url.pathname === "/api/check-content" && request.method === "POST") return handleContentCheck(request, env, ctx, config);
  if (url.pathname === "/api/feedback" && request.method === "POST") return handleFeedbackSubmit(request, env, ctx, config);
  if (url.pathname === "/api/d1/init" && (request.method === "GET" || request.method === "POST")) return handleD1Init(request, env);
  if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/api/source-health") {
    const data = await getHealth24h(env);
    // Lazy ping: if a configured source has no fresh sample, kick one off in the
    // background so the feature works even without a Cron Trigger configured.
    // Only consider configured sources — unconfigured ones are always "stale"
    // and would otherwise trigger a ping on every single page load.
    if (data.some(s => s.configured && s.stale)) ctx.waitUntil(pingSources(env));
    return publicJsonResponse(data, 200, { "Cache-Control": "public, max-age=30" });
  }
  const publicPage = request.method === "GET" ? await handlePublicPage(request, env, config) : null;
  if (publicPage) return publicPage;
  const adminPage = await handleAdminRoute(request, env, ctx, config);
  if (adminPage) return adminPage;
  return renderPublicErrorPage({ request, config, status: 404, code: "not_found", title: "页面不存在", message: "这个地址没有找到可用内容，可能链接已经失效或输入有误。", imageType: "generic", actionText: "返回首页", actionHref: "/", site });
}

export default {
  async fetch(request, env, ctx) {
    try {
      return await handleRequest(request, env, ctx);
    } catch (error) {
      if (env?.DB) ctx.waitUntil(appendLog(env.DB, "error", "Worker internal error", { error: error.message, stack: error.stack?.slice(0, 500) }, env));
      try {
        return renderPublicErrorPage({ request, status: 500, code: "internal_error", title: "系统暂时开小差了", message: "服务器遇到了一点内部问题，请稍后再试。", detail: "错误详情已记录到后台日志，不会直接展示给用户。", imageType: "generic", actionText: "返回首页", actionHref: "/", site });
      } catch (_) {
        return textResponse("Worker internal error", 500, { "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*" });
      }
    }
  },
  async scheduled(event, env, ctx) {
    if (!env?.ADMIN_PASSWORD) return;
    await ensureD1(env);
    ctx.waitUntil(getDailyAdminPath(env.DB, env, ctx));
    ctx.waitUntil(pingSources(env));
    ctx.waitUntil(env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM image_cache WHERE expires_at IS NOT NULL AND julianday(expires_at) <= julianday('now')").run().catch(() => {}));
  },
};

// D1 Database initialization
// Auto-initializes on first request if D1 binding "DB" is configured
let d1InitDone = false;
async function ensureD1(env) {
  if (!env.DB || d1InitDone) return;
  try {
    const stmts = D1_SCHEMA.split(";").filter(s => s.trim().length > 0).map(s => env.DB.prepare(s.trim() + ";"));
    if (stmts.length) await env.DB.batch(stmts);
    // Migrations (best-effort; column may already exist).
    try { await env.DB.prepare("ALTER TABLE rate_limits ADD COLUMN updated_at TEXT").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE rate_limits SET updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE updated_at IS NULL").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS presets (slug TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT NOT NULL, description TEXT DEFAULT '', params_json TEXT NOT NULL, is_public INTEGER DEFAULT 1, created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')), updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')))").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS request_analytics (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, ts TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')), path TEXT, source TEXT, query TEXT, orientation TEXT, color TEXT, aspect_ratio TEXT, cache_hit INTEGER DEFAULT 0, ok INTEGER DEFAULT 1, status INTEGER DEFAULT 200, latency INTEGER DEFAULT 0, ip_hash TEXT, api_key_id TEXT DEFAULT '')").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("ALTER TABLE request_analytics ADD COLUMN api_key_id TEXT DEFAULT ''").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS api_keys (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, name TEXT NOT NULL, key_hash TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL, key_prefix TEXT, status TEXT DEFAULT 'active', minute_limit INTEGER DEFAULT 60, hour_limit INTEGER DEFAULT 1000, day_limit INTEGER DEFAULT 10000, last_used_at TEXT, created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')), updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')))").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS daily_images (date TEXT NOT NULL, orientation TEXT NOT NULL, payload TEXT NOT NULL, source TEXT, image_id TEXT, created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')), PRIMARY KEY(date, orientation))").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS source_failures (source TEXT PRIMARY KEY, fail_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0, last_error TEXT, disabled_until TEXT, updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')))").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_request_analytics_ts ON request_analytics(ts)").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_request_analytics_source ON request_analytics(source, ts)").run(); } catch (_) {}
    // Garbage collection (D1 has no auto-TTL like KV).
    try { await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM image_cache WHERE expires_at IS NOT NULL AND julianday(expires_at) <= julianday('now')").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM source_health WHERE checked_at < datetime('now','-24 hours')").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM rate_limits WHERE updated_at < datetime('now','-7 days')").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM request_analytics WHERE ts < datetime('now','-90 days')").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM daily_images WHERE date < date('now','-30 days')").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM source_failures WHERE disabled_until IS NOT NULL AND julianday(disabled_until) <= julianday('now')").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM config WHERE key LIKE 'trans:%' AND updated_at < datetime('now','-30 days')").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM config WHERE key LIKE 'violations:%' AND updated_at < datetime('now','-30 days')").run(); } catch (_) {}
    try { await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM admin_config WHERE key = 'totp_setup_secret' AND updated_at < datetime('now','-30 minutes')").run(); } catch (_) {}
    d1InitDone = true; // only mark done after success, so a failed init retries next request
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("D1 init error:", e.message);
  }
}

const D1_SCHEMA = `
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS config (
  key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  value TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bans (
  ip_hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  reason TEXT,
  level INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
  created_at TEXT,
  expires_at TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS whitelist (
  ip_hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  ip TEXT,
  added_at TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS keywords (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  word TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  reason TEXT DEFAULT ''
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS changelog (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  date TEXT NOT NULL,
  version TEXT DEFAULT '',
  title TEXT DEFAULT '',
  content TEXT DEFAULT ''
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notify_configs (
  channel TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  config_json TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS admin_config (
  key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  value TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS error_logs (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  level TEXT DEFAULT 'error',
  message TEXT,
  meta TEXT,
  created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notification_history (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  event_type TEXT,
  status TEXT,
  payload TEXT,
  created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS image_cache (
  cache_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  payload TEXT NOT NULL,
  expires_at TEXT,
  created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS quota (
  source TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  used INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  limit_val INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  remaining INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rate_limits (
  ip_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
  window_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  timestamps TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
  PRIMARY KEY (ip_hash, window_type)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS source_health (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  source TEXT NOT NULL,
  ok INTEGER NOT NULL,
  latency INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  status_code INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  checked_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS presets (
  slug TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT DEFAULT '',
  params_json TEXT NOT NULL,
  is_public INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
  created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS request_analytics (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  ts TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  path TEXT,
  source TEXT,
  query TEXT,
  orientation TEXT,
  color TEXT,
  aspect_ratio TEXT,
  cache_hit INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  ok INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
  status INTEGER DEFAULT 200,
  latency INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  ip_hash TEXT,
  api_key_id TEXT DEFAULT ''
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS api_keys (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  key_hash TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  key_prefix TEXT,
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'active',
  minute_limit INTEGER DEFAULT 60,
  hour_limit INTEGER DEFAULT 1000,
  day_limit INTEGER DEFAULT 10000,
  last_used_at TEXT,
  created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS daily_images (
  date TEXT NOT NULL,
  orientation TEXT NOT NULL,
  payload TEXT NOT NULL,
  source TEXT,
  image_id TEXT,
  created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  PRIMARY KEY(date, orientation)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS source_failures (
  source TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  fail_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  last_error TEXT,
  disabled_until TEXT,
  updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS feedback (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  type TEXT NOT NULL,
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  content TEXT NOT NULL,
  contact TEXT DEFAULT '',
  ip_hash TEXT,
  user_agent TEXT,
  severity INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending',
  admin_note TEXT DEFAULT '',
  created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_feedback_status ON feedback(status, severity DESC, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_feedback_created ON feedback(created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_source_health ON source_health (source, checked_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_request_analytics_ts ON request_analytics(ts);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_request_analytics_source ON request_analytics(source, ts);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_request_analytics_api_key ON request_analytics(api_key_id, ts);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_daily_images_date ON daily_images(date DESC);
`;

async function handleD1Init(request, env) {
  if (!env.DB) return textResponse("D1 binding 'DB' not configured. Add it in Cloudflare Worker Settings → Bindings.", 400);
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  const initKey = url.searchParams.get("key");
  const auth = request.headers.get("Authorization") || "";
  const bearer = auth.startsWith("Bearer ") ? auth.slice(7).trim() : "";
  if (initKey !== env.ADMIN_PASSWORD && bearer !== env.ADMIN_PASSWORD) return textResponse("Invalid key", 403);
  // Extract actual table names (skip CREATE INDEX statements) with a regex.
  const tables = [...D1_SCHEMA.matchAll(/CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS\s+(\w+)/g)].map(m => m[1]);
  let created = 0;
  for (const stmt of D1_SCHEMA.split(";").filter(s => s.trim())) {
    try { await env.DB.prepare(stmt.trim()).run(); created++; } catch (e) { /* table may already exist */ }
  }
  return jsonResponse({ ok: true, tables, created });
}

// Legacy standalone helper kept for backward compatibility with any external callers.
async function getD1Config(env, key, defaultValue) {
  if (!env.DB) return null;
  const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM config WHERE key = ?").bind(key).first();
  return row ? row.value : defaultValue;
}

async function setD1Config(env, key, value) {
  if (!env.DB) return;
  await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO config (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))").bind(key, String(value ?? "")).run();
}
